import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_dotenv/flutter_dotenv.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'features/dashboard/presentation/pages/main_layout.dart';
import 'features/recipes/presentation/providers/recipes_provider.dart';
import 'features/inventory/presentation/providers/inventory_provider.dart';
import 'features/purchases/presentation/providers/purchases_provider.dart';
import 'features/sales/presentation/providers/sales_provider.dart';
import 'features/finance/presentation/providers/finance_provider.dart';
import 'features/staff/presentation/providers/staff_provider.dart';
import 'features/production/presentation/providers/production_provider.dart';
import 'features/pos/presentation/providers/pos_provider.dart';
import 'features/inventory/presentation/providers/reviziya_provider.dart';

import 'core/session/session_manager.dart';
import 'core/audit/audit_service.dart';

void main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  
  // Load environment variables
  await dotenv.load(fileName: ".env");
  
  // Initialize Supabase only if keys are available
  final supabaseUrl = dotenv.env['SUPABASE_URL'] ?? '';
  final supabaseKey = dotenv.env['SUPABASE_ANON_KEY'] ?? dotenv.env['BOT_TOKEN'] ?? '';
  
  if (supabaseUrl.isNotEmpty && supabaseKey.isNotEmpty) {
    await Supabase.initialize(
      url: supabaseUrl,
      publishableKey: supabaseKey,
    );
  } else {
    debugPrint('Supabase keys not found in .env, skipping initialization.');
  }

  await EasyLocalization.ensureInitialized();
  await SessionManager().init();

  runApp(
    MultiProvider(
      providers: [
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => SessionManager()),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => AuditService()),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => RecipesProvider()),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => InventoryProvider()),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => PurchasesProvider()),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => SalesProvider()),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => FinanceProvider()),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => StaffProvider()),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => ProductionProvider()),
        ChangeNotifierProvider(create: (_) => PosProvider()),
        ChangeNotifierProxyProvider<InventoryProvider, ReviziyaProvider>(
          create: (context) => ReviziyaProvider(context.read<InventoryProvider>()),
          update: (context, inventoryProvider, previous) => previous ?? ReviziyaProvider(inventoryProvider),
        ),
      ],
      child: EasyLocalization(
        supportedLocales: const [
          Locale('uz'), 
          Locale('ru'), 
          Locale('en'),
          Locale('uz', 'Cyrl')
        ],
        path: 'assets/translations',
        fallbackLocale: const Locale('uz'),
        startLocale: const Locale('uz'),
        child: const ChefControlApp(),
      ),
    ),
  );
}

class ChefControlApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const ChefControlApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'ChefControl AI',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      localizationsDelegates: context.localizationDelegates,
      supportedLocales: context.supportedLocales,
      locale: context.locale,
      theme: AppTheme.darkTheme,
      home: MainLayout(key: ValueKey('main_${context.locale.toString()}')),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../network/supabase_service.dart';
import '../session/session_manager.dart';

class AuditEntry {
  final String id;
  final String employeeId;
  final String employeeName;
  final String department;
  final String action;
  final String details;
  final DateTime timestamp;
  final Map<String, dynamic>? extraData;

  const AuditEntry({
    required this.id,
    required this.employeeId,
    required this.employeeName,
    required this.department,
    required this.action,
    required this.details,
    required this.timestamp,
    this.extraData,
  });

  factory AuditEntry.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    final payload = (json['payload'] as Map<String, dynamic>?) ?? {};
    return AuditEntry(
      id: json['id'] ?? '',
      employeeId: payload['employee_id'] ?? 'EMP-101',
      employeeName: payload['employee_name'] ?? 'Noma`lum xodim',
      department: payload['department'] ?? json['event_type'] ?? 'general',
      action: payload['action'] ?? json['event_type'] ?? '',
      details: payload['details'] ?? '',
      timestamp: DateTime.tryParse(json['created_at'] ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
      extraData: payload,
    );
  }
}

class AuditService extends ChangeNotifier {
  static final AuditService _instance = AuditService._internal();
  factory AuditService() => _instance;
  AuditService._internal() {
    _initSeedData();
  }

  final List<AuditEntry> _entries = [];
  List<AuditEntry> get entries => List.unmodifiable(_entries);

  final bool _isLoading = false;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;

  void _initSeedData() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    _entries.addAll([
      AuditEntry(
        id: 'aud-1',
        employeeId: 'EMP-101',
        employeeName: 'Bobur Aliyev',
        department: 'warehouse',
        action: 'Ombor kirimi',
        details: 'Mol go`shti (lahm) - 25 kg kirim qilindi. Yetkazib beruvchi: Go`sht Savdo MCHJ.',
        timestamp: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 9, 15),
      ),
      AuditEntry(
        id: 'aud-2',
        employeeId: 'EMP-102',
        employeeName: 'Malika Karimova',
        department: 'pos',
        action: 'POS Savdo cheki',
        details: 'Chek #1001 yopildi. Jami: 185 000 so`m (Osh x2, Choy x1, Salat x2).',
        timestamp: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 10, 5),
      ),
      AuditEntry(
        id: 'aud-3',
        employeeId: 'EMP-101',
        employeeName: 'Bobur Aliyev',
        department: 'warehouse',
        action: 'Qoldiq tekshiruvi',
        details: 'O`simlik yog`i qoldig`i tekshirildi (Haqiqiy: 18 litr).',
        timestamp: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 10, 40),
      ),
      AuditEntry(
        id: 'aud-4',
        employeeId: 'EMP-102',
        employeeName: 'Malika Karimova',
        department: 'pos',
        action: 'POS Savdo cheki',
        details: 'Chek #1002 yopildi. Jami: 95 000 so`m (Shashlik x3, Non x2).',
        timestamp: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 11, 20),
      ),
      AuditEntry(
        id: 'aud-5',
        employeeId: 'EMP-101',
        employeeName: 'Bobur Aliyev',
        department: 'warehouse',
        action: 'Chiqim hujjati',
        details: 'Oshxona talabnomasi bo`yicha 10 kg Guruch chiqarildi.',
        timestamp: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 11, 55),
      ),
    ]);
  }

  Future<void> logAction({
    required String department,
    required String action,
    required String details,
    String? employeeId,
    String? employeeName,
    Map<String, dynamic>? extraData,
  }) async {
    final session = SessionManager();
    final effectiveEmpId = employeeId ?? session.currentEmployee?.id ?? 'EMP-101';
    final effectiveEmpName = employeeName ?? session.currentEmployee?.name ?? (session.currentRole == AppRole.owner ? 'Boshliq' : 'Xodim');

    final entry = AuditEntry(
      id: 'aud-${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
      employeeId: effectiveEmpId,
      employeeName: effectiveEmpName,
      department: department,
      action: action,
      details: details,
      timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      extraData: extraData,
    );

    _entries.insert(0, entry);
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final client = SupabaseService.client;
      await client.from('business_events').insert({
        'restaurant_id': SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId,
        'event_type': action,
        'payload': {
          'employee_id': effectiveEmpId,
          'employee_name': effectiveEmpName,
          'department': department,
          'action': action,
          'details': details,
          ...?extraData,
        },
      });
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Audit remote logging bypassed or failed: $e');
    }
  }

  List<AuditEntry> getTodayEvents({String? query}) {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    return _entries.where((entry) {
      final isToday = entry.timestamp.year == now.year &&
          entry.timestamp.month == now.month &&
          entry.timestamp.day == now.day;
      if (!isToday) return false;

      if (query == null || query.trim().isEmpty) return true;

      final q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
      final idMatch = entry.employeeId.toLowerCase().contains(q);
      final nameMatch = entry.employeeName.toLowerCase().contains(q);
      final deptMatch = entry.department.toLowerCase().contains(q);
      final actionMatch = entry.action.toLowerCase().contains(q);
      final detailsMatch = entry.details.toLowerCase().contains(q);

      return idMatch || nameMatch || deptMatch || actionMatch || detailsMatch;
    }).toList();
  }
}

import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

class SupabaseService {
  static final SupabaseClient client = Supabase.instance.client;
  
  static String get currentRestaurantId => SessionManager().bossProfile.restaurantId;
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\core\session\session_manager.dart

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

enum AppRole {
  owner,
  staff,
}

enum Department {
  none,
  warehouse,
  pos,
  purchases,
  kitchen,
  finance,
}

class EmployeeProfile {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final Department assignedDepartment;
  final String status;
  final String roleName;

  const EmployeeProfile({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.assignedDepartment,
    required this.status,
    required this.roleName,
  });

  EmployeeProfile copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? name,
    Department? assignedDepartment,
    String? status,
    String? roleName,
  }) {
    return EmployeeProfile(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      assignedDepartment: assignedDepartment ?? this.assignedDepartment,
      status: status ?? this.status,
      roleName: roleName ?? this.roleName,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'id': id,
    'name': name,
    'assignedDepartment': assignedDepartment.name,
    'status': status,
    'roleName': roleName,
  };

  factory EmployeeProfile.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => EmployeeProfile(
    id: json['id'] ?? '',
    name: json['name'] ?? '',
    assignedDepartment: Department.values.firstWhere(
      (d) => d.name == json['assignedDepartment'],
      orElse: () => Department.none,
    ),
    status: json['status'] ?? 'waiting',
    roleName: json['roleName'] ?? 'Xodim',
  );
}

class BossProfile {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String roleTitle;
  final String restaurantName;
  final String restaurantId;

  const BossProfile({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.roleTitle,
    required this.restaurantName,
    required this.restaurantId,
  });
}

class SessionManager extends ChangeNotifier {
  static final SessionManager _instance = SessionManager._internal();
  factory SessionManager() => _instance;
  SessionManager._internal() {
    init();
  }

  AppRole _currentRole = AppRole.owner;
  AppRole get currentRole => _currentRole;

  BossProfile _bossProfile = const BossProfile(
    id: '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
    name: 'Alisher Usmonov',
    roleTitle: 'Restoran Rahbari (Boshliq)',
    restaurantName: 'Rayhon Milliy Taomlar',
    restaurantId: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000',
  );
  BossProfile get bossProfile => _bossProfile;

  final List<EmployeeProfile> _employees = [
    const EmployeeProfile(
      id: 'EMP-101',
      name: 'Bobur Aliyev',
      assignedDepartment: Department.warehouse,
      status: 'active',
      roleName: 'Ombor mudiri',
    ),
    const EmployeeProfile(
      id: 'EMP-102',
      name: 'Malika Karimova',
      assignedDepartment: Department.pos,
      status: 'active',
      roleName: 'Kassir',
    ),
    const EmployeeProfile(
      id: 'EMP-103',
      name: 'Jasur Shodiyev',
      assignedDepartment: Department.none,
      status: 'waiting',
      roleName: 'Yangi xodim (Kutilmoqda)',
    ),
  ];
  List<EmployeeProfile> get employees => List.unmodifiable(_employees);

  EmployeeProfile? _currentEmployee;
  EmployeeProfile? get currentEmployee => _currentEmployee;

  Department get activeDepartment {
    if (_currentRole == AppRole.owner) {
      return Department.none;
    }
    return _currentEmployee?.assignedDepartment ?? Department.none;
  }

  bool isDepartmentDelegated(Department dept) {
    if (dept == Department.none) return false;
    return _employees.any(
      (emp) => emp.assignedDepartment == dept && emp.status == 'active',
    );
  }

  EmployeeProfile? getEmployeeForDepartment(Department dept) {
    try {
      return _employees.firstWhere(
        (emp) => emp.assignedDepartment == dept && emp.status == 'active',
      );
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  Future<void> init() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final employeesJson = prefs.getString('chefcontrol_employees');
      if (employeesJson != null) {
        final List<dynamic> list = jsonDecode(employeesJson);
        final loaded = list.map((e) => EmployeeProfile.fromJson(e)).toList();
        if (loaded.isNotEmpty) {
          _employees.clear();
          _employees.addAll(loaded);
        }
      }

      final bossName = prefs.getString('chefcontrol_boss_name');
      final restName = prefs.getString('chefcontrol_rest_name');
      final restId = prefs.getString('chefcontrol_rest_id');
      if (bossName != null || restName != null || restId != null) {
        _bossProfile = BossProfile(
          id: _bossProfile.id,
          name: bossName ?? _bossProfile.name,
          roleTitle: _bossProfile.roleTitle,
          restaurantName: restName ?? _bossProfile.restaurantName,
          restaurantId: restId ?? _bossProfile.restaurantId,
        );
      }
      notifyListeners();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error initializing session: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveEmployees() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final data = jsonEncode(_employees.map((e) => e.toJson()).toList());
      await prefs.setString('chefcontrol_employees', data);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error saving employees: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveBoss() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await prefs.setString('chefcontrol_boss_name', _bossProfile.name);
      await prefs.setString('chefcontrol_rest_name', _bossProfile.restaurantName);
      await prefs.setString('chefcontrol_rest_id', _bossProfile.restaurantId);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error saving boss: $e');
    }
  }

  void _syncEmployeeToSupabase(EmployeeProfile emp) {
    try {
      final client = Supabase.instance.client;
      client.from('users').upsert({
        'id': emp.id,
        'restaurant_id': _bossProfile.restaurantId,
        'name': emp.name,
        'role': emp.roleName,
        'department': emp.assignedDepartment.name,
        'status': emp.status,
      }).catchError((e) {
        debugPrint('Supabase employee sync error: $e');
      });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  void _syncRestaurantToSupabase() {
    try {
      final client = Supabase.instance.client;
      client.from('restaurants').update({
        'name': _bossProfile.restaurantName,
      }).eq('id', _bossProfile.restaurantId).catchError((e) {
        debugPrint('Supabase restaurant sync error: $e');
      });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  void switchToBoss() {
    _currentRole = AppRole.owner;
    _currentEmployee = null;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void switchToEmployee(String employeeId) {
    final emp = _employees.firstWhere(
      (e) => e.id.toLowerCase() == employeeId.toLowerCase(),
      orElse: () => EmployeeProfile(
        id: employeeId.toUpperCase(),
        name: 'Xodim $employeeId',
        assignedDepartment: Department.none,
        status: 'waiting',
        roleName: 'Biriktirilmagan',
      ),
    );
    if (!_employees.any((e) => e.id == emp.id)) {
      _employees.add(emp);
      _saveEmployees();
    }
    _currentRole = AppRole.staff;
    _currentEmployee = emp;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void assignDepartment(String employeeId, Department department) {
    final index = _employees.indexWhere((e) => e.id == employeeId);
    if (index != -1) {
      String roleName = 'Biriktirilmagan';
      switch (department) {
        case Department.warehouse:
          roleName = 'Ombor mudiri';
          break;
        case Department.pos:
          roleName = 'Kassir (POS)';
          break;
        case Department.purchases:
          roleName = 'Ta`minotchi (Xaridlar)';
          break;
        case Department.kitchen:
          roleName = 'Oshpaz';
          break;
        case Department.finance:
          roleName = 'Hisobchi';
          break;
        case Department.none:
          roleName = 'Biriktirilmagan';
          break;
      }

      final updated = _employees[index].copyWith(
        assignedDepartment: department,
        status: department == Department.none ? 'waiting' : 'active',
        roleName: roleName,
      );
      _employees[index] = updated;

      if (_currentEmployee?.id == employeeId) {
        _currentEmployee = updated;
      }
      _saveEmployees();
      _syncEmployeeToSupabase(updated);
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  EmployeeProfile registerNewEmployee({required String name}) {
    final nextIdNum = 100 + _employees.length + 1;
    final newId = 'EMP-$nextIdNum';
    final newEmp = EmployeeProfile(
      id: newId,
      name: name,
      assignedDepartment: Department.none,
      status: 'waiting',
      roleName: 'Yangi xodim',
    );
    _employees.add(newEmp);
    _saveEmployees();
    _syncEmployeeToSupabase(newEmp);
    notifyListeners();
    return newEmp;
  }

  void updateBossProfile({
    String? name,
    String? restaurantName,
    String? restaurantId,
  }) {
    _bossProfile = BossProfile(
      id: _bossProfile.id,
      name: name ?? _bossProfile.name,
      roleTitle: _bossProfile.roleTitle,
      restaurantName: restaurantName ?? _bossProfile.restaurantName,
      restaurantId: restaurantId ?? _bossProfile.restaurantId,
    );
    _saveBoss();
    _syncRestaurantToSupabase();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  String getInviteLink(String employeeId) {
    return 'https://chefcontrol.ai/join?code=$employeeId&rest=${_bossProfile.restaurantId}';
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AppColors {
  // Backgrounds
  static const Color background = Color(0xFF0A0E17);
  static const Color surface = Color(0xFF161B26);
  static const Color surfaceLight = Color(0xFF1F2937);

  // Accents
  static const Color primary = Color(0xFF0066FF); // Bright Blue
  static const Color secondary = Color(0xFFFF8C00); // Orange for FAB

  // Text
  static const Color textPrimary = Color(0xFFF9FAFB);
  static const Color textSecondary = Color(0xFF9CA3AF);
  static const Color textMuted = Color(0xFF6B7280);

  // Status
  static const Color success = Color(0xFF10B981);
  static const Color error = Color(0xFFEF4444);
  static const Color warning = Color(0xFFF59E0B);
  
  // Custom Gradients (optional)
  static const LinearGradient primaryGradient = LinearGradient(
    colors: [Color(0xFF0066FF), Color(0xFF3B82F6)],
    begin: Alignment.topLeft,
    end: Alignment.bottomRight,
  );
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'app_colors.dart';

class AppTheme {
  static ThemeData get darkTheme {
    return ThemeData(
      brightness: Brightness.dark,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      primaryColor: AppColors.primary,
      colorScheme: const ColorScheme.dark(
        primary: AppColors.primary,
        secondary: AppColors.secondary,
        surface: AppColors.surface,
        error: AppColors.error,
        onPrimary: Colors.white,
        onSecondary: Colors.white,
        onSurface: AppColors.textPrimary,
      ),
      appBarTheme: const AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
        elevation: 0,
        centerTitle: true,
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(
          color: AppColors.textPrimary,
          fontSize: 18,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
        ),
      ),

      textTheme: const TextTheme(
        displayLarge: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 32, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        displayMedium: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        bodyLarge: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16),
        bodyMedium: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 14),
        bodySmall: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12),
      ),
      elevatedButtonTheme: ElevatedButtonThemeData(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          elevation: 0,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16, horizontal: 24),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 16,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: AppColors.surfaceLight,
        hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textMuted),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide.none,
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide.none,
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary, width: 1.5),
        ),
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
      ),
      bottomNavigationBarTheme: const BottomNavigationBarThemeData(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        selectedItemColor: AppColors.primary,
        unselectedItemColor: AppColors.textSecondary,
        type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
        elevation: 8,
      ),
      floatingActionButtonTheme: const FloatingActionButtonThemeData(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.secondary,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        elevation: 4,
      ),
    );
  }
}

class SearchUtils {
  static const Map<String, String> _cyrillicToLatin = {
    'Ð°': 'a', 'Ð±': 'b', 'Ð²': 'v', 'Ð³': 'g', 'Ð´': 'd', 'Ðµ': 'e', 'Ñ‘': 'yo', 'Ð¶': 'zh',
    'Ð·': 'z', 'Ð¸': 'i', 'Ð¹': 'y', 'Ðº': 'k', 'Ð»': 'l', 'Ð¼': 'm', 'Ð½': 'n', 'Ð¾': 'o',
    'Ð¿': 'p', 'Ñ€': 'r', 'Ñ': 's', 'Ñ‚': 't', 'Ñƒ': 'u', 'Ñ„': 'f', 'Ñ…': 'h', 'Ñ†': 'ts',
    'Ñ‡': 'ch', 'Ñˆ': 'sh', 'Ñ‰': 'shch', 'ÑŠ': '', 'Ñ‹': 'y', 'ÑŒ': '', 'Ñ': 'e', 'ÑŽ': 'yu', 'Ñ': 'ya',
    'Ò›': 'q', 'Ñž': "o'", 'Ò“': "g'", 'Ò³': 'h',
  };

  static String normalize(String text) {
    String result = text.toLowerCase().trim();
    _cyrillicToLatin.forEach((key, value) {
      result = result.replaceAll(key, value);
    });
    return result;
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\core\utils\unit_converter.dart
// ==========================================

class UnitConverter {
  /// Converts [quantity] from [fromUnit] to [toUnit].
  /// Supports common restaurant units:
  /// - kg <-> g / gr
  /// - l / litr <-> ml
  /// Returns original quantity if conversion is not supported or not needed.
  static double convert(double quantity, String fromUnit, String toUnit) {
    if (fromUnit == toUnit) return quantity;

    final from = fromUnit.toLowerCase().trim();
    final to = toUnit.toLowerCase().trim();

    if (from == to) return quantity;

    // Weight conversions
    if ((from == 'kg' || from == 'ÐºÐ³') && (to == 'g' || to == 'gr' || to == 'Ð³' || to == 'Ð³Ñ€')) {
      return quantity * 1000;
    }
    if ((from == 'g' || from == 'gr' || from == 'Ð³' || from == 'Ð³Ñ€') && (to == 'kg' || to == 'ÐºÐ³')) {
      return quantity / 1000;
    }

    // Volume conversions
    if ((from == 'l' || from == 'litr' || from == 'Ð»' || from == 'Ð»Ð¸Ñ‚Ñ€') && (to == 'ml' || to == 'Ð¼Ð»')) {
      return quantity * 1000;
    }
    if ((from == 'ml' || from == 'Ð¼Ð»') && (to == 'l' || to == 'litr' || to == 'Ð»' || to == 'Ð»Ð¸Ñ‚Ñ€')) {
      return quantity / 1000;
    }

    // Unhandled conversions (e.g. dona <-> dona, or mismatched like kg <-> litr)
    return quantity;
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\ai_assistant\data\services\ai_service.dart
// ==========================================

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:flutter_dotenv/flutter_dotenv.dart';

class AiService {
  final String _apiKey = dotenv.env['DEEPSEEK_API_KEY'] ?? '';
  final String _model = dotenv.env['DEEPSEEK_MODEL'] ?? 'deepseek-flash';
  final String _baseUrl = 'https://api.deepseek.com/chat/completions';

  Future<String> sendMessage(List<Map<String, String>> messages) async {
    try {
      if (_apiKey.isEmpty) {
        return "API kaliti topilmadi (.env faylini tekshiring).";
      }

      final response = await http.post(
        Uri.parse(_baseUrl),
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'Authorization': 'Bearer $_apiKey',
        },
        body: jsonEncode({
          'model': _model,
          'messages': messages,
          'temperature': 0.7,
        }),
      ).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 25));

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
        return data['choices'][0]['message']['content'] ?? '';
      } else {
        return "Xatolik: ${response.statusCode}";
      }
    } catch (e) {
      return "Tarmoq xatosi: $e";
    }
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\ai_assistant\data\services\voice_service.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:speech_to_text/speech_to_text.dart' as stt;

class VoiceService {
  final stt.SpeechToText _speech = stt.SpeechToText();
  bool _isInitialized = false;

  bool get isInitialized => _isInitialized;
  bool get isListening => _speech.isListening;

  Future<bool> initialize() async {
    if (_isInitialized) return true;
    try {
      _isInitialized = await _speech.initialize(
        onError: (val) => debugPrint('VoiceService error: ${val.errorMsg}'),
        onStatus: (val) => debugPrint('VoiceService status: $val'),
      );
      return _isInitialized;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('VoiceService init error: $e');
      _isInitialized = false;
      return false;
    }
  }

  Future<void> startListening({
    required Function(String text) onResult,
    required Function(double soundLevel) onSoundLevel,
    String? localeId,
  }) async {
    if (!_isInitialized) {
      final ok = await initialize();
      if (!ok) return;
    }

    try {
      await _speech.listen(
        onResult: (result) {
          onResult(result.recognizedWords);
        },
        onSoundLevelChange: onSoundLevel,
        listenOptions: stt.SpeechListenOptions(
          listenMode: stt.ListenMode.confirmation,
          partialResults: true,
          cancelOnError: true,
        ),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('VoiceService listen error: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> stopListening() async {
    if (_speech.isListening) {
      await _speech.stop();
    }
  }

  Future<void> cancelListening() async {
    if (_speech.isListening) {
      await _speech.cancel();
    }
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\ai_assistant\domain\entities\ai_agent_action_entity.dart
// ==========================================

enum AgentActionType {
  produce,
  expense,
  waste,
  stockQuery,
  salesQuery,
  lowStockQuery,
  financeQuery,
  none,
}

class AgentActionEntity {
  final AgentActionType type;
  final String title;
  final String target;
  final double quantity;
  final String unit;
  final String details;
  final bool isSuccess;
  final String? errorMessage;
  final List<String> impactedItems;

  const AgentActionEntity({
    required this.type,
    required this.title,
    required this.target,
    this.quantity = 0.0,
    this.unit = '',
    required this.details,
    this.isSuccess = true,
    this.errorMessage,
    this.impactedItems = const [],
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'type': type.name,
      'title': title,
      'target': target,
      'quantity': quantity,
      'unit': unit,
      'details': details,
      'isSuccess': isSuccess,
      'errorMessage': errorMessage,
      'impactedItems': impactedItems,
    };
  }

  factory AgentActionEntity.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return AgentActionEntity(
      type: AgentActionType.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == map['type'],
        orElse: () => AgentActionType.none,
      ),
      title: map['title'] ?? '',
      target: map['target'] ?? '',
      quantity: (map['quantity'] ?? 0.0).toDouble(),
      unit: map['unit'] ?? '',
      details: map['details'] ?? '',
      isSuccess: map['isSuccess'] ?? true,
      errorMessage: map['errorMessage'],
      impactedItems: List<String>.from(map['impactedItems'] ?? []),
    );
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\ai_assistant\domain\entities\ai_message_entity.dart
// ==========================================

enum AiMessageType {
  text,
  voice,
  action,
}

enum AiMessageSender {
  user,
  assistant,
}

class AiMessageEntity {
  final String id;
  final String text;
  final AiMessageSender sender;
  final AiMessageType type;
  final DateTime timestamp;
  final int? voiceDurationSeconds;
  final Map<String, dynamic>? actionData;

  const AiMessageEntity({
    required this.id,
    required this.text,
    required this.sender,
    this.type = AiMessageType.text,
    required this.timestamp,
    this.voiceDurationSeconds,
    this.actionData,
  });

  bool get isUser => sender == AiMessageSender.user;
}

          ),
          PopupMenuButton<String>(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.more_vert, color: Colors.white),
            color: AppColors.surface,
            onSelected: (value) {
              if (value == 'clear') {
                _showClearConfirmDialog(context, provider);
              }
            },
            itemBuilder: (context) => [
              PopupMenuItem(
                value: 'clear',
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    const Icon(Icons.delete_sweep, color: Colors.redAccent, size: 20),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Text(
                      'ai_assistant.clear_chat'.tr(),
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 14),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: ListView.builder(
              controller: _scrollController,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 12),
              itemCount: provider.messages.length + (provider.isLoading ? 1 : 0),
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                if (index == 0) {
                  return Column(
                    children: [
                      _buildDateBadge(context),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      _buildMessageItem(provider.messages[index]),
                    ],
                  );
                }

                if (index < provider.messages.length) {
                  return _buildMessageItem(provider.messages[index]);
                }

                return _buildTypingIndicator();
              },
            ),
          ),
          TelegramVoiceRecordBar(
            controller: _textController,
            isListening: provider.isListening,
            isLoading: provider.isLoading,
            soundLevel: provider.soundLevel,
            onStartRecording: () {
              provider.startListening(localeId: context.locale.toString());

            onStopRecording: () {
              provider.stopListeningAndSend();

            onCancelRecording: () {
              provider.cancelListening();
            },
            onSendText: (text) {
              provider.sendTextMessage(text);
            },
            onOpenQuickPresets: () => _showQuickPresets(context, provider),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDateBadge(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 4),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.35),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          border: Border.all(color: Colors.white12, width: 0.5),
        ),
        child: Text(
          DateFormat('d MMMM, yyyy', context.locale.toString()).format(DateTime.now()),
          style: const TextStyle(
            color: Colors.white70,
            fontSize: 11,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMessageItem(AiMessageEntity msg) {
    if (msg.type == AiMessageType.voice) {
      return TelegramVoiceBubble(
        text: msg.text,
        isUser: msg.isUser,
        durationSeconds: msg.voiceDurationSeconds ?? 2,
        timestamp: msg.timestamp,
      );
    }

    return TelegramChatBubble(message: msg);
  }

  Widget _buildTypingIndicator() {
    return Align(
      alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4, horizontal: 8),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: const Color(0xFF1E2633),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16).copyWith(
            bottomLeft: const Radius.circular(3),
          ),
          border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10, width: 0.5),
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const SizedBox(
              width: 14,
              height: 14,
              child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                strokeWidth: 2,
                color: Color(0xFF246BFD),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            Text(
              'ai_assistant.listening'.tr(),
              style: const TextStyle(
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                fontSize: 13,
                fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\ai_assistant\presentation\providers\ai_assistant_provider.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/ai_message_entity.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/ai_agent_action_entity.dart';
import '../../data/services/ai_service.dart';
import '../../data/services/voice_service.dart';
import '../../../../core/network/supabase_service.dart';
import '../../../../core/audit/audit_service.dart';
import '../../../../core/utils/unit_converter.dart';

class AiAssistantProvider extends ChangeNotifier {
  final AiService _aiService = AiService();
  final VoiceService _voiceService = VoiceService();

  final List<AiMessageEntity> _messages = [];
  List<AiMessageEntity> get messages => List.unmodifiable(_messages);

  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;

  bool _isListening = false;
  bool get isListening => _isListening;

  double _soundLevel = 0.0;
  double get soundLevel => _soundLevel;

  String _currentRecognizedText = '';
  String get currentRecognizedText => _currentRecognizedText;

  AiAssistantProvider() {
    _initDefaultGreeting();
  }

  void _initDefaultGreeting() {
    if (_messages.isEmpty) {
      _messages.add(
        AiMessageEntity(
          id: 'msg-init',
          text: 'ai_assistant.greeting'.tr(),
          sender: AiMessageSender.assistant,
          type: AiMessageType.text,
          timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  void clearChat() {
    _messages.clear();
    _initDefaultGreeting();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> startListening({String? localeId}) async {
    _currentRecognizedText = '';
    _isListening = true;
    _soundLevel = 0.0;
    notifyListeners();

    final ready = await _voiceService.initialize();
    if (!ready) {
      _isListening = false;
      notifyListeners();
      return;
    }

    await _voiceService.startListening(
      localeId: localeId,
      onResult: (text) {
        _currentRecognizedText = text;
        notifyListeners();
      },
      onSoundLevel: (level) {
        _soundLevel = level;
        notifyListeners();
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> stopListeningAndSend([int durationSeconds = 2]) async {
    await _voiceService.stopListening();
    _isListening = false;
    final textToSend = _currentRecognizedText.trim();
    _currentRecognizedText = '';
    _soundLevel = 0.0;
    notifyListeners();

    if (textToSend.isNotEmpty) {
      await sendVoiceMessage(textToSend, durationSeconds);
    }
  }

  Future<void> cancelListening() async {
    await _voiceService.cancelListening();
    _isListening = false;
    _currentRecognizedText = '';
    _soundLevel = 0.0;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> sendTextMessage(String text) async {
    final clean = text.trim();
    if (clean.isEmpty) return;

    final userMessage = AiMessageEntity(
      id: 'usr-${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
      text: clean,
      sender: AiMessageSender.user,
      type: AiMessageType.text,
      timestamp: DateTime.now(),
    );
    _messages.add(userMessage);
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    await _processWithAi(clean);
  }

  Future<void> sendVoiceMessage(String text, int durationSeconds) async {
    final clean = text.trim();
    if (clean.isEmpty) return;

    final userMessage = AiMessageEntity(
      id: 'usr-v-${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
      text: clean,
      sender: AiMessageSender.user,
      type: AiMessageType.voice,
      voiceDurationSeconds: durationSeconds < 1 ? 1 : durationSeconds,
      timestamp: DateTime.now(),
    );
    _messages.add(userMessage);
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    await _processWithAi(clean);
  }

  Future<void> _processWithAi(String userPrompt) async {
    final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;
    final supabase = SupabaseService.client;
    final today = DateTime.now().toIso8601String().substring(0, 10);

    double totalSalesToday = 0;
    int receiptsCount = 0;
    double totalExpensesToday = 0;
    final List<String> lowStockList = [];
    final List<String> availableDishes = [];

    try {
      // 1. Fetch Today's Sales
      final salesRes = await supabase
          .from('sales')
          .select('total_amount')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .eq('status', 'completed')
          .gte('date', today);
      receiptsCount = (salesRes as List).length;
      for (var sale in salesRes) {
        totalSalesToday += (sale['total_amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
      }

      // 2. Fetch Today's Operating Expenses
      final expRes = await supabase
          .from('expenses')
          .select('amount, category')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .gte('expense_date', today);
      for (var row in (expRes as List)) {
        totalExpensesToday += (row['amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
      }

      // 3. Fetch Low Stock Products
      final lowStockRes = await supabase
          .from('products_with_stock')
          .select('name, current_stock, minimum_stock, unit')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .eq('is_active', true)
          .limit(10);
      for (var p in (lowStockRes as List)) {
        final cur = (p['current_stock'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        final min = (p['minimum_stock'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        if (min > 0 && cur <= min) {
          lowStockList.add('${p['name']}: $cur ${p['unit']} (min: $min)');
        }
      }

      // 4. Fetch Available Menu Recipes
      final recipesRes = await supabase
          .from('recipes')
          .select('name')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .eq('is_active', true)
          .limit(25);
      for (var r in (recipesRes as List)) {
        availableDishes.add(r['name'].toString());
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('AI Context fetch error: $e');
    }

    final List<Map<String, String>> apiMessages = [
      {
        'role': 'system',
        'content':
            'Sen ChefControl AI - restoran boshqaruvi uchun o\'ta aqlli va professional AI agentisan.\n'
            'Restoran egasi (rahbar), oshpaz va xodimlarga biznes tahlil, ombor va operatsion jarayonlarda yordam berasan. O\'zbek tilida qisqa, aniq va foydali javob ber.\n\n'
            'RESTORANNING HOZIRGI REAL KO\'RSATKICHLARI (BUGUN):\n'
            '- Bugungi umumiy savdo: ${NumberFormat('#,###').format(totalSalesToday)} so\'m ($receiptsCount ta chek)\n'
            '- Bugungi operatsion xarajatlar: ${NumberFormat('#,###').format(totalExpensesToday)} so\'m\n'
            '- Menyudagi taomlar: ${availableDishes.isEmpty ? "Mavjud" : availableDishes.join(", ")}\n'
            '- Kam qolgan mahsulotlar (ogohlantirish): ${lowStockList.isEmpty ? "Barcha zaxiralar me`yorida" : lowStockList.join("; ")}\n\n'
            'BUYRUQ VA HARAKATLARNI ISHLATISH QOIDALARI:\n'
            'Agar foydalanuvchi tizimda amaliy harakat so\'rasa, javobing matni oxiriga mos action tegini qo\'sh:\n'
            '1. Taom tayyorlash (ishlab chiqarish): [ACTION:PRODUCE:Taom_Nomi:Miqdori]\n'
            '2. Ombor qoldig\'i: [ACTION:STOCK:Mahsulot_Nomi]\n'
            '3. Kassa va savdo: [ACTION:SALES]\n'
            '4. Kam qolgan tovarlar: [ACTION:LOW_STOCK]\n'
            '5. Moliya va foyda tahlili: [ACTION:FINANCE]\n'
            '6. Xarajat kiritish: [ACTION:EXPENSE:Kategoriya:Summa]\n'
            '7. Chiqit / Spisaniya: [ACTION:WASTE:Mahsulot_Nomi:Miqdori]'
      },
    ];

    final history = _messages.length > 8 ? _messages.sublist(_messages.length - 8) : _messages;
    for (var msg in history) {
      if (msg.id == 'msg-init') continue;
      apiMessages.add({
        'role': msg.isUser ? 'user' : 'assistant',
        'content': msg.text,
      });
    }

    final rawResponse = await _aiService.sendMessage(apiMessages);
    final actionEntity = await _executeAgentActions(rawResponse);

    var cleanResponse = rawResponse.replaceAll(RegExp(r'\[ACTION:.*?\]'), '').trim();
    if (cleanResponse.isEmpty) {
      cleanResponse = actionEntity != null
          ? actionEntity.details
          : 'ai_assistant.action_done'.tr();
    }

    final assistantMessage = AiMessageEntity(
      id: 'asst-${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
      text: cleanResponse,
      sender: AiMessageSender.assistant,
      type: actionEntity != null ? AiMessageType.action : AiMessageType.text,
      actionData: actionEntity?.toMap(),
      timestamp: DateTime.now(),
    );

    _messages.add(assistantMessage);
    _isLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<AgentActionEntity?> _executeAgentActions(String response) async {
    final client = SupabaseService.client;
    final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;

    // 1. PRODUCE ACTION
    final produceRegex = RegExp(r'\[ACTION:PRODUCE:(.+?):([0-9.]+)\]');
    final produceMatch = produceRegex.firstMatch(response);
    if (produceMatch != null) {
      final recipeName = produceMatch.group(1)?.trim() ?? '';
      final quantity = double.tryParse(produceMatch.group(2) ?? '1') ?? 1.0;

      try {
        final recipesResponse = await client
            .from('recipes')
            .select('id, name, recipe_items(product_id, quantity, unit, products(name, unit))')
            .ilike('name', '%$recipeName%')
            .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
            .limit(1);

        if (recipesResponse.isNotEmpty) {
          final recipe = recipesResponse.first;
          final actualName = recipe['name'] ?? recipeName;
          final items = (recipe['recipe_items'] as List<dynamic>?) ?? [];

          final List<String> deductedSummary = [];
          final List<Map<String, dynamic>> movements = [];

          for (var item in items) {
            final double itemQty = (item['quantity'] ?? 0).toDouble();
            final String recipeUnit = item['unit'] ?? '';
            final prodMap = item['products'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
            final String prodName = prodMap?['name'] ?? 'Masalliq';
            final String baseUnit = prodMap?['unit'] ?? recipeUnit;
            final String finalUnit = baseUnit.isEmpty ? recipeUnit : baseUnit;

            final double convertedQty = UnitConverter.convert(itemQty, recipeUnit, finalUnit);
            final double totalQty = convertedQty * quantity;

            deductedSummary.add('$prodName: -$totalQty $finalUnit');

            movements.add({
              'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
              'product_id': item['product_id'],
              'quantity': -totalQty,
              'unit': finalUnit,
              'type': 'production_usage',
              'reason': 'ChefControl AI: $actualName x $quantity',
            });
          }

          if (movements.isNotEmpty) {
            await client.from('stock_movements').insert(movements);
          }

          AuditService().logAction(
            department: 'kitchen',
            action: 'Taom tayyorlash (AI)',
            details: '$actualName dan $quantity porsiya tayyorlandi. Ombordan masalliqlar avtomatik yechildi.',
          );

          return AgentActionEntity(
            type: AgentActionType.produce,
            title: '$actualName ($quantity ta)',
            target: actualName,
            quantity: quantity,
            unit: 'ta',
            details: '$actualName muvaffaqiyatli tayyorlandi va ombordan tegishli masalliqlar chegirildi.',
            isSuccess: true,
            impactedItems: deductedSummary,
          );
        } else {
          return AgentActionEntity(
            type: AgentActionType.produce,
            title: recipeName,
            target: recipeName,
            quantity: quantity,
            details: '$recipeName bo\'yicha TTK retsepti topilmadi.',
            isSuccess: false,
            errorMessage: 'TTK topilmadi',
          );
        }
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint('AI Produce execution error: $e');
        return AgentActionEntity(
          type: AgentActionType.produce,
          title: recipeName,
          target: recipeName,
          details: 'Xatolik yuz berdi: $e',
          isSuccess: false,
          errorMessage: e.toString(),
        );
      }
    }

    // 2. STOCK QUERY
    final stockRegex = RegExp(r'\[ACTION:STOCK:(.+?)\]');
    final stockMatch = stockRegex.firstMatch(response);
    if (stockMatch != null) {
      final query = stockMatch.group(1)?.trim() ?? '';
      try {
        final productsResponse = await client
            .from('products')
            .select('id, name, unit')
            .ilike('name', '%$query%')
            .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
            .limit(5);

        if (productsResponse.isNotEmpty) {
          final productIds = productsResponse.map((p) => p['id']).toList();
          final movementsResponse = await client
              .from('stock_movements')
              .select('product_id, quantity')
              .inFilter('product_id', productIds)
              .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId);

          final Map<String, double> stockMap = {};
          for (var m in (movementsResponse as List)) {
            final pid = m['product_id']?.toString() ?? '';
            stockMap[pid] = (stockMap[pid] ?? 0.0) + ((m['quantity'] ?? 0).toDouble());
          }

          final List<String> stockList = [];
          for (var p in productsResponse) {
            final pid = p['id']?.toString() ?? '';
            final double currentStock = stockMap[pid] ?? 0.0;
            final String formattedStock = currentStock == currentStock.toInt()
                ? currentStock.toInt().toString()
                : currentStock.toStringAsFixed(2);
            stockList.add('${p['name']}: $formattedStock ${p['unit'] ?? ''}');
          }

          return AgentActionEntity(
            type: AgentActionType.stockQuery,
            title: 'Ombor qoldig\'i: $query',
            target: query,
            details: 'Hozirgi ombordagi qoldiq ko\'rsatkichlari:',
            isSuccess: true,
            impactedItems: stockList,
          );
        } else {
          return AgentActionEntity(
            type: AgentActionType.stockQuery,
            title: 'Ombor qoldig\'i: $query',
            target: query,
            details: '$query nomli mahsulot omborda topilmadi.',
            isSuccess: false,
          );
        }
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint('AI Stock query error: $e');
      }
    }

    // 3. SALES QUERY
    final salesRegex = RegExp(r'\[ACTION:SALES\]');
    if (salesRegex.hasMatch(response)) {
      try {
        final today = DateTime.now().toIso8601String().substring(0, 10);
        final salesRes = await client
            .from('sales')
            .select('total_amount')
            .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
            .gte('created_at', today);

        double total = 0;
        final count = (salesRes as List).length;
        for (var s in salesRes) {
          total += (s['total_amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        }
        final avg = count > 0 ? (total / count).round() : 0;

        AuditService().logAction(
          department: 'pos',
          action: 'Savdo so\'rovi (AI)',
          details: 'Bugungi kassa va tushum ma\'lumoti tekshirildi.',
        );

        return AgentActionEntity(
          type: AgentActionType.salesQuery,
          title: 'Bugungi savdo ko\'rsatkichlari',
          target: 'Bugun',
          details: 'Kassa va tushum holati:',
          isSuccess: true,
          impactedItems: [
            'Jami tushum: ${NumberFormat('#,###').format(total)} so\'m',
            'Cheklar soni: $count ta',
            'O\'rtacha chek: ${NumberFormat('#,###').format(avg)} so\'m',
          ],
        );
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint('AI Sales query error: $e');
      }
    }

    // 4. LOW STOCK ACTION
    final lowStockRegex = RegExp(r'\[ACTION:LOW_STOCK\]');
    if (lowStockRegex.hasMatch(response)) {
      try {
        final lowStockRes = await client
            .from('products_with_stock')
            .select('name, current_stock, minimum_stock, unit')
            .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
            .eq('is_active', true);

        final List<String> items = [];
        for (var p in (lowStockRes as List)) {
          final cur = (p['current_stock'] ?? 0).toDouble();
          final min = (p['minimum_stock'] ?? 0).toDouble();
          if (min > 0 && cur <= min) {
            items.add('${p['name']}: $cur ${p['unit']} (min: $min)');
          }
        }

        return AgentActionEntity(
          type: AgentActionType.lowStockQuery,
          title: 'Kam qolgan mahsulotlar',
          target: 'Ombor',
          details: items.isEmpty
              ? 'Hozirda barcha mahsulotlar yetarli miqdorda mavjud.'
              : 'Quyidagi mahsulotlar minimal qoldiqdan kam qolgan:',
          isSuccess: true,
          impactedItems: items.isEmpty ? ['Barcha zaxiralar me`yorida'] : items,
        );
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint('AI Low Stock query error: $e');
      }
    }

    // 5. FINANCE ACTION
    final financeRegex = RegExp(r'\[ACTION:FINANCE\]');
    if (financeRegex.hasMatch(response)) {
      try {
        final now = DateTime.now();
        final startOfMonth = DateTime(now.year, now.month, 1).toIso8601String();
        final salesRes = await client
            .from('sales')
            .select('total_amount')
            .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
            .eq('status', 'completed')
            .gte('date', startOfMonth);
        double monthlySales = 0;
        for (var s in (salesRes as List)) {
          monthlySales += (s['total_amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        }

        final expRes = await client
            .from('expenses')
            .select('amount')
            .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
            .gte('expense_date', startOfMonth);
        double monthlyExp = 0;
        for (var e in (expRes as List)) {
          monthlyExp += (e['amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        }

        final estProfit = monthlySales - monthlyExp;
        final margin = monthlySales > 0 ? (estProfit / monthlySales) * 100 : 0.0;

        return AgentActionEntity(
          type: AgentActionType.financeQuery,
          title: 'Oylik moliya va foyda tahlili',
          target: 'Moliya',
          details: 'Shu oydagi moliyaviy holat ko\'rsatkichlari:',
          isSuccess: true,
          impactedItems: [
            'Oylik tushum: ${NumberFormat('#,###').format(monthlySales)} so\'m',
            'Operatsion xarajatlar: ${NumberFormat('#,###').format(monthlyExp)} so\'m',
            'Kutilayotgan foyda: ${NumberFormat('#,###').format(estProfit)} so\'m',
            'Rentabellik: ${margin.toStringAsFixed(1)}%',
          ],
        );
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint('AI Finance query error: $e');
      }
    }

    // 6. EXPENSE ACTION
    final expenseRegex = RegExp(r'\[ACTION:EXPENSE:(.+?):([0-9.]+)\]');
    final expenseMatch = expenseRegex.firstMatch(response);
    if (expenseMatch != null) {
      final category = expenseMatch.group(1)?.trim() ?? 'Boshqa';
      final amount = double.tryParse(expenseMatch.group(2) ?? '0') ?? 0.0;

      if (amount > 0) {
        try {
          await client.from('expenses').insert({
            'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
            'category': category,
            'amount': amount,
            'expense_date': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
          });

          AuditService().logAction(
            department: 'finance',
            action: 'Xarajat kiritildi (AI)',
            details: '$category uchun ${NumberFormat('#,###').format(amount)} so\'m xarajat kiritildi.',
          );

          return AgentActionEntity(
            type: AgentActionType.expense,
            title: 'Xarajat: $category',
            target: category,
            quantity: amount,
            unit: 'so\'m',
            details: '${NumberFormat('#,###').format(amount)} so\'m xarajatlar ro\'yxatiga kiritildi.',
            isSuccess: true,
            impactedItems: [
              'Toifa: $category',
              'Summa: ${NumberFormat('#,###').format(amount)} so\'m',
            ],
          );
        } catch (e) {
          debugPrint('AI Expense action error: $e');
        }
      }
    }

    // 5. WASTE ACTION
    final wasteRegex = RegExp(r'\[ACTION:WASTE:(.+?):([0-9.]+)\]');
    final wasteMatch = wasteRegex.firstMatch(response);
    if (wasteMatch != null) {
      final productName = wasteMatch.group(1)?.trim() ?? '';
      final quantity = double.tryParse(wasteMatch.group(2) ?? '0') ?? 0.0;

      if (quantity > 0) {
        try {
          final productsResponse = await client
              .from('products')
              .select('id, name, unit')
              .ilike('name', '%$productName%')
              .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
              .limit(1);

          if (productsResponse.isNotEmpty) {
            final product = productsResponse.first;
            final actualName = product['name'] ?? productName;
            final unit = product['unit'] ?? 'dona';

            await client.from('stock_movements').insert({
              'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
              'product_id': product['id'],
              'quantity': -quantity,
              'unit': unit,
              'type': 'waste',
              'reason': 'AI orqali spisaniya: $actualName x $quantity',
            });

            AuditService().logAction(
              department: 'warehouse',
              action: 'Chiqit / Spisaniya (AI)',
              details: '$actualName dan $quantity $unit hisobdan chiqarildi.',
            );

            return AgentActionEntity(
              type: AgentActionType.waste,
              title: 'Spisaniya: $actualName',
              target: actualName,
              quantity: quantity,
              unit: unit,
              details: '$actualName ombordan hisobdan chiqarildi (spisaniya).',
              isSuccess: true,
              impactedItems: [
                '$actualName: -$quantity $unit',
              ],
            );
          }
        } catch (e) {
          debugPrint('AI Waste action error: $e');
        }
      }
    }

    return null;
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/ai_agent_action_entity.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';

class AgentActionCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final AgentActionEntity action;

  const AgentActionCard({
    super.key,
    required this.action,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    Color themeColor;
    IconData actionIcon;

    switch (action.type) {
      case AgentActionType.produce:
        themeColor = Colors.deepOrange;
        actionIcon = Icons.soup_kitchen;
        break;
      case AgentActionType.expense:
        themeColor = Colors.amber;
        actionIcon = Icons.monetization_on;
        break;
      case AgentActionType.waste:
        themeColor = Colors.redAccent;
        actionIcon = Icons.delete_sweep;
        break;
      case AgentActionType.stockQuery:
        themeColor = Colors.blueAccent;
        actionIcon = Icons.inventory_2;
        break;
      case AgentActionType.salesQuery:
        themeColor = Colors.green;
        actionIcon = Icons.point_of_sale;
        break;
      case AgentActionType.lowStockQuery:
        themeColor = Colors.deepOrangeAccent;
        actionIcon = Icons.warning_amber_rounded;
        break;
      case AgentActionType.financeQuery:
        themeColor = Colors.purpleAccent;
        actionIcon = Icons.insights;
        break;
      case AgentActionType.none:
        themeColor = Colors.teal;
        actionIcon = Icons.bolt;
        break;
    }

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8, bottom: 4),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(
          color: action.isSuccess
              ? themeColor.withValues(alpha: 0.4)
              : Colors.redAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.4),
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: themeColor.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: Icon(actionIcon, size: 18, color: themeColor),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  action.title,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: 14,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: (action.isSuccess ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error)
                      .withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      action.isSuccess ? Icons.check_circle : Icons.error_outline,
                      size: 12,
                      color: action.isSuccess ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Text(
                      action.isSuccess
                          ? 'ai_assistant.action_success'.tr()
                          : 'ai_assistant.action_failed'.tr(),
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: action.isSuccess ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error,
                        fontSize: 10,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            action.details,
            style: const TextStyle(
              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
              fontSize: 12,
              height: 1.3,
            ),
          ),
          if (action.impactedItems.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            const Divider(color: Colors.white10, height: 1),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              'ai_assistant.ingredients_deducted'.tr(),
              style: TextStyle(
                color: themeColor,
                fontSize: 11,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            ...action.impactedItems.map(
              (item) => Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 2),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.remove, size: 12, color: themeColor),
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        item,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                          fontSize: 12,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.history, size: 12, color: Colors.blueAccent),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Text(
                'ai_assistant.audit_recorded'.tr(),
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.blueAccent,
                  fontSize: 10,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';

class QuickVoiceSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  final Function(String command) onSelectCommand;

  const QuickVoiceSheet({
    super.key,
    required this.onSelectCommand,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 24),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Center(
            child: Container(
              width: 36,
              height: 4,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white24,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.bolt, color: Colors.amber, size: 22),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text(
                'ai_assistant.quick_commands'.tr(),
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
              const Spacer(),
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: AppColors.textSecondary, size: 20),
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          Flexible(
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  _buildCommandTile(
                    context,
                    icon: Icons.soup_kitchen,
                    iconColor: Colors.deepOrange,
                    title: 'ai_assistant.command_produce_sample'.tr(),
                    subtitle: 'ai_assistant.sample_produce_desc'.tr(),
                  ),
                  _buildCommandTile(
                    context,
                    icon: Icons.inventory_2,
                    iconColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                    title: 'ai_assistant.command_stock_sample'.tr(),
                    subtitle: 'ai_assistant.sample_stock_desc'.tr(),
                  ),
                  _buildCommandTile(
                    context,
                    icon: Icons.warning_amber_rounded,
                    iconColor: Colors.deepOrangeAccent,
                    title: 'ai_assistant.command_low_stock'.tr(),
                    subtitle: 'ai_assistant.sample_low_stock_desc'.tr(),
                  ),
                  _buildCommandTile(
                    context,
                    icon: Icons.point_of_sale,
                    iconColor: Colors.green,
                    title: 'ai_assistant.command_sales_sample'.tr(),
                    subtitle: 'ai_assistant.sample_sales_desc'.tr(),
                  ),
                  _buildCommandTile(
                    context,
                    icon: Icons.insights,
                    iconColor: Colors.purpleAccent,
                    title: 'ai_assistant.command_finance_sample'.tr(),
                    subtitle: 'ai_assistant.sample_finance_desc'.tr(),
                  ),
                  _buildCommandTile(
                    context,
                    icon: Icons.monetization_on,
                    iconColor: Colors.amber,
                    title: 'ai_assistant.command_expense_sample'.tr(),
                    subtitle: 'ai_assistant.sample_expense_desc'.tr(),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCommandTile(
    BuildContext context, {
    required IconData icon,
    required Color iconColor,
    required String title,
    required String subtitle,
  }) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: () {
        Navigator.pop(context);
        onSelectCommand(title);
      },
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: AppColors.surfaceLight,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: iconColor.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              ),
              child: Icon(icon, color: iconColor, size: 20),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    title,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                      fontSize: 14,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 2),
                  Text(
                    subtitle,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                      fontSize: 11,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios, size: 14, color: Colors.white30),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\ai_assistant\presentation\widgets\telegram_chat_bubble.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/ai_message_entity.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/ai_agent_action_entity.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'agent_action_card.dart';

class TelegramChatBubble extends StatelessWidget {
  final AiMessageEntity message;

  const TelegramChatBubble({
    super.key,
    required this.message,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isUser = message.isUser;
    final timeStr = DateFormat('HH:mm').format(message.timestamp);
    final hasAction = message.actionData != null;

    return Align(
      alignment: isUser ? Alignment.centerRight : Alignment.centerLeft,
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4, horizontal: 8),
        constraints: BoxConstraints(
          maxWidth: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.82,
        ),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: isUser
              ? const Color(0xFF246BFD)
              : const Color(0xFF1E2633),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16).copyWith(
            bottomRight: isUser ? const Radius.circular(3) : const Radius.circular(16),
            bottomLeft: !isUser ? const Radius.circular(3) : const Radius.circular(16),
          ),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
              offset: const Offset(0, 1),
              blurRadius: 3,
            ),
          ],
          border: Border.all(
            color: isUser
                ? Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.3)
                : Colors.white12,
            width: 0.5,
          ),
        ),
        child: Material(
          color: Colors.transparent,
          child: InkWell(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            onLongPress: () {
              Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: message.text));
              HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text('staff.link_copied'.tr()),
                  duration: const Duration(seconds: 1),
                ),
              );
            },
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  if (!isUser) ...[
                    Row(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(3),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                            shape: BoxShape.circle,
                          ),
                          child: const Icon(
                            Icons.auto_awesome,
                            size: 12,
                            color: Colors.blueAccent,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                        const Text(
                          'ChefControl AI',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Colors.blueAccent,
                            fontSize: 11,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  ],
                  if (message.text.isNotEmpty)
                    Text(
                      message.text,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: isUser ? Colors.white : AppColors.textPrimary,
                        fontSize: 15,
                        height: 1.35,
                      ),
                    ),
                  if (hasAction) ...[
                    AgentActionCard(
                      action: AgentActionEntity.fromMap(message.actionData!),
                    ),
                  ],
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                    children: [
                      const Spacer(),
                      Text(
                        timeStr,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: isUser ? Colors.white70 : Colors.white38,
                          fontSize: 11,
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (isUser) ...[
                        const SizedBox(width: 4),
                        const Icon(
                          Icons.done_all,
                          size: 14,
                          color: Colors.white70,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\ai_assistant\presentation\widgets\telegram_voice_bubble.dart
