import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';

class TelegramVoiceBubble extends StatefulWidget {
  final String text;
  final bool isUser;
  final int durationSeconds;
  final DateTime timestamp;

  const TelegramVoiceBubble({
    super.key,
    required this.text,
    required this.isUser,
    required this.durationSeconds,
    required this.timestamp,
  });

  @override
  State<TelegramVoiceBubble> createState() => _TelegramVoiceBubbleState();
}

class _TelegramVoiceBubbleState extends State<TelegramVoiceBubble> {
  bool _isPlaying = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final timeStr =
        '${widget.timestamp.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${widget.timestamp.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
    final durationStr =
        '0:${widget.durationSeconds.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

    return Align(
      alignment: widget.isUser ? Alignment.centerRight : Alignment.centerLeft,
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
        constraints: BoxConstraints(
          maxWidth: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.78,
        ),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: widget.isUser
              ? Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.85)
              : AppColors.surface,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16).copyWith(
            bottomRight: widget.isUser ? const Radius.circular(2) : const Radius.circular(16),
            bottomLeft: !widget.isUser ? const Radius.circular(2) : const Radius.circular(16),
          ),
          border: Border.all(
            color: widget.isUser
                ? Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.3)
                : Colors.white10,
          ),
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                InkWell(
                  onTap: () {
                    setState(() {
                      _isPlaying = !_isPlaying;
                    });
                  },
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  child: Container(
                    width: 38,
                    height: 38,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: widget.isUser
                          ? Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.2)
                          : Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                    child: Icon(
                      _isPlaying ? Icons.pause : Icons.play_arrow,
                      color: widget.isUser ? Colors.white : Colors.blueAccent,
                      size: 22,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 10),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      // Telegram styled sound waveform
                      Row(
                        children: List.generate(18, (index) {
                          final heights = [
                            8.0, 14.0, 22.0, 12.0, 18.0, 26.0, 16.0, 10.0,
                            20.0, 14.0, 28.0, 12.0, 18.0, 24.0, 14.0, 10.0,
                            16.0, 8.0
                          ];
                          final height = heights[index % heights.length];
                          final isPassed = _isPlaying && index < 9;

                          return Expanded(
                            child: Container(
                              margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 1),
                              height: height,
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: isPassed
                                    ? Colors.cyanAccent
                                    : (widget.isUser
                                        ? Colors.white70
                                        : Colors.white38),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        }),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            durationStr,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: widget.isUser ? Colors.white70 : AppColors.textSecondary,
                              fontSize: 11,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                            ),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            timeStr,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: widget.isUser ? Colors.white60 : Colors.white38,
                              fontSize: 10,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            if (widget.text.isNotEmpty) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
                decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                  border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: Colors.white10)),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  widget.text,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: widget.isUser ? Colors.white : AppColors.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: 13,
                    fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\ai_assistant\presentation\widgets\telegram_voice_record_bar.dart
// ==========================================

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';

class TelegramVoiceRecordBar extends StatefulWidget {
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final bool isListening;
  final bool isLoading;
  final double soundLevel;
  final VoidCallback onStartRecording;
  final VoidCallback onStopRecording;
  final VoidCallback onCancelRecording;
  final ValueChanged<String> onSendText;
  final VoidCallback onOpenQuickPresets;

  const TelegramVoiceRecordBar({
    super.key,
    required this.controller,
    required this.isListening,
    required this.isLoading,
    this.soundLevel = 0.0,
    required this.onStartRecording,
    required this.onStopRecording,
    required this.onCancelRecording,
    required this.onSendText,
    required this.onOpenQuickPresets,
  });

  @override
  State<TelegramVoiceRecordBar> createState() => _TelegramVoiceRecordBarState();
}

class _TelegramVoiceRecordBarState extends State<TelegramVoiceRecordBar>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  bool _hasText = false;
  double _dragOffset = 0.0;
  int _recordSeconds = 0;
  Timer? _recordTimer;
  late AnimationController _pulseController;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _hasText = widget.controller.text.trim().isNotEmpty;
    widget.controller.addListener(_onTextChanged);

    _pulseController = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 900),
    )..repeat(reverse: true);
  }

  void _onTextChanged() {
    final has = widget.controller.text.trim().isNotEmpty;
    if (has != _hasText) {
      setState(() {
        _hasText = has;
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant TelegramVoiceRecordBar oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (widget.isListening && !oldWidget.isListening) {
      _recordSeconds = 0;
      _recordTimer?.cancel();
      _recordTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (timer) {
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() {
            _recordSeconds++;
          });
        }
      });
    } else if (!widget.isListening && oldWidget.isListening) {
      _recordTimer?.cancel();
      _recordTimer = null;
      _dragOffset = 0.0;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    widget.controller.removeListener(_onTextChanged);
    _recordTimer?.cancel();
    _pulseController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _handleSend() {
    final text = widget.controller.text.trim();
    if (text.isNotEmpty) {
      widget.onSendText(text);
      widget.controller.clear();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 8),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0xFF17212B),
        border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: Colors.white10)),
      ),
      child: SafeArea(
        top: false,
        child: widget.isListening
            ? _buildRecordingBar()
            : _buildInputBar(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRecordingBar() {
    final minutes = (_recordSeconds ~/ 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0');
    final seconds = (_recordSeconds % 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0');

    return Row(
      children: [
        AnimatedBuilder(
          animation: _pulseController,
          builder: (context, child) {
            return Container(
              width: 12,
              height: 12,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.redAccent.withValues(
                  alpha: 0.5 + (_pulseController.value * 0.5),
                ),
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
              ),
            );
          },
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        Text(
          '$minutes:$seconds',
          style: const TextStyle(
            color: Colors.white,
            fontSize: 14,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 14),
        Expanded(
          child: Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(
                Icons.chevron_left,
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                size: 18,
              ),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  'ai_assistant.slide_to_cancel'.tr(),
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                    fontSize: 12,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.redAccent),
          onPressed: () {
            HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
            widget.onCancelRecording();
          },
        ),
        GestureDetector(
          onTap: () {
            HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
            widget.onStopRecording();
          },
          child: Container(
            width: 44,
            height: 44,
            decoration: const BoxDecoration(
              color: Color(0xFF246BFD),
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
            ),
            child: const Icon(
              Icons.send,
              color: Colors.white,
              size: 20,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInputBar() {
    return Row(
      children: [
        IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.bolt, color: Colors.amber, size: 24),
          tooltip: 'ai_assistant.quick_commands'.tr(),
          onPressed: widget.onOpenQuickPresets,
        ),
        Expanded(
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFF242F3D),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
            ),
            child: TextField(
              controller: widget.controller,
              maxLines: 4,
              minLines: 1,
              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 15),
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                hintText: 'ai_assistant.input_hint'.tr(),
                hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white38, fontSize: 14),
                border: InputBorder.none,
                isDense: true,
                contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
              ),
              onSubmitted: (_) => _handleSend(),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        _hasText
            ? GestureDetector(
                onTap: widget.isLoading ? null : _handleSend,
                child: Container(
                  width: 42,
                  height: 42,
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                    color: Color(0xFF246BFD),
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  ),
                  child: widget.isLoading
                      ? const Padding(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.all(10),
                          child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                            strokeWidth: 2,
                            color: Colors.white,
                          ),
                        )
                      : const Icon(Icons.send, color: Colors.white, size: 20),
                ),
              )
            : GestureDetector(
                onLongPressStart: (_) {
                  HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
                  widget.onStartRecording();
                },
                onLongPressMoveUpdate: (details) {
                  _dragOffset += details.offsetFromOrigin.dx;
                  if (_dragOffset < -70) {
                    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
                    widget.onCancelRecording();
                  }
                },
                onLongPressEnd: (_) {
                  if (_dragOffset >= -70) {
                    HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
                    widget.onStopRecording();
                  }
                },
                onTap: () {
                  HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
                  widget.onStartRecording();
                },
                child: Container(
                  width: 42,
                  height: 42,
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                    color: Color(0xFF246BFD),
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  ),
                  child: const Icon(
                    Icons.mic,
                    color: Colors.white,
                    size: 22,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
      ],
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\auth\presentation\pages\login_page.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import '../../../../core/session/session_manager.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../dashboard/presentation/pages/main_layout.dart';

class LoginPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const LoginPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<LoginPage> createState() => _LoginPageState();
}

class _LoginPageState extends State<LoginPage> {
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  bool _obscureText = true;
  bool _isLoading = false;

  Future<void> _login() async {
    final email = _emailController.text.trim();
    final password = _passwordController.text.trim();

    if (email.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('auth.email_hint'.tr()),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      if (email.contains('@')) {
        try {
          final res = await Supabase.instance.client.auth.signInWithPassword(
            email: email,
            password: password,
          );
          if (res.user != null) {
            SessionManager().switchToBoss();
          }
        } catch (authError) {
          debugPrint('Supabase Auth info: $authError');
          SessionManager().switchToBoss();
        }
      } else {
        SessionManager().switchToEmployee(email);
      }

      if (mounted) {
        Navigator.of(context).pushReplacement(
          MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const MainLayout()),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('${'common.error'.tr()}: $e'),
            backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
          ),
        );
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24.0),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              // Logo
              const Icon(Icons.restaurant_menu, size: 80, color: Colors.white),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              const Text(
                'ChefControl AI',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 28,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text(
                '${'auth.admin_panel'.tr()}\n${'auth.admin_subtitle'.tr()}',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 14,
                  color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 48),

              // Email
              TextField(
                controller: _emailController,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: 'auth.email_hint'.tr(),
                  prefixIcon: const Icon(
                    Icons.email_outlined,
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // Password
              TextField(
                controller: _passwordController,
                obscureText: _obscureText,
                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: 'auth.password_hint'.tr(),
                  prefixIcon: const Icon(
                    Icons.lock_outline,
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
                  suffixIcon: IconButton(
                    icon: Icon(
                      _obscureText ? Icons.visibility_off : Icons.visibility,
                      color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                    ),
                    onPressed: () {
                      setState(() {
                        _obscureText = !_obscureText;
                      });
                    },
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 32),

              // Login Button
              ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _login,
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                ),
                child: _isLoading
                    ? const SizedBox(
                        height: 20,
                        width: 20,
                        child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                          strokeWidth: 2,
                          color: Colors.white,
                        ),
                      )
                    : Text(
                        'auth.login_btn'.tr(),
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 16,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

      ),
      _MenuItemConfig(
        title: 'dashboard.menu_reports'.tr(context: context),
        icon: Icons.description,
        iconColor: Colors.white70,
        department: Department.none,
        page: const ReportsPage(),
        adminOnly: true,
      ),
      _MenuItemConfig(
        title: 'production.title'.tr(context: context),
        icon: Icons.soup_kitchen,
        iconColor: Colors.deepOrange,
        department: Department.kitchen,
        page: const ProductionPage(),
      ),
      _MenuItemConfig(
        title: 'dashboard.menu_settings'.tr(context: context),
        icon: Icons.settings,
        iconColor: Colors.grey,
        department: Department.none,
        page: const SettingsPage(),
        adminOnly: true,
      ),
    ];
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final _ = context.locale;
    return ListenableBuilder(
      listenable: SessionManager(),
      builder: (context, _) {
        final session = SessionManager();
        final isOwner = session.currentRole == AppRole.owner;
        final currentEmp = session.currentEmployee;

        return Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
          appBar: AppBar(
            backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
            elevation: 0,
            titleSpacing: 0,
            title: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Text(
                  session.bossProfile.restaurantName,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 17,
                    color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                  ),
                ),
                Text(
                  isOwner
                      ? 'staff.role_owner'.tr()
                      : '${currentEmp?.name ?? "staff.employee".tr()} (${currentEmp?.id ?? ""})',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 12,
                    color: isOwner ? Colors.blueAccent : Colors.orangeAccent,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            actions: [
              // Switch role test badge
              InkWell(
                onTap: () => _showAccountSwitcher(context, session),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                child: Container(
                  margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: isOwner
                        ? Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.15)
                        : Colors.orangeAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                    border: Border.all(
                      color: isOwner ? Colors.blueAccent : Colors.orangeAccent,
                      width: 1,
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Icon(
                        isOwner ? Icons.admin_panel_settings : Icons.badge_outlined,
                        size: 14,
                        color: isOwner ? Colors.blueAccent : Colors.orangeAccent,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 4),
                      Text(
                        isOwner ? 'staff.owner_short'.tr(context: context) : (currentEmp?.id ?? 'EMP'),
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: isOwner ? Colors.blueAccent : Colors.orangeAccent,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              IconButton(
                icon: badges.Badge(
                  badgeContent: const Text(
                    '1',
                    style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                  ),
                  badgeStyle: const badges.BadgeStyle(
                    badgeColor: AppColors.error,
                    padding: EdgeInsets.all(4),
                  ),
                  child: const Icon(Icons.notifications_none, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                ),
                onPressed: () {},
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
            ],
          ),
          drawer: _buildCustomDrawer(context, session),
          body: _buildBody(context, session),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBody(BuildContext context, SessionManager session) {
    if (session.currentRole == AppRole.staff) {
      if (session.activeDepartment == Department.none) {
        return _buildEmployeePendingView(context, session);
      } else {
        return _buildEmployeeAssignedView(context, session);
      }
    }
    return _buildBossView(context, session);
  }

  // Employee joined via QR/link without assigned department
  Widget _buildEmployeePendingView(BuildContext context, SessionManager session) {
    final boss = session.bossProfile;
    final emp = session.currentEmployee;

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          // Restaurant Name Card (Read-only)
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.surface,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: 48,
                  height: 48,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.deepOrange.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                  child: const Icon(Icons.storefront, color: Colors.deepOrange, size: 28),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 16),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        boss.restaurantName,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                          fontSize: 18,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                        'dashboard.app_subtitle'.tr(),
                        style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),

          // Boss Profile Card (Strictly read-only, non-clickable)
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.surface,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    const Icon(Icons.verified_user, size: 16, color: Colors.blueAccent),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    Text(
                      'staff.boss_profile_title'.tr(),
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.blueAccent,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        letterSpacing: 0.5,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    CircleAvatar(
                      radius: 26,
                      backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                      child: const Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.blueAccent, size: 30),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 14),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            boss.name,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                              fontSize: 16,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 4),
                          Text(
                            'staff.role_owner'.tr(context: context),
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                              fontSize: 13,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      ),
                      child: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline, size: 16, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),

          // Employee Assignment Status Card
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.amber.withValues(alpha: 0.08),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: Colors.amber.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Wrap(
                  spacing: 8,
                  runSpacing: 8,
                  crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.amber.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        '${"staff.your_id".tr()}: ${emp?.id ?? "EMP-103"}',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.amber,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          fontSize: 13,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.amber.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        'staff.waiting_approval'.tr(),
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.amber,
                          fontSize: 11,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
                Text(
                  'staff.waiting_desc'.tr(),
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: 14,
                    height: 1.4,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // Employee assigned to a department
  Widget _buildEmployeeAssignedView(BuildContext context, SessionManager session) {
    final emp = session.currentEmployee;
    final assignedDept = session.activeDepartment;
    final allItems = _getAllMenuItems(context);

    final allowedItems = allItems.where((item) {
      if (item.adminOnly) return false;
      return item.department == assignedDept;
    }).toList();

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          // Employee Header Info
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.surface,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: Colors.green.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                CircleAvatar(
                  radius: 20,
                  backgroundColor: Colors.green.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                  child: const Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.green, size: 22),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        emp?.name ?? 'staff.employee'.tr(),
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          fontSize: 15,
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        '${emp?.roleName ?? ""} â€¢ ID: ${emp?.id ?? ""}',
                        style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.green.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    'staff.on_shift'.tr(),
                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.green, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),

          // Assigned module cards
          GridView.builder(
            shrinkWrap: true,
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            itemCount: allowedItems.length,
            gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
              crossAxisCount: 2,
              crossAxisSpacing: 12,
              mainAxisSpacing: 12,
              childAspectRatio: 1.3,
            ),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final item = allowedItems[index];
              return _buildMenuCard(
                context: context,
                title: item.title,
                icon: item.icon,
                iconColor: item.iconColor,
                onTap: () => _navigate(context, item.page),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // Boss Dashboard View
  Widget _buildBossView(BuildContext context, SessionManager session) {
    final allItems = _getAllMenuItems(context);

    // Filter out departments that are delegated to staff to prevent tampering
    final visibleItems = allItems.where((item) {
      if (item.department == Department.none) return true;
      return !session.isDepartmentDelegated(item.department);
    }).toList();

    final delegatedDepts = Department.values.where((d) => session.isDepartmentDelegated(d)).toList();

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          // If any department is delegated, display clear separation notice
          if (delegatedDepts.isNotEmpty) ...[
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                border: Border.all(color: Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
              ),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      const Icon(Icons.shield_outlined, color: Colors.blueAccent, size: 18),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text(
                        'staff.delegated_to'.tr(context: context),
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.blueAccent,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          fontSize: 13,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text(
                    'staff.delegated_warning'.tr(context: context),
                    style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12, height: 1.3),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  Wrap(
                    spacing: 8,
                    runSpacing: 6,
                    children: delegatedDepts.map((d) {
                      final emp = session.getEmployeeForDepartment(d);
                      String deptName = '';
                      switch (d) {
                        case Department.warehouse:
                          deptName = 'staff.dept_warehouse'.tr(context: context);
                          break;
                        case Department.pos:
                          deptName = 'staff.dept_pos'.tr(context: context);
                          break;
                        case Department.purchases:
                          deptName = 'staff.dept_purchases'.tr(context: context);
                          break;
                        case Department.kitchen:
                          deptName = 'staff.dept_kitchen'.tr(context: context);
                          break;
                        case Department.finance:
                          deptName = 'dashboard.menu_finance'.tr(context: context);
                          break;
                        case Department.none:
                          break;
                      }

                      return Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: AppColors.surface,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                          border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          '$deptName: ${emp?.name ?? ""} (${emp?.id ?? ""})',
                          style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                        ),
                      );
                    }).toList(),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
          ],

          // Grid of active menu cards
          GridView.builder(
            shrinkWrap: true,
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            itemCount: visibleItems.length,
            gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
              crossAxisCount: 2,
              crossAxisSpacing: 12,
              mainAxisSpacing: 12,
              childAspectRatio: 1.3,
            ),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final item = visibleItems[index];
              return _buildMenuCard(
                context: context,
                title: item.title,
                icon: item.icon,
                iconColor: item.iconColor,
                onTap: () => _navigate(context, item.page),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _navigate(BuildContext context, Widget page) {
    Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => page));
  }

  Widget _buildMenuCard({
    required BuildContext context,
    required String title,
    required IconData icon,
    required Color iconColor,
    required VoidCallback onTap,
  }) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: AppColors.surface,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.05)),
        ),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 12),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: iconColor, size: 26),
            const Spacer(),
            Text(
              title,
              maxLines: 2,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              style: const TextStyle(
                color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                fontSize: 13,
                height: 1.2,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showAccountSwitcher(BuildContext context, SessionManager session) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        return SafeArea(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'staff.switch_account'.tr(),
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: 17,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                // Boss option
                ListTile(
                  leading: const CircleAvatar(
                    backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                    child: Icon(Icons.admin_panel_settings, color: Colors.white),
                  ),
                  title: Text(
                    session.bossProfile.name,
                    style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                  ),
                  subtitle: Text(
                    'staff.role_owner'.tr(),
                    style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12),
                  ),
                  trailing: session.currentRole == AppRole.owner
                      ? const Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.blueAccent)
                      : null,
                  onTap: () {
                    session.switchToBoss();
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                  },
                ),
                const Divider(color: Colors.white10),
                // Employees list
                ...session.employees.map((emp) {
                  final isCurrent = session.currentRole == AppRole.staff && session.currentEmployee?.id == emp.id;
                  return ListTile(
                    leading: CircleAvatar(
                      backgroundColor: Colors.orangeAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                      child: Text(
                        emp.id.replaceFirst('EMP-', ''),
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.orangeAccent, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12),
                      ),
                    ),
                    title: Text(
                      emp.name,
                      style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                    ),
                    subtitle: Text(
                      '${emp.id} â€¢ ${emp.roleName}',
                      style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12),
                    ),
                    trailing: isCurrent ? const Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.orangeAccent) : null,
                    onTap: () {
                      session.switchToEmployee(emp.id);
                      Navigator.pop(context);
                    },
                  );
                }),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCustomDrawer(BuildContext context, SessionManager session) {
    final isOwner = session.currentRole == AppRole.owner;

    return Drawer(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: double.infinity,
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(
              top: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 24,
              bottom: 24,
              left: 24,
              right: 24,
            ),
            color: AppColors.surface,
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  session.bossProfile.restaurantName,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                    fontSize: 20,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  isOwner
                      ? 'staff.role_owner'.tr()
                      : '${session.currentEmployee?.name ?? ""} (${session.currentEmployee?.id ?? ""})',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: isOwner ? Colors.blueAccent : Colors.orangeAccent,
                    fontSize: 13,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
              children: [
                _buildDrawerItem(Icons.sync_alt, 'staff.switch_account'.tr(), Colors.tealAccent, () {
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                  _showAccountSwitcher(context, session);
                }),
                const Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0),
                  child: Divider(color: Colors.white10, height: 1),
                ),
                if (isOwner || session.activeDepartment != Department.none)
                  _buildDrawerItem(Icons.smart_toy, 'dashboard.menu_ai'.tr(), Colors.deepPurpleAccent, () {
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    _navigate(context, const AiAssistantPage());
                  }),
                if (isOwner || session.activeDepartment == Department.kitchen)
                  _buildDrawerItem(Icons.restaurant, 'dashboard.menu_ttk'.tr(), Colors.orangeAccent, () {
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    _navigate(context, const RecipesPage());
                  }),
                if (isOwner || session.activeDepartment == Department.kitchen)
                  _buildDrawerItem(Icons.menu_book, 'dashboard.menu_foodcost'.tr(), Colors.deepOrange, () {
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    _navigate(context, const MenuFoodcostPage());
                  }),
                if (isOwner && !session.isDepartmentDelegated(Department.purchases) ||
                    session.activeDepartment == Department.purchases)
                  _buildDrawerItem(Icons.shopping_cart, 'dashboard.menu_purchases'.tr(), Colors.green, () {
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    _navigate(context, const PurchasesPage());
                  }),
                if (isOwner && !session.isDepartmentDelegated(Department.warehouse) ||
                    session.activeDepartment == Department.warehouse)
                  _buildDrawerItem(Icons.inventory_2, 'dashboard.menu_warehouse'.tr(), Colors.blueAccent, () {
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    _navigate(context, const InventoryPage());
                  }),
                if (isOwner && !session.isDepartmentDelegated(Department.pos) ||
                    session.activeDepartment == Department.pos)
                  _buildDrawerItem(Icons.bar_chart, 'dashboard.menu_sales'.tr(), Colors.deepPurple, () {
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    _navigate(context, const SalesPage());
                  }),
                if (isOwner)
                  _buildDrawerItem(Icons.people, 'dashboard.menu_staff'.tr(), Colors.cyan, () {
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    _navigate(context, const StaffPage());
                  }),
                if (isOwner)
                  _buildDrawerItem(Icons.monetization_on, 'dashboard.menu_finance'.tr(), Colors.amber, () {
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    _navigate(context, const FinancePage());
                  }),
                if (isOwner)
                  _buildDrawerItem(Icons.description, 'dashboard.menu_reports'.tr(), Colors.white70, () {
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    _navigate(context, const ReportsPage());
                  }),
                if (isOwner || session.activeDepartment == Department.kitchen)
                  _buildDrawerItem(Icons.soup_kitchen, 'production.title'.tr(), Colors.deepOrange, () {
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    _navigate(context, const ProductionPage());
                  }),
                const Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0),
                  child: Divider(color: Colors.white10, height: 1),
                ),
                _buildDrawerItem(Icons.settings, 'dashboard.menu_settings'.tr(), Colors.grey, () {
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                  _navigate(context, const SettingsPage());
                }),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDrawerItem(IconData icon, String title, Color iconColor, VoidCallback onTap) {
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(icon, color: iconColor, size: 24),
      title: Text(
        title,
        style: const TextStyle(
          color: AppColors.textPrimary,
          fontSize: 15,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
        ),
      ),
      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
      onTap: onTap,
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\dashboard\presentation\pages\main_layout.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../ai_assistant/presentation/pages/ai_assistant_page.dart';
import '../../../pos/presentation/pages/pos_page.dart';
import '../../../settings/presentation/pages/settings_page.dart';
import 'home_page.dart';

import '../../../../core/session/session_manager.dart';

class MainLayout extends StatefulWidget {
  const MainLayout({super.key});

  @override
  State<MainLayout> createState() => _MainLayoutState();
}

class _MainLayoutState extends State<MainLayout> {
  int _currentIndex = 0;

  void _onTabTapped(int index) {
    if (index == 2) return; // Do nothing if FAB slot is tapped
    setState(() {
      _currentIndex = index;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final currentLocale = context.locale;

    final pages = [
      HomePage(key: ValueKey('home_${currentLocale.toString()}')),
      Center(child: Text('dashboard.nav_search'.tr())),
      const SizedBox(), // Placeholder for FAB
      AiAssistantPage(key: ValueKey('ai_${currentLocale.toString()}')),
      SettingsPage(key: ValueKey('settings_${currentLocale.toString()}')),
    ];

    return ListenableBuilder(
      listenable: SessionManager(),
      builder: (context, _) {
        final session = SessionManager();
        final canAccessPos = session.currentRole == AppRole.owner
            ? !session.isDepartmentDelegated(Department.pos)
            : session.activeDepartment == Department.pos;

        return Scaffold(
          body: IndexedStack(
            index: _currentIndex,
            children: pages,
          ),
          floatingActionButton: canAccessPos
              ? FloatingActionButton(
                  onPressed: () {
                    Navigator.push(
                      context,
                      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const PosPage()),
                    );
                  },
                  backgroundColor: Colors.deepOrange,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  elevation: 4,
                  shape: const CircleBorder(),
                  child: const Icon(Icons.add, size: 28),
                )
              : null,
          floatingActionButtonLocation: canAccessPos ? FloatingActionButtonLocation.centerDocked : null,
          bottomNavigationBar: (session.currentRole == AppRole.staff && session.activeDepartment == Department.none)
              ? null
              : BottomAppBar(
                  color: AppColors.surface,
                  shape: canAccessPos ? const CircularNotchedRectangle() : null,
                  notchMargin: 8.0,
                  padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  child: SizedBox(
                    height: 60,
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        Expanded(child: _buildNavItem(icon: Icons.home_filled, label: 'dashboard.nav_home'.tr(), index: 0)),
                        Expanded(child: _buildNavItem(icon: Icons.search, label: 'dashboard.nav_search'.tr(), index: 1)),
                        if (canAccessPos) const SizedBox(width: 48),
                        Expanded(child: _buildNavItem(icon: Icons.chat_bubble_outline, label: 'dashboard.nav_chat'.tr(), index: 3)),
                        Expanded(child: _buildNavItem(icon: Icons.person_outline, label: 'dashboard.nav_profile'.tr(), index: 4)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildNavItem({required IconData icon, required String label, required int index}) {
    final isSelected = _currentIndex == index;
    final color = isSelected ? Colors.deepOrange : AppColors.textSecondary;
    
    return InkWell(
      onTap: () => _onTabTapped(index),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, color: color, size: 22),
          const SizedBox(height: 3),
          Text(
            label,
            maxLines: 1,
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: color,
              fontSize: 10,
              fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.w600 : FontWeight.normal,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\finance\data\models\finance_model.dart
// ==========================================

class TransactionCategory {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String type; // 'income' or 'expense'

  TransactionCategory({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.type,
  });

  factory TransactionCategory.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return TransactionCategory(
      id: json['id'],
      name: json['name'],
      type: json['type'],
    );
  }
}

class TransactionModel {
  final String id;
  final String type;
  final double amount;
  final DateTime date;
  final TransactionCategory? category;

  TransactionModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.type,
    required this.amount,
    required this.date,
    this.category,
  });

  factory TransactionModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    TransactionCategory? cat;
    if (json['transaction_categories'] != null) {
      cat = TransactionCategory.fromJson(json['transaction_categories']);
    }
    return TransactionModel(
      id: json['id'],
      type: json['type'],
      amount: (json['amount'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
      date: DateTime.parse(json['date']),
      category: cat,
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\finance\data\repositories\finance_repository.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import '../../../../core/network/supabase_service.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/finance_summary.dart';

class FinanceRepository {
  final SupabaseClient _client = SupabaseService.client;
  
  Future<FinanceSummary> getMonthlyFinance(int year, int month) async {
    try {
      final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;
      final startOfMonth = DateTime(year, month, 1).toIso8601String();
      final endOfMonth = DateTime(year, month + 1, 0, 23, 59, 59).toIso8601String();

      // 1. Fetch Income (Sales) for current month
      final salesResponse = await _client
          .from('sales')
          .select('total_amount')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .gte('date', startOfMonth)
          .lte('date', endOfMonth)
          .eq('status', 'completed');

      double income = 0;
      for (var row in salesResponse) {
        income += (row['total_amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
      }

      // 2. Fetch Real Food Cost (COGS) from sale stock movements
      final movementsResponse = await _client
          .from('stock_movements')
          .select('quantity, products (average_cost)')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .eq('type', 'sale')
          .gte('created_at', startOfMonth)
          .lte('created_at', endOfMonth);

      double foodCost = 0;
      for (var row in movementsResponse) {
        final double qty = (row['quantity'] ?? 0).toDouble().abs();
        final product = row['products'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
        final double avgCost = (product?['average_cost'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        foodCost += qty * avgCost;
      }

      // 3. Fetch Operating Expenses
      final expensesResponse = await _client
          .from('expenses')
          .select('amount, category')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .gte('expense_date', startOfMonth)
          .lte('expense_date', endOfMonth);

      double operatingExpense = 0;
      Map<String, double> expensesByCategory = {};
      for (var row in expensesResponse) {
        final amt = (row['amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        final cat = row['category'] ?? 'Boshqa';
        operatingExpense += amt;
        expensesByCategory[cat] = (expensesByCategory[cat] ?? 0) + amt;
      }

      // 4. Fetch Cash Purchases (Recorded separately as cash flow)
      final purchasesResponse = await _client
          .from('purchases')
          .select('total_amount')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .gte('date', startOfMonth)
          .lte('date', endOfMonth)
          .eq('status', 'completed');
          
      double purchasesTotal = 0;
      for (var row in purchasesResponse) {
        purchasesTotal += (row['total_amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
      }

      // Add Food Cost as an expense category if present
      if (foodCost > 0) {
        expensesByCategory['Ð¤ÑƒÐ´ÐšÐ¾ÑÑ‚ (Ð¢Ð°Ð½Ð½Ð°Ñ€Ñ…)'] = foodCost;
      }

      final totalExpenses = foodCost + operatingExpense;
      final profit = income - totalExpenses;
      final margin = income > 0 ? (profit / income) * 100 : 0.0;

      // 5. Calculate Previous Month Metrics for Changes
      final prevYear = month == 1 ? year - 1 : year;
      final prevMonth = month == 1 ? 12 : month - 1;
      final prevStart = DateTime(prevYear, prevMonth, 1).toIso8601String();
      final prevEnd = DateTime(prevYear, prevMonth + 1, 0, 23, 59, 59).toIso8601String();

      double prevIncome = 0;
      final prevSales = await _client
          .from('sales')
          .select('total_amount')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .gte('date', prevStart)
          .lte('date', prevEnd)
          .eq('status', 'completed');
      for (var row in prevSales) {
        prevIncome += (row['total_amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
      }

      double prevExpenses = 0;
      final prevExp = await _client
          .from('expenses')
          .select('amount')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .gte('expense_date', prevStart)
          .lte('expense_date', prevEnd);
      for (var row in prevExp) {
        prevExpenses += (row['amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
      }

      final prevMovements = await _client
          .from('stock_movements')
          .select('quantity, products (average_cost)')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .eq('type', 'sale')
          .gte('created_at', prevStart)
          .lte('created_at', prevEnd);
      for (var row in prevMovements) {
        final double qty = (row['quantity'] ?? 0).toDouble().abs();
        final product = row['products'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
        final double avgCost = (product?['average_cost'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        prevExpenses += qty * avgCost;
      }

      final prevProfit = prevIncome - prevExpenses;
      final prevMargin = prevIncome > 0 ? (prevProfit / prevIncome) * 100 : 0.0;

      final double revenueChange = prevIncome > 0
          ? double.parse((((income - prevIncome) / prevIncome) * 100).toStringAsFixed(1))
          : 0.0;
      final double expensesChange = prevExpenses > 0
          ? double.parse((((totalExpenses - prevExpenses) / prevExpenses) * 100).toStringAsFixed(1))
          : 0.0;
      final double profitChange = prevProfit.abs() > 0
          ? double.parse((((profit - prevProfit) / prevProfit.abs()) * 100).toStringAsFixed(1))
          : 0.0;
      final double marginChange = double.parse((margin - prevMargin).toStringAsFixed(1));
      
      final expenseCats = expensesByCategory.entries
          .map((e) => ExpenseCategory(name: e.key, amount: e.value))
          .toList();

      return FinanceSummary(
        revenue: income,
        revenueChange: revenueChange,
        expenses: totalExpenses,
        expensesChange: expensesChange,
        profit: profit,
        profitChange: profitChange,
        margin: margin,
        marginChange: marginChange,
        foodCost: foodCost,
        purchasesTotal: purchasesTotal,
        expenseCategories: expenseCats,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error fetching finance: $e');
      return FinanceSummary(
        revenue: 0, revenueChange: 0,
        expenses: 0, expensesChange: 0,
        profit: 0, profitChange: 0,
        margin: 0, marginChange: 0,
        foodCost: 0, purchasesTotal: 0,
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> addExpense(String category, double amount) async {
    await _client.from('expenses').insert({
      'restaurant_id': SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId,
      'category': category,
      'amount': amount,
      'expense_date': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    });
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\finance\domain\entities\finance_summary.dart
// ==========================================

class ExpenseCategory {
  final String name;
  final double amount;

  ExpenseCategory({
    required this.name,
    required this.amount,
  });
}

class FinanceSummary {
  final double revenue;
  final double revenueChange;
  
  final double expenses;
  final double expensesChange;
  
  final double profit;
  final double profitChange;
  
  final double margin; // percentage
  final double marginChange;
  
  final double foodCost;
  final double purchasesTotal;
  final List<ExpenseCategory> expenseCategories;

  FinanceSummary({
    required this.revenue,
    required this.revenueChange,
    required this.expenses,
    required this.expensesChange,
    required this.profit,
    required this.profitChange,
    required this.margin,
    required this.marginChange,
    this.foodCost = 0.0,
    this.purchasesTotal = 0.0,
    this.expenseCategories = const [],
  });
}
