import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../providers/finance_provider.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

class FinancePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const FinancePage({super.key});

  @override
  State<FinancePage> createState() => _FinancePageState();
}

class _FinancePageState extends State<FinancePage> {

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      context.read<FinanceProvider>().fetchFinance();
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        elevation: 0,
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
        title: Text('finance.title'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.more_vert, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
            onPressed: () {},
          )
        ],
      ),
      body: DefaultTabController(
        length: 2,
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // Segmented Control
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
              child: Container(
                height: 40,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.surface,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: TabBar(
                  indicator: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.blueAccent,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                  ),
                  indicatorSize: TabBarIndicatorSize.tab,
                  labelColor: Colors.white,
                  unselectedLabelColor: AppColors.textSecondary,
                  dividerColor: Colors.transparent,
                  tabs: [
                    Tab(text: 'finance.income'.tr()),
                    Tab(text: 'finance.expenses'.tr()),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: Consumer<FinanceProvider>(
                builder: (context, provider, child) {
                  if (provider.isLoading) {
                    return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
                  }

                  if (provider.error != null) {
                    return Center(child: Text(provider.error!, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)));
                  }

                  final summary = provider.summary;
                  if (summary == null) {
                    return Center(child: Text('common.no_data'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)));
                  }

                  final formatCurrency = NumberFormat.currency(locale: 'ru', symbol: 'common.currency_sum'.tr(), decimalDigits: 0);

                  return TabBarView(
                    children: [
                      // Income Tab
                      ListView(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                        children: [
                          Row(
                            children: [
                              Expanded(child: _buildMetricCard('finance.revenue'.tr(), formatCurrency.format(summary.revenue), '${summary.revenueChange >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${summary.revenueChange}%', summary.revenueChange >= 0 ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error)),
                              const SizedBox(width: 12),
                              Expanded(child: _buildMetricCard('finance.net_profit'.tr(), formatCurrency.format(summary.profit), '${summary.profitChange >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${summary.profitChange}%', summary.profit >= 0 ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error)),
                            ],
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 24),
                          Text(
                            'finance.income_articles'.tr(),
                            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 16),
                          if (summary.revenue > 0)
                            _buildFinanceItem('finance.cat_revenue'.tr(), summary.revenue, true)
                          else
                            Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 24),
                              child: Center(
                                child: Text('common.no_data'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                              ),
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      // Expenses Tab
                      ListView(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                        children: [
                          Row(
                            children: [
                              Expanded(child: _buildMetricCard('finance.expenses'.tr(), formatCurrency.format(summary.expenses), '${summary.expensesChange >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${summary.expensesChange}%', summary.expensesChange >= 0 ? AppColors.error : AppColors.success)),
                              const SizedBox(width: 12),
                              Expanded(child: _buildMetricCard('finance.margin'.tr(), '${summary.margin.toStringAsFixed(1)}%', '${summary.marginChange >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${summary.marginChange}%', summary.margin >= 20 ? AppColors.success : AppColors.warning)),
                            ],
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 24),
                          Text(
                            'finance.expense_structure'.tr(),
                            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 16),
                          Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: AppColors.surface,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                              border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                            ),
                            child: Row(
                              children: [
                                Container(
                                  width: 100,
                                  height: 100,
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                    border: Border.all(color: Colors.blueAccent, width: 20),
                                  ),
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(width: 24),
                                Expanded(
                                  child: summary.expenseCategories.isEmpty
                                      ? Center(child: Text('common.no_data'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)))
                                      : Column(
                                          children: summary.expenseCategories.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                                            const colors = [Colors.blue, Colors.orange, Colors.green, Colors.red, Colors.purple, Colors.teal, Colors.amber];
                                            final c = entry.value;
                                            final pct = summary.expenses > 0
                                                ? (c.amount / summary.expenses * 100).toStringAsFixed(1)
                                                : '0';
                                            return _buildLegendItem(
                                              colors[entry.key % colors.length],
                                              c.name,
                                              '$pct%',
                                            );
                                          }).toList(),
                                        ),
                                )
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 24),
                          Text(
                            'finance.expense_articles'.tr(),
                            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 16),
                          ...summary.expenseCategories.map((c) => _buildFinanceItem(c.name, c.amount, false)),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(
        backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
        onPressed: _showAddTransactionModal,
        child: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showAddTransactionModal() {
    final titleController = TextEditingController();
    final amountController = TextEditingController();

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        return Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.only(
            bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom,
            left: 20,
            right: 20,
            top: 20,
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text('finance.new_transaction'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              TextField(
                controller: titleController,
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'finance.category_hint'.tr(),
                  labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              TextField(
                controller: amountController,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'finance.amount_hint'.tr(),
                  labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              SizedBox(
                width: double.infinity,
                height: 50,
                child: ElevatedButton(
                  onPressed: () async {
                    final title = titleController.text.trim();
                    final amount = double.tryParse(amountController.text) ?? 0;
                    if (title.isNotEmpty && amount > 0) {
                      await context.read<FinanceProvider>().addExpense(title, amount);
                      if (context.mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
                    }
                  },
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  ),
                  child: Text('finance.add'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16)),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLegendItem(Color color, String label, String percent) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8.0),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(width: 12, height: 12, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle)),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              label,
              maxLines: 1,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 6),
          Text(percent, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMetricCard(String title, String value, String percent, Color percentColor) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(title, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(
                percent.startsWith('+') ? Icons.arrow_upward : Icons.arrow_downward, 
                color: percentColor, 
                size: 14
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Text(percent, style: TextStyle(color: percentColor, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ],
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFinanceItem(String titleKey, double amount, bool isIncome) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              titleKey,
              maxLines: 1,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Text(
            '${isIncome ? '+' : '-'} ${amount.toInt()} ${'common.currency_sum'.tr()}',
            style: TextStyle(
              color: isIncome ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error,
              fontSize: 16,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\finance\presentation\providers\finance_provider.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/finance_summary.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/finance_repository.dart';

class FinanceProvider extends ChangeNotifier {
  final FinanceRepository _repository = FinanceRepository();
  
  FinanceSummary? _summary;
  FinanceSummary? get summary => _summary;
  
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;
  
  String? _error;
  String? get error => _error;

  final bool _useMockData = false;
  DateTime _currentDate = DateTime.now();
  DateTime get currentDate => _currentDate;

  Future<void> fetchFinance({bool background = false}) async {
    if (!background && _summary == null) {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
      notifyListeners();
    }
    
    try {
      final data = await _repository.getMonthlyFinance(_currentDate.year, _currentDate.month);
      if (data.revenue == 0 && data.expenses == 0 && _useMockData) {
        _summary = _getMockSummary();
      } else {
        _summary = data;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      if (_useMockData) {
         _summary = _getMockSummary();
      }
    }
    if (_isLoading) {
      _isLoading = false;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void changeDate(DateTime newDate) {
    _currentDate = newDate;
    fetchFinance();
  }

  FinanceSummary _getMockSummary() {
    return FinanceSummary(
      revenue: 125000000,
      revenueChange: 15.0,
      expenses: 85000000,
      expensesChange: -5.0,
      profit: 40000000,
      profitChange: 10.0,
      margin: 32.0,
      marginChange: 2.0,
      expenseCategories: [
        ExpenseCategory(name: 'Ð¤ÑƒÐ´ÐšÐ¾ÑÑ‚ (ÐŸÑ€Ð¾Ð´ÑƒÐºÑ‚Ñ‹)', amount: 18495000),
        ExpenseCategory(name: 'Ð—Ð°Ñ€Ð¿Ð»Ð°Ñ‚Ð°', amount: 15000000),
        ExpenseCategory(name: 'ÐÑ€ÐµÐ½Ð´Ð°', amount: 12000000),
        ExpenseCategory(name: 'ÐÐ°Ð»Ð¾Ð³Ð¸', amount: 4110000),
        ExpenseCategory(name: 'ÐŸÑ€Ð¾Ñ‡ÐµÐµ', amount: 6445000),
      ],
    );
  }

  Future<void> addExpense(String category, double amount) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      await _repository.addExpense(category, amount);
      await fetchFinance(); // Refresh
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\inventory\data\models\product_model.dart
// ==========================================

class ProductModel {
  final String id;
  final String restaurantId;
  final String name;
  final String? normalizedName;
  final String category;
  final String unit;
  final double minimumStock;
  final bool isActive;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
  
  // For UI representation of current stock
  final double currentStock;
  final double averageCost;
  final bool isNew;

  ProductModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.restaurantId,
    required this.name,
    this.normalizedName,
    required this.category,
    required this.unit,
    required this.minimumStock,
    required this.isActive,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    this.currentStock = 0.0,
    this.averageCost = 0.0,
    this.isNew = false,
  });

  factory ProductModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return ProductModel(
      id: json['id'],
      restaurantId: json['restaurant_id'],
      name: json['name'],
      normalizedName: json['normalized_name'],
      category: json['category'] ?? 'Ð‘ÐµÐ· ÐºÐ°Ñ‚ÐµÐ³Ð¾Ñ€Ð¸Ð¸',
      unit: json['unit'] ?? 'ÑˆÑ‚',
      minimumStock: (json['minimum_stock'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
      isActive: json['is_active'] ?? true,
      createdAt: DateTime.tryParse(json['created_at']?.toString() ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt: DateTime.tryParse(json['updated_at']?.toString() ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
      currentStock: (json['current_stock'] ?? 0).toDouble(), // If we have a view
      averageCost: (json['average_cost'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
      isNew: json['is_new'] ?? false,
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\inventory\data\repositories\inventory_repository.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';
import '../../../../core/network/supabase_service.dart';
import '../models/product_model.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/inventory_item.dart';

class InventoryRepository {
  final SupabaseClient _client = SupabaseService.client;
  
  Future<List<InventoryItem>> getProducts() async {
    try {
      final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;
      
      // Fetch products with stock from SQL view
      final response = await _client
          .from('products_with_stock')
          .select('*')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId)
          .eq('is_active', true)
          .order('name');
          
      final List<ProductModel> models = (response as List)
          .map((json) => ProductModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
          
      return models.map((e) => InventoryItem(
        id: e.id,
        name: e.name,
        quantity: e.currentStock,
        unit: e.unit,
        totalValue: e.currentStock * e.averageCost,
        category: e.category,
        isLowStock: e.currentStock <= e.minimumStock,
        isNew: e.isNew,
      )).toList();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error fetching products: $e');
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<InventoryItem> addProduct({
    required String name,
    required String category,
    required String unit,
    required double minimumStock,
    double initialStock = 0,
    double costPrice = 0,
  }) async {
    final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;

    // Insert into products table
    final response = await _client.from('products').insert({
      'id': const Uuid().v4(),
      'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
      'name': name,
      'normalized_name': name.toLowerCase(),
      'category': category,
      'unit': unit,
      'minimum_stock': minimumStock,
      'average_cost': costPrice,
      'is_active': true,
    }).select().single();

    final ProductModel newProduct = ProductModel.fromJson(response);
    
    // Insert initial stock movement if quantity > 0
    if (initialStock > 0) {
      await _client.from('stock_movements').insert({
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
        'product_id': newProduct.id,
        'quantity': initialStock,
        'unit': unit,
        'type': 'initial_stock',
        'reason': 'ÐÐ°Ñ‡Ð°Ð»ÑŒÐ½Ñ‹Ð¹ Ð¾ÑÑ‚Ð°Ñ‚Ð¾Ðº',
      });
    }

    return InventoryItem(
      id: newProduct.id,
      name: newProduct.name,
      quantity: initialStock,
      unit: newProduct.unit,
      totalValue: initialStock * costPrice,
      category: newProduct.category,
      isLowStock: initialStock <= newProduct.minimumStock,
    );
  }

  Future<List<InventoryItem>> addProductsBulk(List<Map<String, dynamic>> products) async {
    final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;
    final List<Map<String, dynamic>> productInserts = [];
    final List<Map<String, dynamic>> movementInserts = [];
    final List<InventoryItem> items = [];
    
    for (var p in products) {
      final pid = const Uuid().v4();
      final initialStock = (p['initialStock'] ?? 0.0) as double;
      final costPrice = (p['costPrice'] ?? 0.0) as double;
      
      productInserts.add({
        'id': pid,
        'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
        'name': p['name'],
        'normalized_name': p['name'].toString().toLowerCase(),
        'category': p['category'],
        'unit': p['unit'],
        'minimum_stock': p['minimumStock'] ?? 0.0,
        'average_cost': costPrice,
        'is_active': true,
      });
      
      if (initialStock > 0) {
        movementInserts.add({
          'id': const Uuid().v4(),
          'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
          'product_id': pid,
          'quantity': initialStock,
          'unit': p['unit'],
          'type': 'initial_stock',
          'reason': 'ÐÐ°Ñ‡Ð°Ð»ÑŒÐ½Ñ‹Ð¹ Ð¾ÑÑ‚Ð°Ñ‚Ð¾Ðº',
        });
      }
      
      items.add(InventoryItem(
        id: pid,
        name: p['name'],
        quantity: initialStock,
        unit: p['unit'],
        totalValue: initialStock * costPrice,
        category: p['category'],
        isLowStock: initialStock <= (p['minimumStock'] ?? 0.0),
      ));
    }

    if (productInserts.isNotEmpty) {
      await _client.from('products').insert(productInserts);
    }
    
    if (movementInserts.isNotEmpty) {
      await _client.from('stock_movements').insert(movementInserts);
    }

    return items;
  }

  Future<void> updateProduct({
    required String id,
    required String name,
    required String category,
    required String unit,
  }) async {
    await _client.from('products').update({
      'name': name,
      'normalized_name': name.toLowerCase(),
      'category': category,
      'unit': unit,
      'updated_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    }).eq('id', id);
  }

  Future<void> deleteProduct(String id) async {
    await _client.from('products').update({
      'is_active': false,
      'updated_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    }).eq('id', id);
  }

  Future<void> clearNewStatus() async {
    final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;
    await _client.from('products').update({
      'is_new': false,
    }).eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId).eq('is_new', true);
  }

  Future<void> submitInventoryCount(List<InventoryItem> currentItems, Map<String, double> actualCounts) async {
    try {
      final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;
      
      // 1. Create inventory_counts record
      final countResponse = await _client.from('inventory_counts').insert({
        'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
        'status': 'completed',
        'completed_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      }).select().single();
      
      final countId = countResponse['id'];

      // 2. Create inventory_count_items AND stock_movements
      List<Map<String, dynamic>> countItems = [];
      List<Map<String, dynamic>> movements = [];

      for (var item in currentItems) {
        if (actualCounts.containsKey(item.id)) {
          final double expectedQty = item.quantity;
          final double actualQty = actualCounts[item.id]!;
          final double difference = actualQty - expectedQty;

          countItems.add({
            'inventory_count_id': countId,
            'product_id': item.id,
            'expected_qty': expectedQty,
            'actual_qty': actualQty,
            'difference': difference,
          });

          if (difference != 0) {
            movements.add({
              'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
              'product_id': item.id,
              'quantity': difference,
              'unit': item.unit,
              'type': 'inventory_adjustment',
              'reference_id': countId,
              'reason': 'Reviziya (Farq: $difference ${item.unit})',
            });
          }
        }
      }

      if (countItems.isNotEmpty) {
        await _client.from('inventory_count_items').insert(countItems);
      }
      
      if (movements.isNotEmpty) {
        await _client.from('stock_movements').insert(movements);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      throw Exception('Reviziyani saqlashda xatolik: $e');
    }
  }
}

class InventoryItem {

  final String name;

  final String unit;
  final double totalValue;
  final String category;
  final bool isLowStock;
  final bool isNew;

  InventoryItem({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.quantity,
    required this.unit,
    required this.totalValue,
    required this.category,
    this.isLowStock = false,
    this.isNew = false,
  });
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\inventory\presentation\pages\bulk_import_page.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:file_picker/file_picker.dart';
import 'package:excel/excel.dart' hide Border;
import 'dart:io';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../providers/inventory_provider.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

class BulkImportPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const BulkImportPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<BulkImportPage> createState() => _BulkImportPageState();
}

List<Map<String, dynamic>> _parseExcelInIsolate(String path) {
  final bytes = File(path).readAsBytesSync();
  final excel = Excel.decodeBytes(bytes);
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> parsedRows = [];

  if (excel.tables.isNotEmpty) {
    final sheetName = excel.tables.keys.first;
    final table = excel.tables[sheetName];
    
    if (table != null && table.rows.isNotEmpty) {
      int nameIdx = -1, catIdx = -1, qtyIdx = -1, unitIdx = -1;
      final headerRow = table.rows[0];
      int colCount = headerRow.length;
      
      // AI/Heuristic Approach: analyze the first 15 rows of data
      List<int> stringColumns = [];
      List<int> numericColumns = [];
      int maxRows = table.rows.length < 15 ? table.rows.length : 15;
      
      for (int c = 0; c < colCount; c++) {
        int strScore = 0;
        int numScore = 0;
        int unitScore = 0;
        
        for (int r = 1; r < maxRows; r++) {
          if (c >= table.rows[r].length) continue;
          final val = table.rows[r][c]?.value?.toString().trim() ?? '';
          if (val.isEmpty || val == 'null') continue;
          
          if (double.tryParse(val.replaceAll(',', '.')) != null) {
            numScore++;
          } else {
            strScore++;
            String vLower = val.toLowerCase();
            if (['ÐºÐ³', 'ÑˆÑ‚', 'Ð»', 'Ð³', 'Ð³Ñ€', 'Ð¿Ð¾Ñ€Ñ†', 'ÑƒÐ¿Ð°Ðº', 'kg', 'litr', 'dona'].contains(vLower)) {
              unitScore += 5;
            }
          }
        }
        
        if (unitScore >= 3 || (unitScore > 0 && unitScore >= strScore)) {
          unitIdx = c;
        } else if (strScore > numScore) {
          stringColumns.add(c);
        } else if (numScore > 0 && numScore >= strScore) {
          numericColumns.add(c);
        }
      }
      
      // Determine Category vs Name from String columns
      // Logic: Categories repeat (low unique count), Names don't (high unique count)
      if (stringColumns.isNotEmpty) {
        if (stringColumns.length == 1) {
          nameIdx = stringColumns[0];
        } else {
          int minUniques = 9999;
          int maxUniques = -1;
          for (int c in stringColumns) {
            Set<String> uniques = {};
            for (int r = 1; r < maxRows; r++) {
               if (c < table.rows[r].length) {
                 final val = table.rows[r][c]?.value?.toString().trim() ?? '';
                 if (val.isNotEmpty) uniques.add(val);
               }
            }
            if (uniques.length <= minUniques && uniques.isNotEmpty) {
               minUniques = uniques.length;
               catIdx = c;
            }
            if (uniques.length > maxUniques) {
               maxUniques = uniques.length;
               nameIdx = c;
            }
          }
          if (catIdx == nameIdx) {
             catIdx = stringColumns[0];
             nameIdx = stringColumns[1];
          }
        }
      }
      
      // Determine Quantity from Numeric columns
      // Logic: If a column has huge numbers (like > 1000), it's probably Price, not Quantity.
      if (numericColumns.isNotEmpty) {
        for (int c in numericColumns) {
           bool isHugeNumber = false;
           for (int r = 1; r < maxRows; r++) {
              if (c < table.rows[r].length) {
                 final valStr = table.rows[r][c]?.value?.toString().replaceAll(',', '.') ?? '0';
                 final val = double.tryParse(valStr) ?? 0.0;
                 if (val > 2000) { // If value is over 2000, it's definitely a price (e.g. 15000 UZS)
                    isHugeNumber = true;
                    break;
                 }
              }
           }
           if (!isHugeNumber) {
              qtyIdx = c;
              break;
           }
        }
      }
      
      // Fallbacks if heuristic fails
      if (nameIdx == -1) nameIdx = 0;
      if (catIdx == -1) catIdx = 1;
      if (qtyIdx == -1) qtyIdx = 2;
      if (unitIdx == -1) unitIdx = 3;

      for (int i = 1; i < table.rows.length; i++) {
        final row = table.rows[i];
        if (row.isEmpty) continue;
        
        final nameVal = nameIdx < row.length ? row[nameIdx]?.value?.toString() : null;
        if (nameVal == null || nameVal.trim().isEmpty || nameVal == 'null') continue;
        
        final rawQty = qtyIdx < row.length ? (row[qtyIdx]?.value?.toString() ?? '0') : '0';
        
        parsedRows.add({
          'name': nameVal.trim(),
          'category': catIdx < row.length ? (row[catIdx]?.value?.toString() ?? 'Kategoriyasiz') : 'Kategoriyasiz',
          'quantity': double.tryParse(rawQty.replaceAll(',', '.')) ?? 0.0,
          'unit': unitIdx < row.length ? (row[unitIdx]?.value?.toString() ?? 'dona') : 'dona',
        });
      }
    }
  }
  return parsedRows;
}

class _BulkImportPageState extends State<BulkImportPage> {
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _rows = [];
  bool _isEditing = false;
  bool _isLoading = false;

  Future<void> _pickAndParseExcel() async {
    try {
      final result = await FilePicker.pickFiles(
        type: FileType.custom,
        allowedExtensions: ['xlsx'],
      );

      if (result.isNotEmpty && result.first.path != null) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = true);

        // Move heavy parsing to a separate isolate
        final parsedRows = await compute(_parseExcelInIsolate, result.first.path!);

        setState(() {
          _rows = parsedRows;
          _isLoading = false;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('${'common.error'.tr()}: $e')),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  void _addNewRow() {
    setState(() {
      _rows.add({
        'name': '',
        'category': 'Kategoriyasiz',
        'quantity': 0.0,
        'unit': 'dona',
      });
    });
  }

  Future<void> _saveToDatabase() async {
    // Filter valid rows
    final validRows = _rows.where((r) => r['name'].toString().trim().isNotEmpty).toList();
    if (validRows.isEmpty) return;

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    try {
      final provider = context.read<InventoryProvider>();
      
      final productsToInsert = validRows.map((r) => {
        'name': r['name'],
        'category': r['category'],
        'unit': r['unit'],
        'minimumStock': 5.0,
        'initialStock': r['quantity'],
        'costPrice': 0.0,
      }).toList();
      
      await provider.addProductsBulk(productsToInsert);
      
      if (mounted) {
        Navigator.pop(context); // Go back after success
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('${'common.error'.tr()}: $e')),
        );
      }
    } finally {
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        title: Text('inventory.excel_import_title'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: Icon(_isEditing ? Icons.check : Icons.edit, color: AppColors.primary),
            onPressed: () {
              setState(() {
                _isEditing = !_isEditing;
              });
            },
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.upload_file, color: AppColors.success),
            tooltip: 'inventory.upload_excel'.tr(),
            onPressed: _pickAndParseExcel,
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: _rows.isEmpty || _isLoading ? null : _saveToDatabase,
            child: Text('common.save'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.primary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: _isLoading 
        ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()) 
        : Column(
            children: [
              if (_rows.isEmpty)
                Expanded(
                  child: Center(
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                      children: [
                        const Icon(Icons.table_view, size: 64, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        Text(
                          'inventory.table_empty_hint'.tr(),
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 16),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 24),
                        ElevatedButton.icon(
                          onPressed: _pickAndParseExcel,
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.file_upload),
                          label: Text('inventory.excel_upload'.tr()),
                          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                            foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                          ),
                        )
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                )
              else
                Expanded(
                  child: SingleChildScrollView(
                    scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                    child: SizedBox(
                      width: 800,
                      child: Column(
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            color: AppColors.surface,
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12, horizontal: 8),
                            child: Row(
                              children: [
                                Expanded(flex: 3, child: Text('inventory.col_name'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                                Expanded(flex: 2, child: Text('inventory.col_category'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                                Expanded(flex: 2, child: Text('inventory.col_quantity'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                                Expanded(flex: 2, child: Text('inventory.col_unit'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                                SizedBox(width: 50, child: Text('inventory.col_action'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          const Divider(height: 1, color: Colors.white10),
                          Expanded(
                            child: ListView.builder(
                              itemCount: _rows.length,
                              itemBuilder: (context, idx) {
                                final row = _rows[idx];
                                return Container(
                                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                                    border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: Colors.white10, width: 0.5)),
                                  ),
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4, horizontal: 8),
                                  child: Row(
                                    children: [
                                      Expanded(
                                        flex: 3, 
                                        child: _isEditing 
                                          ? TextFormField(
                                              initialValue: row['name'],
                                              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                                              decoration: const InputDecoration(isDense: true, contentPadding: EdgeInsets.all(8)),
                                              onChanged: (v) => row['name'] = v,
                                            )
                                          : Text(row['name'], style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                                      ),
                                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                      Expanded(
                                        flex: 2, 
                                        child: _isEditing 
                                          ? TextFormField(
                                              initialValue: row['category'],
                                              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                                              decoration: const InputDecoration(isDense: true, contentPadding: EdgeInsets.all(8)),
                                              onChanged: (v) => row['category'] = v,
                                            )
                                          : Text(row['category'], style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                                      ),
                                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                      Expanded(
                                        flex: 2, 
                                        child: _isEditing 
                                          ? TextFormField(
                                              initialValue: row['quantity'].toString(),
                                              keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                                              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                                              decoration: const InputDecoration(isDense: true, contentPadding: EdgeInsets.all(8)),
                                              onChanged: (v) => row['quantity'] = double.tryParse(v) ?? 0.0,
                                            )
                                          : Text(row['quantity'].toString(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                                      ),
                                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                      Expanded(
                                        flex: 2, 
                                        child: _isEditing 
                                          ? TextFormField(
                                              initialValue: row['unit'],
                                              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                                              decoration: const InputDecoration(isDense: true, contentPadding: EdgeInsets.all(8)),
                                              onChanged: (v) => row['unit'] = v,
                                            )
                                          : Text(row['unit'], style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                                      ),
                                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                      SizedBox(
                                        width: 50,
                                        child: IconButton(
                                          icon: const Icon(Icons.delete, color: AppColors.error),
                                          onPressed: () {
                                            setState(() {
                                              _rows.removeAt(idx);
                                            });
                                          },
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                );
                              },
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
      floatingActionButton: _isEditing ? FloatingActionButton(
        onPressed: _addNewRow,
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
        child: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
      ) : null,
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\inventory\presentation\pages\inventory_page.dart
