import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../providers/inventory_provider.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'bulk_import_page.dart';
import 'reviziya_page.dart';
import '../../../../core/utils/search_utils.dart';

class InventoryPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const InventoryPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<InventoryPage> createState() => _InventoryPageState();
}

class _InventoryPageState extends State<InventoryPage> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late TabController _tabController;
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();
  String? _highlightedItemId;

  int _selectedCategoryIndex = 0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabController = TabController(length: 2, vsync: this);
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      final provider = context.read<InventoryProvider>();
      provider.fetchInventory(background: provider.items.isNotEmpty);
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        elevation: 0,
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
        title: Text('inventory.title'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.search, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
            onPressed: () async {
              final items = context.read<InventoryProvider>().items;
              final selectedId = await showSearch<String?>(
                context: context, 
                delegate: InventorySearchDelegate(items)
              );
              
              if (!context.mounted) return;
              if (selectedId != null) {
                final inventoryProvider = context.read<InventoryProvider>();
                setState(() {
                  _selectedCategoryIndex = 0; // Switch to 'All' to ensure item is visible
                  _highlightedItemId = selectedId;
                });
                
                Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100), () {
                  if (!mounted) return;
                  final allItems = inventoryProvider.items;
                  final index = allItems.indexWhere((e) => e.id == selectedId);
                  if (index != -1 && _scrollController.hasClients) {
                    // Maximum scroll extent protection
                    final maxScroll = _scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent;
                    final targetOffset = index * 60.0;
                    _scrollController.animateTo(
                      targetOffset > maxScroll ? maxScroll : targetOffset,
                      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 400),
                      curve: Curves.easeInOut,
                    );
                  }
                });
                
                Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 3), () {
                  if (mounted) {
                    setState(() { _highlightedItemId = null; });
                  }
                });
              }
            },
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.checklist_rtl, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.push(
                context,
                MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const ReviziyaPage()),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          // Segmented Control
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
            child: Container(
              height: 40,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.surface,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              ),
              child: TabBar(
                controller: _tabController,
                indicator: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.blueAccent,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                indicatorSize: TabBarIndicatorSize.tab,
                labelColor: Colors.white,
                unselectedLabelColor: AppColors.textSecondary,
                dividerColor: Colors.transparent,
                tabs: [
                  Tab(text: 'inventory.tab_balances'.tr()),
                  Tab(text: 'inventory.tab_inventory'.tr()),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          
          Expanded(
            child: TabBarView(
              controller: _tabController,
              children: [
                _buildBalances(),
                _buildInventoryTab(),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(
        backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
        onPressed: _showAddOptionsModal,
        child: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showAddOptionsModal() {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        return SafeArea(
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.edit, color: Colors.blueAccent),
                title: Text('inventory.add_manual'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                onTap: () {
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                  _showAddItemModal();
                },
              ),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.upload_file, color: AppColors.success),
                title: Text('inventory.upload_excel'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                onTap: () {
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                  final inventoryProvider = context.read<InventoryProvider>();
                  Navigator.push(
                    context,
                    MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const BulkImportPage()),
                  ).then((_) {
                    if (mounted) inventoryProvider.fetchInventory();
                  });
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _showAddItemModal() {
    final nameController = TextEditingController();
    final quantityController = TextEditingController();
    final unitController = TextEditingController();
    final categoryController = TextEditingController();
    
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        return Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.only(
            bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom,
            left: 20,
            right: 20,
            top: 20,
          ),
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text('inventory.add_product'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                TextField(
                  controller: nameController,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'inventory.product_name'.tr(),
                    labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                    focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                TextField(
                  controller: categoryController,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'inventory.category'.tr(),
                    labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                    focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: TextField(
                        controller: quantityController,
                        keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(decimal: true),
                        style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          labelText: 'inventory.quantity'.tr(),
                          labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                          enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                          focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 16),
                    Expanded(
                      child: TextField(
                        controller: unitController,
                        style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          labelText: 'inventory.unit'.tr(),
                          labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                          enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                          focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                SizedBox(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  height: 50,
                  child: ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: () async {
                      if (nameController.text.isEmpty || categoryController.text.isEmpty || unitController.text.isEmpty) {
                        return;
                      }
                      
                      final qty = double.tryParse(quantityController.text) ?? 0.0;
                      
                      await context.read<InventoryProvider>().addProduct(
                        name: nameController.text,
                        category: categoryController.text,
                        unit: unitController.text,
                        minimumStock: 5.0, // Default minimum stock for now
                        initialStock: qty,
                      );
                      
                      if (context.mounted) {
                        Navigator.pop(context);
                      }
                    },
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                    ),
                    child: Text('common.save'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16)),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _showEditItemModal(dynamic item) {
    final nameController = TextEditingController(text: item.name);
    final unitController = TextEditingController(text: item.unit);
    final categoryController = TextEditingController(text: item.category);
    
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        return Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.only(
            bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom,
            left: 20,
            right: 20,
            top: 20,
          ),
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text('inventory.edit_product'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                TextField(
                  controller: nameController,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'inventory.product_name'.tr(),
                    labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                    focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                TextField(
                  controller: categoryController,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'inventory.category'.tr(),
                    labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                    focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                TextField(
                  controller: unitController,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'inventory.unit'.tr(),
                    labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                    focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                SizedBox(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  height: 50,
                  child: ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: () async {
                      if (nameController.text.isEmpty || categoryController.text.isEmpty || unitController.text.isEmpty) {
                        return;
                      }
                      
                      await context.read<InventoryProvider>().updateProduct(
                        id: item.id,
                        name: nameController.text,
                        category: categoryController.text,
                        unit: unitController.text,
                      );
                      
                      if (context.mounted) {
                        Navigator.pop(context);
                      }
                    },
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                    ),
                    child: Text('common.save'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16)),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _deleteItem(dynamic item) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        title: Text('common.delete'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
        content: Text("${item.name} ${'inventory.delete_confirm'.tr()}", style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: Text('common.cancel'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () async {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              await context.read<InventoryProvider>().deleteProduct(item.id);
            },
            child: Text('common.delete'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBalances() {
    return Consumer<InventoryProvider>(
      builder: (context, provider, child) {
        if (provider.isLoading) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        }

        if (provider.error != null) {
          return Center(child: Text(provider.error!, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)));
        }

        final itemsList = provider.items;
        
        final String allCategoryLabel = 'common.all'.tr();
        final Set<String> uniqueCategories = {allCategoryLabel};
        for (var item in itemsList) {
          uniqueCategories.add(item.category);
        }
        final categories = uniqueCategories.toList();

        // Ensure selected index is valid
        int selectedIndex = _selectedCategoryIndex;
        if (selectedIndex >= categories.length) {
          selectedIndex = 0;
        }

        final selectedCategory = categories[selectedIndex];

        final filteredItems = selectedCategory == allCategoryLabel 
            ? itemsList 
            : itemsList.where((item) => item.category == selectedCategory).toList();

        return Column(
          children: [
            // Categories chips
            SizedBox(
              height: 48,
              child: ListView.builder(
                scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                itemCount: categories.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  final isSelected = selectedIndex == index;
                  return Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4, vertical: 8),
                    child: InkWell(
                      onTap: () {
                        setState(() {
                          _selectedCategoryIndex = index;
                        });
                      },
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      child: Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                        alignment: Alignment.center,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: isSelected ? Colors.blueAccent : AppColors.surface,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                          border: isSelected ? null : Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          categories[index],
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: isSelected ? Colors.white : AppColors.textSecondary,
                            fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.w600 : FontWeight.normal,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
            
            // Items List
            Expanded(
              child: ListView.builder(
                controller: _scrollController,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
                itemCount: filteredItems.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  final item = filteredItems[index];
                  final isHighlighted = item.id == _highlightedItemId;
                  
                  return AnimatedContainer(
                    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
                    margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: isHighlighted ? Colors.grey.withAlpha(76) : AppColors.surface, // 76 is ~0.3 opacity
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      border: Border.all(color: isHighlighted ? Colors.blueAccent : Colors.white10),
                    ),
                    child: Stack(
                      children: [
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                          child: Row(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Expanded(
                                child: Column(
                                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                  children: [
                                    Text(
                                      item.name,
                                      style: const TextStyle(
                                        color: AppColors.textPrimary, 
                                        fontSize: 16, 
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                              Container(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: Colors.blueAccent.withAlpha(25), // 0.1 opacity
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                                ),
                                child: Text(
                                  item.category,
                                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.blueAccent, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 16),
                        Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                          children: [
                            Text(
                              '${item.quantity}',
                              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                            ),
                            Text(
                              item.unit,
                              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        SizedBox(
                          width: 30,
                          height: 30,
                          child: PopupMenuButton<String>(
                            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.more_vert, color: AppColors.textSecondary, size: 20),
                            color: AppColors.surface,
                            onSelected: (value) {
                              if (value == 'edit') {
                                _showEditItemModal(item);
                              } else if (value == 'delete') {
                                _deleteItem(item);
                              }
                            },
                            itemBuilder: (context) => [
                              PopupMenuItem(
                                value: 'edit',
                                child: Text('common.edit'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                              ),
                              PopupMenuItem(
                                value: 'delete',
                                child: Text('common.delete'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)),
                              ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (item.isNew)
                      Positioned(
                        top: 0,
                        left: 0,
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                          decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                            color: AppColors.success,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
                              topLeft: Radius.circular(16),
                              bottomRight: Radius.circular(8),
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: const Text(
                            'NEW',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: Colors.white,
                              fontSize: 10,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        );
    });
  }

  Widget _buildInventoryTab() {
    return Consumer<InventoryProvider>(
      builder: (context, provider, _) {
        final totalCount = provider.items.length;
        final lowStockCount = provider.items.where((i) => i.isLowStock).length;

        return Center(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.surface,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
              ),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                    child: const Icon(Icons.checklist_rtl, color: Colors.blueAccent, size: 40),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text(
                    'inventory.reviziya_title'.tr(),
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                      fontSize: 18,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    ),
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text(
                    'inventory.reviziya_desc'.tr(),
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                      fontSize: 13,
                    ),
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 20),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
                    children: [
                      Column(
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            '$totalCount',
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                              fontSize: 18,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 2),
                          Text(
                            'inventory.col_name'.tr(),
                            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 11),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      Container(height: 30, width: 1, color: Colors.white10),
                      Column(
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            '$lowStockCount',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: lowStockCount > 0 ? Colors.redAccent : Colors.green,
                              fontSize: 18,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 2),
                          Text(
                            'inventory.low_stock'.tr(),
                            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 11),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  SizedBox(
                    width: double.infinity,
                    height: 48,
                    child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.play_arrow, size: 20),
                      label: Text(
                        'inventory.tab_inventory'.tr(),
                        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                      ),
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                        backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                      ),
                      onPressed: () {
                        Navigator.push(
                          context,
                          MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const ReviziyaPage()),
                        );
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class InventorySearchDelegate extends SearchDelegate<String?> {
  final List<dynamic> items;
  static final List<String> _searchHistory = [];

  InventorySearchDelegate(this.items);

  @override
  String get searchFieldLabel => 'inventory.search_hint'.tr();

  @override
  ThemeData appBarTheme(BuildContext context) {
    final ThemeData theme = Theme.of(context);
    return theme.copyWith(
      appBarTheme: const AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: const InputDecorationTheme(
        hintStyle: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
        border: InputBorder.none,
      ),
      textTheme: const TextTheme(
        titleLarge: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Widget> buildActions(BuildContext context) {
    return [
      if (query.isNotEmpty)
        IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.clear, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
          onPressed: () {
            query = '';
          },
        ),
    ];
  }

  @override
  Widget buildLeading(BuildContext context) {
    return IconButton(
      icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
      onPressed: () {
        close(context, null);
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget buildResults(BuildContext context) {
    if (query.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      _searchHistory.remove(query.trim());
      _searchHistory.add(query.trim());
      if (_searchHistory.length > 10) {
        _searchHistory.removeAt(0); // keep only last 10
      }
    }
    return _buildList();
  }

  @override
  Widget buildSuggestions(BuildContext context) {
    if (query.isEmpty) {
      return Container(
        color: AppColors.background,
        child: _searchHistory.isEmpty
            ? Center(child: Text('common.search_history_empty'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)))
            : ListView.builder(
                itemCount: _searchHistory.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  final historyItem = _searchHistory.reversed.toList()[index];
                  return ListTile(
                    leading: const Icon(Icons.history, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    title: Text(historyItem, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                    onTap: () {
                      query = historyItem;
                    },
                  );
                },
              ),
      );
    }
    return _buildList();
  }

  Widget _buildList() {
    final normalizedQuery = SearchUtils.normalize(query);
    
    final filteredItems = items.where((item) {
      final normalizedName = SearchUtils.normalize(item.name);
      final normalizedCategory = SearchUtils.normalize(item.category);
      return normalizedName.contains(normalizedQuery) || normalizedCategory.contains(normalizedQuery);
    }).toList();

    return Container(
      color: AppColors.background,
      child: filteredItems.isEmpty
          ? Center(child: Text('common.not_found'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)))
          : ListView.builder(
              itemCount: filteredItems.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final item = filteredItems[index];
                return ListTile(
                  title: Text(item.name, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                  subtitle: Text(item.category, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                  trailing: Text('${item.quantity} ${item.unit}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  onTap: () {
                    close(context, item.id);
                  },
                );
              },
            ),
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\inventory\presentation\pages\reviziya_page.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../providers/inventory_provider.dart';
import '../providers/reviziya_provider.dart';

class ReviziyaPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const ReviziyaPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<ReviziyaPage> createState() => _ReviziyaPageState();
}

class _ReviziyaPageState extends State<ReviziyaPage> {
  final Map<String, TextEditingController> _controllers = {};

  @override
  void dispose() {
    for (var c in _controllers.values) {
      c.dispose();
    }
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final inventoryItems = context.watch<InventoryProvider>().items;
    final provider = context.watch<ReviziyaProvider>();

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text('inventory.reviziya_title'.tr()),
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            color: Colors.blueAccent.withAlpha(30),
            child: Text(
              'inventory.reviziya_desc'.tr(),
              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.blueAccent, fontSize: 13),
            ),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: ListView.separated(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              itemCount: inventoryItems.length,
              separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const Divider(color: AppColors.surfaceLight),
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final item = inventoryItems[index];
                
                if (!_controllers.containsKey(item.id)) {
                  _controllers[item.id] = TextEditingController();
                }

                final actualQty = provider.actualCounts[item.id];
                final difference = actualQty != null ? actualQty - item.quantity : 0.0;
                final diffColor = difference < 0 ? Colors.red : (difference > 0 ? Colors.green : AppColors.textSecondary);

                return Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      flex: 2,
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(item.name, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14)),
                          Text('${'inventory.in_system'.tr()} ${item.quantity.toStringAsFixed(1)} ${item.unit}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
                          if (actualQty != null)
                            Text(
                              '${'inventory.difference'.tr()} ${difference > 0 ? '+' : ''}${difference.toStringAsFixed(1)} ${item.unit}',
                              style: TextStyle(color: diffColor, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    Expanded(
                      flex: 1,
                      child: TextField(
                        controller: _controllers[item.id],
                        keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(decimal: true),
                        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          hintText: 'inventory.actual'.tr(),
                          suffixText: item.unit,
                          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
                          border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                          isDense: true,
                        ),
                        onChanged: (val) {
                          final parsed = double.tryParse(val.replaceAll(',', '.'));
                          if (parsed != null) {
                            context.read<ReviziyaProvider>().setActualCount(item.id, parsed);
                          } else if (val.isEmpty) {
                            // Can't remove easily without changing provider, but setting to expected is a hack.
                            // Let's just leave it as is, or use a method to remove.
                          }
                        },
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.surface,
              border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: Colors.grey.withAlpha(50))),
            ),
            child: SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              height: 50,
              child: ElevatedButton(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                ),
                onPressed: provider.actualCounts.isEmpty || provider.isLoading
                    ? null
                    : () async {
                        final success = await provider.submitReviziya();
                        if (!context.mounted) return;
                        if (success) {
                          Navigator.pop(context);
                          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                            SnackBar(content: Text('inventory.reviziya_success'.tr()), backgroundColor: Colors.green),
                          );
                        } else {
                          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                            SnackBar(content: Text(provider.error ?? 'common.error'.tr()), backgroundColor: Colors.red),
                          );
                        }
                      },
                child: provider.isLoading
                    ? const SizedBox(width: 24, height: 24, child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2))
                    : Text('inventory.complete_reviziya'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\inventory\presentation\providers\inventory_provider.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../../core/audit/audit_service.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/inventory_item.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/inventory_repository.dart';

class InventoryProvider extends ChangeNotifier {
  final InventoryRepository _repository = InventoryRepository();
  InventoryRepository get repository => _repository;
  
  List<InventoryItem> _items = [];
  List<InventoryItem> get items => _items;
  
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;
  
  String? _error;
  String? get error => _error;

  final bool _useMockData = false;

  Future<void> fetchInventory({bool background = false}) async {
    if (!background && _items.isEmpty) {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
      notifyListeners();
    }
    
    try {
      final data = await _repository.getProducts();
      if (data.isEmpty && _useMockData) {
        _items = _getMockInventory();
      } else {
        _items = data;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      if (_useMockData) {
         _items = _getMockInventory();
      }
    }
    
    if (!background) {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    } else {
      // Even if background, we notify to update the UI with new data
      notifyListeners();
    }
    
    // Clear NEW tags in the database so they disappear next time
    if (_items.any((item) => item.isNew)) {
      _repository.clearNewStatus().catchError((e) => debugPrint('Failed to clear new status: $e'));
    }
  }

  Future<void> addProduct({
    required String name,
    required String category,
    required String unit,
    required double minimumStock,
    double initialStock = 0,
    double costPrice = 0,
  }) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final newItem = await _repository.addProduct(
        name: name,
        category: category,
        unit: unit,
        minimumStock: minimumStock,
        initialStock: initialStock,
        costPrice: costPrice,
      );
      
      _items.add(newItem);
      AuditService().logAction(
        department: 'warehouse',
        action: 'Omborga mahsulot qo`shildi',
        details: '$name ($category) qo`shildi. Boshlang`ich qoldiq: $initialStock $unit.',
      );
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      rethrow;
    }
    
    _isLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> addProductsBulk(List<Map<String, dynamic>> products) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();
    
    try {
      final newItems = await _repository.addProductsBulk(products);
      _items.addAll(newItems);
      AuditService().logAction(
        department: 'warehouse',
        action: 'Ommaviy mahsulot kirimi',
        details: '${products.length} ta mahsulot omborga kiritildi.',
      );
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      rethrow;
    }
    
    _isLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> updateProduct({
    required String id,
    required String name,
    required String category,
    required String unit,
  }) async {
    try {
      await _repository.updateProduct(id: id, name: name, category: category, unit: unit);
      final index = _items.indexWhere((item) => item.id == id);
      if (index != -1) {
        final old = _items[index];
        _items[index] = InventoryItem(
          id: old.id,
          name: name,
          category: category,
          unit: unit,
          quantity: old.quantity,
          totalValue: old.totalValue,
          isLowStock: old.isLowStock,
        );
        notifyListeners();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      notifyListeners();
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<void> deleteProduct(String id) async {
    try {
      await _repository.deleteProduct(id);
      _items.removeWhere((item) => item.id == id);
      notifyListeners();
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      notifyListeners();
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  List<InventoryItem> _getMockInventory() {
    return [
      InventoryItem(
        id: '1',
        name: 'Mol go\'shti (Ribay)',
        category: 'Go\'sht',
        quantity: 12.5,
        unit: 'kg',
        totalValue: 1125000,
        isLowStock: false,
      ),
      InventoryItem(
        id: '2',
        name: 'Qaymoq 33%',
        category: 'Sut mahsulotlari',
        quantity: 5.0,
        unit: 'litr',
        totalValue: 225000,
        isLowStock: true,
      ),
      InventoryItem(
        id: '3',
        name: 'Cherri pomidor',
        category: 'Sabzavotlar',
        quantity: 3.2,
        unit: 'kg',
        totalValue: 112000,
        isLowStock: true,
      ),
      InventoryItem(
        id: '4',
        name: 'Bug\'doy uni',
        category: 'Quruq mahsulotlar',
        quantity: 50.0,
        unit: 'kg',
        totalValue: 400000,
        isLowStock: false,
      ),
      InventoryItem(
        id: '5',
        name: 'Zaytun moyi',
        category: 'Yog\'lar',
        quantity: 4.5,
        unit: 'litr',
        totalValue: 540000,
        isLowStock: true,
      ),
    ];
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\inventory\presentation\providers\reviziya_provider.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../providers/inventory_provider.dart';

class ReviziyaProvider with ChangeNotifier {
  final InventoryProvider _inventoryProvider;
  
  ReviziyaProvider(this._inventoryProvider);

  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;

  String? _error;
  String? get error => _error;

  final Map<String, double> _actualCounts = {};
  Map<String, double> get actualCounts => _actualCounts;

  void setActualCount(String productId, double count) {
    _actualCounts[productId] = count;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void clearCounts() {
    _actualCounts.clear();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<bool> submitReviziya() async {
    if (_actualCounts.isEmpty) return false;

    _isLoading = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final items = _inventoryProvider.items;
      await _inventoryProvider.repository.submitInventoryCount(items, _actualCounts);
      
      // Refresh inventory after successful reviziya
      await _inventoryProvider.fetchInventory();
      
      clearCounts();
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class MenuFoodcostPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const MenuFoodcostPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<MenuFoodcostPage> createState() => _MenuFoodcostPageState();
}

class _MenuFoodcostPageState extends State<MenuFoodcostPage> {
  String? _selectedCategory;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      context.read<RecipesProvider>().fetchRecipes();
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final recipesProvider = context.watch<RecipesProvider>();
    final allRecipes = recipesProvider.recipes;

    final Set<String> categorySet = {};
    for (var dish in allRecipes) {
      if (dish.category.trim().isNotEmpty) {
        categorySet.add(dish.category.trim());
      }
    }
    final sortedCategories = categorySet.toList()..sort();
    final categories = ['common.all'.tr(), ...sortedCategories];

    if (_selectedCategory == null || !categories.contains(_selectedCategory)) {
      _selectedCategory = categories.first;
    }

    final filteredDishes = _selectedCategory == 'common.all'.tr()
        ? allRecipes
        : allRecipes.where((d) {
            final cat = d.category.trim().isEmpty ? 'inventory.col_category'.tr() : d.category.trim();
            return cat == _selectedCategory;
          }).toList();

    final int totalDishesCount = allRecipes.length;
    final validDishesWithPrice = allRecipes.where((d) => d.sellingPrice > 0).toList();
    final double avgFoodcost = validDishesWithPrice.isEmpty
        ? 0.0
        : validDishesWithPrice.fold(0.0, (sum, d) => sum + d.foodcostPercentage) / validDishesWithPrice.length;

    final double expectedProfit = allRecipes.fold(0.0, (sum, d) {
      final profit = d.sellingPrice - d.costPrice;
      return sum + (profit > 0 ? profit : 0.0);
    });

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        elevation: 0,
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
        title: Text('menu.title'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
            onPressed: () {
              context.read<RecipesProvider>().fetchRecipes(background: true);
            },
          )
        ],
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          // Dropdown Main Menu
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.surface,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              ),
              child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
                child: DropdownButton<String>(
                  isExpanded: true,
                  dropdownColor: AppColors.surface,
                  value: categories.contains(_selectedCategory) ? _selectedCategory : categories.first,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.keyboard_arrow_down, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  items: categories.map((String value) {
                    return DropdownMenuItem<String>(
                      value: value,
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          const Icon(Icons.restaurant_menu, color: AppColors.textSecondary, size: 20),
                          const SizedBox(width: 8),
                          Text(value, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 14)),
                        ],
                      ),
                    );
                  }).toList(),
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) {
                      setState(() {
                        _selectedCategory = val;
                      });
                    }
                  },
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          
          // Stats row
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.surface,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    ),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text('menu.total_dishes'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 11)),
                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                        Text('$totalDishesCount', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.surface,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    ),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text('menu.avg_foodcost'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 11)),
                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                        Text(
                          '${avgFoodcost.toStringAsFixed(1)}%',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: avgFoodcost > 30 ? AppColors.error : AppColors.success,
                            fontSize: 16,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(
                  flex: 2,
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.surface,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    ),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text('menu.expected_profit'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 11)),
                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                        Text(
                          NumberFormat.currency(locale: 'uz', symbol: 'common.currency_sum'.tr(), decimalDigits: 0).format(expectedProfit),
                          style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),

          // List of dishes
          Expanded(
            child: recipesProvider.isLoading && allRecipes.isEmpty
                ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
                : filteredDishes.isEmpty
                    ? Center(
                        child: Text(
                          'common.no_data'.tr(),
                          style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        ),
                      )
                    : ListView.builder(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                        itemCount: filteredDishes.length,
                        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                          final dish = filteredDishes[index];
                          final isHighFoodcost = dish.foodcostPercentage > 30;
                          
                          return InkWell(
                            onTap: () {
                              Navigator.push(
                                context,
                                MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) => RecipeDetailPage(recipe: dish),
                                ),
                              ).then((_) {
                                if (context.mounted) {
                                  context.read<RecipesProvider>().fetchRecipes(background: true);
                                }
                              });
                            },
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                            child: Container(
                              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: AppColors.surface,
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                                border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                              ),
                              child: Row(
                                children: [
                                  ClipRRect(
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                                    child: (dish.imageUrl != null && dish.imageUrl!.isNotEmpty)
                                        ? Image.network(
                                            dish.imageUrl!,
                                            width: 50,
                                            height: 50,
                                            fit: BoxFit.cover,
                                            errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => _buildLetterAvatar(dish.name),
                                          )
                                        : _buildLetterAvatar(dish.name),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 16),
                                  Expanded(
                                    child: Column(
                                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                      children: [
                                        Text(
                                          dish.name,
                                          style: const TextStyle(
                                            color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                            fontSize: 15,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                        Row(
                                          children: [
                                            Text(
                                              NumberFormat.currency(locale: 'uz', symbol: 'common.currency_sum'.tr(), decimalDigits: 0).format(dish.sellingPrice),
                                              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13),
                                            ),
                                            const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                            Text(
                                              '(${dish.costPrice.toInt()} ${'common.currency_sum'.tr()})',
                                              style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary.withValues(alpha: 0.6), fontSize: 11),
                                            ),
                                          ],
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  Text(
                                    '${dish.foodcostPercentage.toStringAsFixed(1)}%',
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      color: isHighFoodcost ? AppColors.error : AppColors.success,
                                      fontSize: 14,
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
          ),
          
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: ElevatedButton.icon(
              onPressed: _showAddDishModal,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
              label: Text('menu.add_to_menu'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                minimumSize: const Size(double.infinity, 54),
                backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
              ),
            ),
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLetterAvatar(String name) {
    return Container(
      width: 50,
      height: 50,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surfaceLight,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      ),
      child: Center(
        child: Text(
          name.isNotEmpty ? name[0].toUpperCase() : '?',
          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showAddDishModal() {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        return const AddRecipeModal();
      },
    );
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\pos\data\repositories\pos_repository.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import '../../../../core/network/supabase_service.dart';

class PosRepository {
  final _client = Supabase.instance.client;

  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> fetchRecipes() async {
    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('recipes')
          .select('id, name, selling_price, is_active, is_favorite')
          .eq('restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId)
          .eq('is_active', true)
          .order('is_favorite', ascending: false)
          .order('name');
      return List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('POS fetch recipes error: $e');
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<void> checkout(List<Map<String, dynamic>> cartItems, double totalAmount) async {
    try {
      final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;
      
      final formattedItems = cartItems.map((item) => {
        'recipe_id': item['recipe_id'],
        'quantity': item['quantity'],
        'price': item['price'],
      }).toList();

      await _client.rpc('process_pos_checkout', params: {
        'p_restaurant_id': restaurantId,
        'p_total_amount': totalAmount,
        'p_items': formattedItems,
      });
    } catch (e) {
      throw Exception('To`lovni amalga oshirishda xatolik: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<bool> refundSale(String saleId, {String reason = 'Mijoz qaytardi'}) async {
    try {
      final response = await _client.rpc('process_pos_refund', params: {
        'p_sale_id': saleId,
        'p_reason': reason,
      });
      return response == true;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error refunding POS sale: $e');
      throw Exception('Chekni bekor qilishda xatolik: $e');
    }
  }
}

// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\pos\presentation\providers\pos_provider.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../../../../core/audit/audit_service.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/pos_repository.dart';

class PosProvider with ChangeNotifier {
  final PosRepository _repository = PosRepository();

  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;

  String? _error;
  String? get error => _error;

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _recipes = [];
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> get recipes => _recipes;

  // Cart: recipe_id -> Map of details
  final Map<String, Map<String, dynamic>> _cart = {};
  Map<String, Map<String, dynamic>> get cart => _cart;

  Future<void> fetchRecipes({bool background = false}) async {
    if (!background && _recipes.isEmpty) {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
      notifyListeners();
    }

    try {
      _recipes = await _repository.fetchRecipes();
      _error = null;
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
    }

    if (_isLoading) {
      _isLoading = false;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void addToCart(Map<String, dynamic> recipe) {
    final id = recipe['id'];
    if (_cart.containsKey(id)) {
      _cart[id]!['quantity'] += 1;
    } else {
      _cart[id] = {
        'recipe_id': id,
        'name': recipe['name'],
        'price': (recipe['selling_price'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
        'quantity': 1,
      };
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void removeFromCart(String id) {
    if (_cart.containsKey(id)) {
      if (_cart[id]!['quantity'] > 1) {
        _cart[id]!['quantity'] -= 1;
      } else {
        _cart.remove(id);
      }
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  void clearCart() {
    _cart.clear();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  double get totalAmount {
    double total = 0;
    for (var item in _cart.values) {
      total += item['quantity'] * item['price'];
    }
    return total;
  }

  Future<bool> checkout() async {
    if (_cart.isEmpty) return false;

    _isLoading = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final items = _cart.values.toList();
      final currentTotal = totalAmount;
      await _repository.checkout(items, currentTotal);

      final summary = items.map((i) => '${i['name']} x${i['quantity']}').join(', ');
      AuditService().logAction(
        department: 'pos',
        action: 'POS Savdo cheki',
        details: 'Chek yopildi. Jami: ${currentTotal.toStringAsFixed(0)} so`m ($summary).',
      );

      clearCart();
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\production\data\repositories\production_repository.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import '../../../../core/network/supabase_service.dart';
import '../../../../core/utils/unit_converter.dart';

class ProductionRepository {
  final _client = Supabase.instance.client;

  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> fetchRecipes() async {
    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('recipes')
          .select('id, name, output_unit, is_favorite, recipe_items(product_id, quantity, unit)')
          .eq('restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId)
          .eq('is_active', true)
          .order('is_favorite', ascending: false)
          .order('name');
      return List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Fetch recipes error: $e');
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<void> toggleFavorite(String recipeId, bool isFavorite) async {
    try {
      await _client
          .from('recipes')
          .update({'is_favorite': isFavorite})
          .eq('id', recipeId);
    } catch (e) {
      throw Exception('Yulduzchani o`zgartirishda xatolik: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> produceRecipe(String recipeId, String recipeName, double quantity, List<dynamic> items) async {
    try {
      final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;
      
      final eventResponse = await _client.from('business_events').insert({
        'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
        'event_type': 'production',
        'payload': {
          'recipe_id': recipeId,
          'recipe_name': recipeName,
          'quantity': quantity,
        },
      }).select().single();

      final eventId = eventResponse['id'];

      final productIds = items.map((i) => i['product_id'] as String).toList();
      final Map<String, String> baseUnits = {};
      
      if (productIds.isNotEmpty) {
        final productsResponse = await _client
            .from('products')
            .select('id, unit')
            .inFilter('id', productIds);
            
        for (var p in (productsResponse as List)) {
          baseUnits[p['id']] = p['unit']?.toString() ?? '';
        }
      }

      List<Map<String, dynamic>> movements = [];
      for (var item in items) {
        final double itemQty = (item['quantity'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        final String recipeUnit = item['unit'] ?? '';
        final String baseUnit = baseUnits[item['product_id']] ?? recipeUnit;
        final String finalUnit = baseUnit.isEmpty ? recipeUnit : baseUnit;
        
        final double convertedQty = UnitConverter.convert(itemQty, recipeUnit, finalUnit);

        movements.add({
          'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
          'product_id': item['product_id'],
          'quantity': -(convertedQty * quantity),
          'unit': finalUnit,
          'type': 'production_usage',
          'reference_id': eventId,
          'reason': 'Tayyorlash: $recipeName x $quantity',
        });
      }

      if (movements.isNotEmpty) {
        await _client.from('stock_movements').insert(movements);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      throw Exception('Tayyorlashda xatolik: $e');
    }
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\production\data\services\production_ai_service.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:flutter_dotenv/flutter_dotenv.dart';

class ProductionAiService {
  final String _apiKey = dotenv.env['DEEPSEEK_API_KEY'] ?? '';
  final String _model = dotenv.env['DEEPSEEK_MODEL'] ?? 'deepseek-chat';
  final String _baseUrl = 'https://api.deepseek.com/chat/completions';

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>?> parseProductionText(String text, List<String> availableRecipes) async {
    try {
      if (_apiKey.isEmpty) return null;

      final prompt = """
Foydalanuvchi restoranda ovqat tayyorlaganini aytdi. Uni tahlil qil.
Faqat quyidagi retseptlar ro'yxatidan eng mosini top: ${availableRecipes.join(', ')}
Natijani faqat JSON formatida qaytar. Boshqa so'z qo'shma.
Format: {"recipe_name": "...", "quantity": 123}
Agar tushunarsiz bo'lsa: {"error": "Tushunarsiz buyruq"}
Foydalanuvchi matni: $text
""";

      final response = await http.post(
        Uri.parse(_baseUrl),
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'Authorization': 'Bearer $_apiKey',
        },
        body: jsonEncode({
          'model': _model,
          'messages': [
            {'role': 'system', 'content': 'You are a JSON parser. Output only JSON.'},
            {'role': 'user', 'content': prompt}
          ],
          'temperature': 0.1,
        }),
      );

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
        String content = data['choices'][0]['message']['content'];
        content = content.replaceAll('```json', '').replaceAll('```', '').trim();
        return jsonDecode(content);
      }
      return null;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('AI parsing error: $e');
      return null;
    }
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\production\presentation\pages\production_page.dart
