import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:toastification/toastification.dart';
import '../providers/production_provider.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';

class ProductionPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const ProductionPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<ProductionPage> createState() => _ProductionPageState();
}

class _ProductionPageState extends State<ProductionPage> {
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();
  String? _highlightedRecipeId;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      context.read<ProductionProvider>().fetchRecipes();
    });
  }

  void _scrollToItem(String recipeId) {
    setState(() {
      _highlightedRecipeId = recipeId;
    });

    final provider = context.read<ProductionProvider>();
    final index = provider.filteredRecipes.indexWhere((r) => r['id'] == recipeId);
    if (index != -1) {
      double position = index * 100.0;
      _scrollController.animateTo(
        position,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
        curve: Curves.easeInOut,
      );

      Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2), () {
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() {
            _highlightedRecipeId = null;
          });
        }
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scrollController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final provider = context.watch<ProductionProvider>();

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        title: Text('production.title'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        iconTheme: const IconThemeData(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.search),
            onPressed: () async {
              final selectedId = await showSearch(
                context: context,
                delegate: ProductionSearchDelegate(provider.filteredRecipes),
              );
              if (selectedId != null) {
                _scrollToItem(selectedId);
              }
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [

          Expanded(
            child: provider.isLoading && provider.filteredRecipes.isEmpty
                ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
                : ListView.builder(
                    controller: _scrollController,
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 12, right: 12, top: 12, bottom: 100),
                    itemCount: provider.filteredRecipes.length,
                    itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                      final recipe = provider.filteredRecipes[index];
                      final recipeId = recipe['id'] as String;
                      final quantity = provider.getQuantity(recipeId);

                      final isHighlighted = _highlightedRecipeId == recipeId;
                      return Card(
                        color: isHighlighted ? AppColors.surface.withAlpha(150) : AppColors.surface,
                        shape: isHighlighted 
                            ? RoundedRectangleBorder(
                                side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent, width: 2),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                              )
                            : null,
                        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12.0),
                          child: Row(
                            crossAxisAlignment: quantity > 0 ? CrossAxisAlignment.center : CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Expanded(
                                child: Column(
                                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                  children: [
                                    Text(
                                      recipe['name'], 
                                      style: const TextStyle(
                                        color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                                        fontSize: 16,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                    Text(
                                      '${'production.output'.tr()}: ${recipe["output_unit"] ?? ""}', 
                                      style: const TextStyle(
                                        color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                                        fontSize: 12,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                              if (quantity == 0)
                                Column(
                                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                                  children: [
                                    IconButton(
                                      icon: Icon(
                                        recipe['is_favorite'] == true ? Icons.star : Icons.star_border,
                                        color: recipe['is_favorite'] == true ? Colors.amber : AppColors.textSecondary,
                                      ),
                                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                                      constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
                                      onPressed: () => context.read<ProductionProvider>().toggleFavorite(recipeId),
                                    ),
                                    IconButton(
                                      icon: const Icon(Icons.add_circle, color: AppColors.primary),
                                      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                                      constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
                                      onPressed: () => context.read<ProductionProvider>().increment(recipeId),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                )
                              else
                                Row(
                                  children: [
                                    IconButton(
                                      icon: const Icon(Icons.remove_circle_outline, color: Colors.red),
                                      onPressed: () => context.read<ProductionProvider>().decrement(recipeId),
                                    ),
                                    Text(
                                      quantity.toInt().toString(),
                                      style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                                    ),
                                    IconButton(
                                      icon: const Icon(Icons.add_circle, color: AppColors.primary),
                                      onPressed: () => context.read<ProductionProvider>().increment(recipeId),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
          ),
          // Removed AI Input Container
        ],
      ),
      floatingActionButton: provider.totalCartItems > 0
          ? FloatingActionButton.extended(
              onPressed: provider.isLoading ? null : () async {
                try {
                  final msg = await context.read<ProductionProvider>().submitCart();
                  if (msg.isNotEmpty && context.mounted) {
                    toastification.show(
                      context: context,
                      type: ToastificationType.success,
                      style: ToastificationStyle.flat,
                      title: Text('common.success'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      description: Text(msg),
                      alignment: Alignment.topRight,
                      autoCloseDuration: const Duration(seconds: 4),
                      showProgressBar: true,
                    );
                  }
                } catch (e) {
                  if (context.mounted) {
                    toastification.show(
                      context: context,
                      type: ToastificationType.error,
                      style: ToastificationStyle.flat,
                      title: Text('common.error'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      description: Text(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', '')),
                      alignment: Alignment.topRight,
                      autoCloseDuration: const Duration(seconds: 4),
                      showProgressBar: true,
                    );
                  }
                }
              },
              backgroundColor: provider.isLoading ? Colors.grey : AppColors.primary,
              icon: provider.isLoading 
                ? const SizedBox(width: 16, height: 16, child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2))
                : const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.white),
              label: Text(
                '${provider.totalCartItems} ${'production.produce_btn'.tr()}',
                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
              ),
            )
          : null,
      floatingActionButtonLocation: FloatingActionButtonLocation.centerFloat,
    );
  }
}


class ProductionSearchDelegate extends SearchDelegate<String?> {
  final List<Map<String, dynamic>> recipes;
  final List<String> _searchHistory = [];

  ProductionSearchDelegate(this.recipes) : super(searchFieldLabel: 'common.search'.tr());

  @override
  ThemeData appBarTheme(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return theme.copyWith(
      appBarTheme: const AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: const InputDecorationTheme(
        hintStyle: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
        border: InputBorder.none,
      ),
      textTheme: const TextTheme(
        titleLarge: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Widget> buildActions(BuildContext context) {
    return [
      if (query.isNotEmpty)
        IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.clear, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
          onPressed: () {
            query = '';
            showSuggestions(context);
          },
        ),
    ];
  }

  @override
  Widget buildLeading(BuildContext context) {
    return IconButton(
      icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
      onPressed: () {
        close(context, null);
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget buildResults(BuildContext context) {
    if (query.isNotEmpty && !_searchHistory.contains(query)) {
      _searchHistory.insert(0, query);
      if (_searchHistory.length > 10) {
        _searchHistory.removeLast();
      }
    }
    return _buildList();
  }

  @override
  Widget buildSuggestions(BuildContext context) {
    if (query.isEmpty) {
      return Container(
        color: AppColors.background,
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            if (_searchHistory.isNotEmpty)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Text('common.search_history'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    TextButton(
                      onPressed: () {
                        _searchHistory.clear();
                        query = ''; // Triggers rebuild
                      },
                      child: Text('common.clear'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.blueAccent)),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            Expanded(
              child: ListView.builder(
                itemCount: _searchHistory.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  return ListTile(
                    leading: const Icon(Icons.history, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    title: Text(_searchHistory[index], style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                    onTap: () {
                      query = _searchHistory[index];
                      showResults(context);
                    },
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }
    return _buildList();
  }

  Widget _buildList() {
    final normalizedQuery = query.toLowerCase();
    
    final filteredRecipes = recipes.where((recipe) {
      final normalizedName = (recipe['name'] as String? ?? '').toLowerCase();
      return normalizedName.contains(normalizedQuery);
    }).toList();

    return Container(
      color: AppColors.background,
      child: filteredRecipes.isEmpty
          ? Center(child: Text('common.not_found'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)))
          : ListView.builder(
              itemCount: filteredRecipes.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final recipe = filteredRecipes[index];
                return ListTile(
                  title: Text(recipe['name'] ?? '', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                  trailing: const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios, color: AppColors.textSecondary, size: 16),
                  onTap: () {
                    close(context, recipe['id'] as String?);
                  },
                );
              },
            ),
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\production\presentation\providers\production_provider.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../../core/audit/audit_service.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/production_repository.dart';

class ProductionProvider extends ChangeNotifier {
  final ProductionRepository _repository = ProductionRepository();

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _recipes = [];
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> get recipes => _recipes;

  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;

  String _searchQuery = '';
  String get searchQuery => _searchQuery;

  void setSearchQuery(String query) {
    _searchQuery = query;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> get filteredRecipes {
    if (_searchQuery.trim().isEmpty) return _recipes;
    return _recipes.where((r) => 
      r['name'].toString().toLowerCase().contains(_searchQuery.toLowerCase())
    ).toList();
  }



  // Shopping Cart logic (Recipe ID -> Quantity)
  final Map<String, double> _cart = {};
  Map<String, double> get cart => _cart;

  double getQuantity(String recipeId) => _cart[recipeId] ?? 0.0;

  void increment(String recipeId) {
    _cart[recipeId] = getQuantity(recipeId) + 1;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void decrement(String recipeId) {
    if (getQuantity(recipeId) > 0) {
      _cart[recipeId] = getQuantity(recipeId) - 1;
      if (_cart[recipeId] == 0) {
        _cart.remove(recipeId);
      }
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  int get totalCartItems => _cart.values.fold(0, (sum, item) => sum + item.toInt());

  void clearCart() {
    _cart.clear();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> fetchRecipes({bool background = false}) async {
    if (!background && _recipes.isEmpty) {
      _isLoading = true;
      notifyListeners();
    }
    _recipes = await _repository.fetchRecipes();
    _sortRecipes();
    if (_isLoading) {
      _isLoading = false;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void _sortRecipes() {
    _recipes.sort((a, b) {
      final aFav = a['is_favorite'] == true ? 1 : 0;
      final bFav = b['is_favorite'] == true ? 1 : 0;
      if (aFav != bFav) {
        return bFav.compareTo(aFav); // 1 comes before 0
      }
      return a['name'].toString().compareTo(b['name'].toString());
    });
  }

  Future<void> toggleFavorite(String recipeId) async {
    final index = _recipes.indexWhere((r) => r['id'] == recipeId);
    if (index == -1) return;
    
    final currentStatus = _recipes[index]['is_favorite'] == true;
    
    // Optimistic UI update
    _recipes[index]['is_favorite'] = !currentStatus;
    _sortRecipes();
    notifyListeners();
    
    try {
      await _repository.toggleFavorite(recipeId, !currentStatus);
    } catch (e) {
      // Revert if error
      _recipes[index]['is_favorite'] = currentStatus;
      _sortRecipes();
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<String> submitCart() async {
    if (_cart.isEmpty) return "";
    
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    
    try {
      final cartEntries = _cart.entries.toList();
      final total = totalCartItems;
      
      for (var entry in cartEntries) {
        final recipeId = entry.key;
        final qty = entry.value;
        final recipe = _recipes.firstWhere((r) => r['id'] == recipeId);
        final items = recipe['recipe_items'] as List<dynamic>? ?? [];
        await _repository.produceRecipe(recipeId, recipe['name'], qty, items);
      }
      AuditService().logAction(
        department: 'kitchen',
        action: 'Taom tayyorlandi',
        details: 'Jami $total ta porsiya tayyorlandi va ombordan chegirildi.',
      );
      _cart.clear();
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      return "Muvaffaqiyatli: $total ta porsiya tayyorlandi va ombordan chegirildi.";
    } catch (e) {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      throw Exception(e.toString());
    }
  }

}

class SupplierModel {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String contactPerson;
  final String phone;

  SupplierModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.contactPerson,
    required this.phone,
  });

  factory SupplierModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return SupplierModel(
      id: json['id'],
      name: json['name'],
      contactPerson: json['contact_person'] ?? '',
      phone: json['phone'] ?? '',
    );
  }
}

class PurchaseItemModel {
  final String id;
  final String purchaseId;
  final String productId;
  final double quantity;
  final double price;
  final String unit;
  
  final String productName;

  PurchaseItemModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.purchaseId,
    required this.productId,
    required this.quantity,
    required this.price,
    required this.unit,
    this.productName = '',
  });

  factory PurchaseItemModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    String pName = '';
    if (json['products'] != null) {
      pName = json['products']['name'] ?? '';
    }
    return PurchaseItemModel(
      id: json['id'],
      purchaseId: json['purchase_id'],
      productId: json['product_id'],
      quantity: (json['quantity'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
      price: (json['price'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
      unit: json['unit'] ?? 'ÑˆÑ‚',
      productName: pName,
    );
  }
}

class PurchaseModel {
  final String id;
  final String restaurantId;
  final String? supplierId;
  final String invoiceNumber;
  final String status;
  final double totalAmount;
  final DateTime date;
  
  final SupplierModel? supplier;
  final List<PurchaseItemModel> items;

  PurchaseModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.restaurantId,
    this.supplierId,
    required this.invoiceNumber,
    required this.status,
    required this.totalAmount,
    required this.date,
    this.supplier,
    this.items = const [],
  });

  factory PurchaseModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    SupplierModel? sModel;
    if (json['suppliers'] != null) {
      sModel = SupplierModel.fromJson(json['suppliers']);
    }

    var itemsList = <PurchaseItemModel>[];
    if (json['purchase_items'] != null) {
      itemsList = (json['purchase_items'] as List)
          .map((i) => PurchaseItemModel.fromJson(i))
          .toList();
    }

    return PurchaseModel(
      id: json['id'],
      restaurantId: json['restaurant_id'],
      supplierId: json['supplier_id'],
      invoiceNumber: json['invoice_number'] ?? '',
      status: json['status'] ?? 'pending',
      totalAmount: (json['total_amount'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
      date: DateTime.parse(json['date']),
      supplier: sModel,
      items: itemsList,
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import '../../../../core/network/supabase_service.dart';
import '../../../../core/utils/unit_converter.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/order.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';
import '../models/purchase_model.dart';
class PurchasesRepository {
  final SupabaseClient _client = SupabaseService.client;
  
  Future<List<PurchaseOrder>> getPurchases() async {
    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('purchases')
          .select('''
            *,
            suppliers (*),
            purchase_items (
              *,
              products (name)
            )
          ''')
          .eq('restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId)
          .order('date', ascending: false);
          
      final List<PurchaseModel> models = (response as List)
          .map((json) => PurchaseModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
          
      return models.map((e) {
        return PurchaseOrder(
          id: e.id,
          supplierName: e.supplier?.name ?? 'ÐÐµÐ¸Ð·Ð²ÐµÑÑ‚Ð½Ñ‹Ð¹ Ð¿Ð¾ÑÑ‚Ð°Ð²Ñ‰Ð¸Ðº',
          date: e.date.toString().split(' ')[0], // simple formatting
          totalAmount: e.totalAmount,
          status: e.status == 'completed' ? OrderStatus.delivered : OrderStatus.active,
          itemsCount: e.items.length,
        );
      }).toList();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error fetching purchases: $e');
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getSuppliers() async {
    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('suppliers')
          .select('id, name, notes, phone')
          .eq('restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId);
      // Ensure notes is renamed to contact_name in the result if needed
      return List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response).map((e) {
        return {
          'id': e['id'],
          'name': e['name'],
          'contact_name': e['notes'] ?? '',
          'phone': e['phone'] ?? '',
        };
      }).toList();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error fetching suppliers: $e');
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<void> addSupplier({required String name, required String contactName, required String phone}) async {
    try {
      await _client.from('suppliers').insert({
        'id': const Uuid().v4(),
        'restaurant_id': SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId,
        'name': name,
        'phone': phone,
        'notes': contactName,
      });
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error adding supplier: $e');
      rethrow;
    }
  }
  
  Future<void> createRequest(List<Map<String, dynamic>> items, String supplierId) async {
    try {
      final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;
      final purchaseId = const Uuid().v4();
      
      // Calculate total amount (can be 0 for requests if prices are unknown)
      double totalAmount = 0;
      for (var item in items) {
        totalAmount += (item['quantity'] ?? 0) * (item['price'] ?? 0);
      }
      
      // Fetch products to match or create new ones
      final existingProducts = await _client
          .from('products')
          .select('id, name')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId);
          
      // Insert into purchases
      await _client.from('purchases').insert({
        'id': purchaseId,
        'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
        'supplier_id': supplierId,
        'date': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
        'total_amount': totalAmount,
        'status': 'active', // Active status for unfulfilled requests
      });
      
      // Process items
      final List<Map<String, dynamic>> purchaseItemsToInsert = [];
      for (var item in items) {
        final String itemName = item['name'].toString().trim();
        final double itemQty = (item['quantity'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        final double itemPrice = (item['price'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        final String itemUnit = item['unit'] ?? 'kg';
        
        // Find existing product or create
        var match = existingProducts.firstWhere(
            (p) => p['name'].toString().toLowerCase() == itemName.toLowerCase(),
            orElse: () => {});
            
        String productId;
        if (match.isNotEmpty) {
          productId = match['id'];
        } else {
          productId = const Uuid().v4();
          await _client.from('products').insert({
            'id': productId,
            'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
            'name': itemName,
            'normalized_name': itemName.toLowerCase(),
            'category': 'Ð Ð°Ð·Ð½Ð¾Ðµ',
            'unit': itemUnit,
            'minimum_stock': 0,
            'is_active': true,
            'is_new': true,
          });
          existingProducts.add({'id': productId, 'name': itemName, 'unit': itemUnit});
        }
        
        purchaseItemsToInsert.add({
          'id': const Uuid().v4(),
          'purchase_id': purchaseId,
          'product_id': productId,
          'quantity': itemQty,
          'unit': itemUnit,
          'unit_price': itemPrice,
          'total_price': itemQty * itemPrice,
        });
      }

      if (purchaseItemsToInsert.isNotEmpty) {
        await _client.from('purchase_items').insert(purchaseItemsToInsert);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error creating request: $e');
      rethrow;
    }
  }
  
  Future<void> updateRequest(String orderId, List<Map<String, dynamic>> items, String supplierId) async {
    try {
      final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;
      double totalAmount = 0;
      for (var item in items) {
        totalAmount += (item['quantity'] ?? 0) * (item['price'] ?? 0);
      }
      
      await _client.from('purchases').update({
        'supplier_id': supplierId,
        'total_amount': totalAmount,
      }).eq('id', orderId);
      
      await _client.from('purchase_items').delete().eq('purchase_id', orderId);
      
      final existingProducts = await _client.from('products').select('id, name').eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId);
      
      final List<Map<String, dynamic>> purchaseItemsToInsert = [];
      for (var item in items) {
        final String itemName = item['name'].toString().trim();
        final double itemQty = (item['quantity'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        final double itemPrice = (item['price'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        final String itemUnit = item['unit'] ?? 'kg';
        
        var match = existingProducts.firstWhere(
            (p) => p['name'].toString().toLowerCase() == itemName.toLowerCase(),
            orElse: () => {});
            
        String productId;
        if (match.isNotEmpty) {
          productId = match['id'];
        } else {
          productId = const Uuid().v4();
          await _client.from('products').insert({
            'id': productId,
            'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
            'name': itemName,
            'normalized_name': itemName.toLowerCase(),
            'category': 'Ð Ð°Ð·Ð½Ð¾Ðµ',
            'unit': itemUnit,
            'minimum_stock': 0,
            'is_active': true,
            'is_new': true,
          });
          existingProducts.add({'id': productId, 'name': itemName, 'unit': itemUnit});
        }
        
        purchaseItemsToInsert.add({
          'id': const Uuid().v4(),
          'purchase_id': orderId,
          'product_id': productId,
          'quantity': itemQty,
          'unit': itemUnit,
          'unit_price': itemPrice,
          'total_price': itemQty * itemPrice,
        });
      }

      if (purchaseItemsToInsert.isNotEmpty) {
        await _client.from('purchase_items').insert(purchaseItemsToInsert);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error updating request: $e');
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<void> deleteRequest(String orderId) async {
    try {
      await _client.from('purchase_items').delete().eq('purchase_id', orderId);
      await _client.from('purchases').delete().eq('id', orderId);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error deleting request: $e');
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<void> saveAIPurchase(List<Map<String, dynamic>> items, {File? imageFile, String? existingPurchaseId}) async {
    try {
      final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;


      double totalAmount = 0;
      for (var item in items) {
        totalAmount += (item['quantity'] ?? 0) * (item['price'] ?? 0);
      }

      // 1. Fetch all products with stock to match and update average cost
      final existingProducts = await _client
          .from('products_with_stock')
          .select('id, name, unit, current_stock, average_cost')
          .eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId);

      // 2. Create or Update the Purchase record
      final purchaseId = existingPurchaseId ?? const Uuid().v4();
      if (existingPurchaseId != null) {
        await _client.from('purchases').update({
          'status': 'completed',
          'total_amount': totalAmount,
        }).eq('id', existingPurchaseId);
        
        await _client.from('purchase_items').delete().eq('purchase_id', existingPurchaseId);
      } else {
        await _client.from('purchases').insert({
          'id': purchaseId,
          'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
          'date': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
          'total_amount': totalAmount,
          'status': 'completed',
        });
      }
      
      // 3. Process each item
      final List<Map<String, dynamic>> stockMovementsToInsert = [];
      final List<Map<String, dynamic>> purchaseItemsToInsert = [];

      for (var item in items) {
        final String aiName = item['name'].toString().trim().toLowerCase();
        final double aiQuantity = (item['quantity'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        final double aiPrice = (item['price'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        final String aiUnit = item['unit'] ?? 'ÑˆÑ‚';

        // Find match
        var match = existingProducts.firstWhere(
            (p) => p['name'].toString().toLowerCase().contains(aiName) || aiName.contains(p['name'].toString().toLowerCase()),
            orElse: () => {});

        String productId;
        String baseUnit;
        
        if (match.isNotEmpty) {
          productId = match['id'];
          baseUnit = match['unit']?.toString() ?? aiUnit;
        } else {
          baseUnit = aiUnit;
          productId = const Uuid().v4();
        }

        final double convertedQty = UnitConverter.convert(aiQuantity, aiUnit, baseUnit);
        final double incomingUnitCost = convertedQty > 0 ? (aiQuantity * aiPrice) / convertedQty : aiPrice;

        if (match.isNotEmpty) {
          final double currentStock = (match['current_stock'] ?? 0).toDouble();
          final double currentAvgCost = (match['average_cost'] ?? 0).toDouble();
          final double newAvgCost = (currentStock <= 0 || currentAvgCost <= 0)
              ? incomingUnitCost
              : ((currentStock * currentAvgCost) + (convertedQty * incomingUnitCost)) / (currentStock + convertedQty);

          await _client.from('products').update({
            'average_cost': newAvgCost,
            'updated_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
          }).eq('id', productId);
        } else {
          await _client.from('products').insert({
            'id': productId,
            'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
            'name': item['name'],
            'normalized_name': item['name'].toString().toLowerCase(),
            'category': 'Ð Ð°Ð·Ð½Ð¾Ðµ',
            'unit': aiUnit,
            'minimum_stock': 0,
            'average_cost': incomingUnitCost,
            'is_active': true,
            'is_new': true,
          });
          existingProducts.add({
            'id': productId,
            'name': item['name'],
            'unit': aiUnit,
            'current_stock': convertedQty,
            'average_cost': incomingUnitCost,
          });
        }

        // Add to stock_movements for inventory balance
        stockMovementsToInsert.add({
          'id': const Uuid().v4(),
          'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
          'product_id': productId,
          'quantity': convertedQty,
          'unit': baseUnit,
          'type': 'purchase',
          'reference_id': purchaseId,
          'reason': 'ÐŸÑ€Ð¸Ñ…Ð¾Ð´ Ð¿Ð¾ Ð½Ð°ÐºÐ»Ð°Ð´Ð½Ð¾Ð¹',
        });

        // Add to purchase_items
        purchaseItemsToInsert.add({
          'id': const Uuid().v4(),
          'purchase_id': purchaseId,
          'product_id': productId,
          'quantity': aiQuantity,
          'unit': aiUnit,
          'unit_price': aiPrice,
          'total_price': aiQuantity * aiPrice,
        });
      }

      if (stockMovementsToInsert.isNotEmpty) {
        await _client.from('stock_movements').insert(stockMovementsToInsert);
      }
      if (purchaseItemsToInsert.isNotEmpty) {
        await _client.from('purchase_items').insert(purchaseItemsToInsert);
      }
      
      // 4. Save image document if provided
      if (imageFile != null) {
        final ext = imageFile.path.split('.').last;
        final fileName = '${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}.$ext';
        final storagePath = '$restaurantId/invoices/$fileName';
        
        try {
          await _client.storage.from('documents').upload(storagePath, imageFile);
          final String publicUrl = _client.storage.from('documents').getPublicUrl(storagePath);
          
          await _client.from('purchase_documents').insert({
            'id': const Uuid().v4(),
            'purchase_id': purchaseId,
            'document_url': publicUrl,
            'storage_path': storagePath,
            'mime_type': 'image/$ext',
            'file_size': await imageFile.length(),
          });
        } catch (storageError) {
          debugPrint('Warning: Failed to upload invoice image: $storageError');
          // Proceed anyway as purchase items are saved
        }
      }
      
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error saving AI purchase: $e');
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getPurchaseDetails(String purchaseId) async {
    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('purchase_items')
          .select('quantity, unit, unit_price, total_price, products(name)')
          .eq('purchase_id', purchaseId);
      
      return List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error fetching purchase details: $e');
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<String?> getPurchaseDocumentUrl(String purchaseId) async {
    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('purchase_documents')
          .select('document_url')
          .eq('purchase_id', purchaseId)
          .maybeSingle();
      return response?['document_url'] as String?;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error fetching purchase document url: $e');
      return null;
    }
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\purchases\data\services\ai_scanner_service.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:flutter_dotenv/flutter_dotenv.dart';

class AiScannerService {
  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> scanInvoice(File imageFile) async {
    try {
      final geminiApiKey = dotenv.env['GEMINI_API_KEY'];
      final openAiApiKey = dotenv.env['OPENAI_API_KEY'];
      final visionApiKey = dotenv.env['AI_VISION_API_KEY'];
      
      final bytes = await imageFile.readAsBytes();
      final base64Image = base64Encode(bytes);

      if (geminiApiKey != null && geminiApiKey.isNotEmpty) {
        final url = Uri.parse(
          'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-1.5-flash:generateContent?key=$geminiApiKey',
        );

        final payload = {
          "contents": [
            {
              "parts": [
                {
                  "text": "Extract all products, quantities, prices, and units from this invoice. "
                          "Return ONLY a raw JSON array of objects with keys: name (string), quantity (number), price (number), unit (string). "
                          "No markdown tags, no commentary. Example: [{\"name\":\"Go'sht\",\"quantity\":5,\"price\":95000,\"unit\":\"kg\"}]"
                },
                {
                  "inlineData": {
                    "mimeType": "image/jpeg",
                    "data": base64Image
                  }
                }
              ]
            }
          ]
        };

        final response = await http.post(
          url,
          headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
          body: jsonEncode(payload),
        );

        if (response.statusCode == 200) {
          final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
          final text = data['candidates']?[0]?['content']?['parts']?[0]?['text']?.toString().trim() ?? '[]';
          return _parseJsonList(text);
        } else {
          throw Exception('Gemini Vision API xatosi: ${response.statusCode} - ${response.body}');
        }
      }

      final apiKey = openAiApiKey ?? visionApiKey;
      if (apiKey == null || apiKey.isEmpty) {
        throw Exception('Vision OCR API kaliti topilmadi (.env faylida GEMINI_API_KEY yoki OPENAI_API_KEY kiriting)');
      }

      final url = Uri.parse('https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions');
      final payload = {
        "model": "gpt-4o-mini",
        "messages": [
          {
            "role": "system",
            "content": "You are a professional invoice parsing AI. Extract products, quantities, prices, and units from invoice image. Output ONLY valid JSON array of objects. Example: [{\"name\": \"Meat\", \"quantity\": 5, \"price\": 100000, \"unit\": \"kg\"}]"
          },
          {
            "role": "user",
            "content": [
              {
                "type": "text",
                "text": "Please extract the items from this invoice and return them as JSON."
              },
              {
                "type": "image_url",
                "image_url": {
                  "url": "data:image/jpeg;base64,$base64Image"
                }
              }
            ]
          }
        ],
        "temperature": 0.1
      };

      final response = await http.post(
        url,
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'Authorization': 'Bearer $apiKey',
        },
        body: jsonEncode(payload),
      );

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
        final content = data['choices'][0]['message']['content'].toString().trim();
        return _parseJsonList(content);
      } else {
        throw Exception('Vision API xatosi: ${response.statusCode} - ${response.body}');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('AI_SCAN_ERROR: $e');
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _parseJsonList(String raw) {
    String jsonStr = raw.trim();
    if (jsonStr.startsWith('```json')) {
      jsonStr = jsonStr.substring(7);
    }
    if (jsonStr.startsWith('```')) {
      jsonStr = jsonStr.substring(3);
    }
    if (jsonStr.endsWith('```')) {
      jsonStr = jsonStr.substring(0, jsonStr.length - 3);
    }
    final List<dynamic> parsedJson = jsonDecode(jsonStr.trim());
    return List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(parsedJson);
  }
}

enum OrderStatus {
  active,
  delivered,
  cancelled,
}

class PurchaseOrder {
  final String id;
  final String supplierName;
  final String date;
  final double totalAmount;
  final OrderStatus status;
  final int itemsCount;

  PurchaseOrder({
    required this.id,
    required this.supplierName,
    required this.date,
    required this.totalAmount,
    required this.status,
    this.itemsCount = 0,
  });
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../providers/purchases_provider.dart';
import '../../../inventory/presentation/providers/inventory_provider.dart';
import '../../../../core/utils/search_utils.dart';

class CreateRequestPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final PurchaseOrder? initialOrder;
  final List<Map<String, dynamic>>? initialItems;
  const CreateRequestPage({super.key, this.initialOrder, this.initialItems});

  @override
  State<CreateRequestPage> createState() => _CreateRequestPageState();
}

class _CreateRequestPageState extends State<CreateRequestPage> {
  final List<Map<String, dynamic>> _items = [];
  final TextEditingController _itemController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _quantityController = TextEditingController();
  String _selectedUnit = 'kg';
  final List<String> _units = ['kg', 'litr', 'dona', 'gr', 'quti'];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    if (widget.initialItems != null) {
      _items.addAll(widget.initialItems!);
    }
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      context.read<InventoryProvider>().fetchInventory(background: true);
    });
  }

  void _addItem() {
    final name = _itemController.text.trim();
    final qty = double.tryParse(_quantityController.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;
    
    if (name.isNotEmpty && qty > 0) {
      setState(() {
        _items.add({
          'name': name,
          'quantity': qty,
          'unit': _selectedUnit, 
        });
      });
      _itemController.clear();
      _quantityController.clear();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _sendToSupplier() async {
    if (_items.isEmpty) return;

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20))),
      builder: (sheetContext) {
        return _SupplierSelectionSheet(
          items: _items,
          onSupplierSelected: (Map<String, dynamic> supplier) async {
            Navigator.pop(sheetContext);
            await _createRequestAndOpenTelegram(supplier);
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> _createRequestAndOpenTelegram(Map<String, dynamic> supplier) async {
    try {
      final provider = context.read<PurchasesProvider>();
      if (widget.initialOrder != null) {
        await provider.updateRequest(widget.initialOrder!.id, _items, supplier['id']);
      } else {
        await provider.createRequest(_items, supplier['id']);
      }
      // After saving, open telegram
      await _openTelegram(supplier['phone'] ?? '');
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('${'purchases.save_error'.tr()}: $e'), 
            backgroundColor: AppColors.error
          ),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _openTelegram(String phone) async {
    final buffer = StringBuffer();
    buffer.writeln("purchases.telegram_msg_title".tr());
    buffer.writeln("purchases.telegram_msg_subtitle".tr());
    buffer.writeln("ðŸ“… ${DateTime.now().toString().substring(0, 10)}\n");
    
    for (var i = 0; i < _items.length; i++) {
      final item = _items[i];
      buffer.writeln("${i + 1}. ${item['name']} - ${item['quantity']} ${item['unit']}");
    }
    
    buffer.writeln("\n${"purchases.telegram_msg_footer".tr()}");

    final text = Uri.encodeComponent(buffer.toString());
    
    String formattedPhone = phone.trim();
    bool isUsername = false;
    
    if (formattedPhone.startsWith('@')) {
      formattedPhone = formattedPhone.substring(1);
      isUsername = true;
    } else if (double.tryParse(formattedPhone.replaceAll('+', '')) == null) {
      isUsername = true;
    } else if (!formattedPhone.startsWith('+')) {
      formattedPhone = '+$formattedPhone';
    }

    Uri directUrl;
    if (isUsername) {
      directUrl = Uri.parse("tg://resolve?domain=$formattedPhone&text=$text");
    } else {
      directUrl = Uri.parse("tg://msg?to=$formattedPhone&text=$text");
    }
    final webUrl = Uri.parse("https://t.me/$formattedPhone?text=$text");
    
    try {
      bool launched = await launchUrl(directUrl, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
      if (!launched) {
        await launchUrl(webUrl, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
      }
      if (mounted) {
        Navigator.pop(context);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      try {
        await launchUrl(webUrl, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
        if (mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
      } catch (e2) {
        if (mounted) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            SnackBar(
              content: Text('purchases.cant_send_telegram'.tr()), 
              backgroundColor: AppColors.error
            ),
          );
        }
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        title: Text('purchases.create_request'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
      ),
      body: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            // Item name input
            InkWell(
              onTap: () async {
                final provider = context.read<InventoryProvider>();
                final selected = await showSearch<dynamic>(
                  context: context,
                  delegate: ProductSearchDelegate(provider.items),
                );
                if (selected != null) {
                  setState(() {
                    if (selected is Map) {
                      _itemController.text = selected['name'];
                      if (!_units.contains(selected['unit'])) {
                        _units.add(selected['unit']);
                      }
                      _selectedUnit = selected['unit'];
                    } else {
                      _itemController.text = selected.name;
                      if (!_units.contains(selected.unit)) {
                        _units.add(selected.unit);
                      }
                      _selectedUnit = selected.unit;
                    }
                  });
                }
              },
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              child: IgnorePointer(
                child: TextField(
                  controller: _itemController,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    hintText: 'purchases.product_name_hint'.tr(),
                    hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54),
                    filled: true,
                    fillColor: AppColors.surface,
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
                    suffixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, color: Colors.white54),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            // Quantity and Unit Row
            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  flex: 2,
                  child: TextField(
                    controller: _quantityController,
                    keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(decimal: true),
                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      hintText: 'purchases.quantity'.tr(),
                      hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54),
                      filled: true,
                      fillColor: AppColors.surface,
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: AppColors.surface,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                  child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
                    child: DropdownButton<String>(
                      value: _selectedUnit,
                      dropdownColor: AppColors.surface,
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16),
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_drop_down, color: Colors.white70),
                      onChanged: (v) {
                        if (v != null) setState(() => _selectedUnit = v);
                      },
                      items: _units.map((u) => DropdownMenuItem(value: u, child: Text(u))).toList(),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),

                Container(

                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),

            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            if (_items.isNotEmpty)
              Expanded(

                    boxShadow: [

                        blurRadius: 10,
                        offset: const Offset(0, 5),
                      )
                    ]
                  ),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),

                      Text(
                        'purchases.request_title'.tr(),
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,

                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      const Divider(color: Colors.black38, thickness: 1),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Expanded(

                            final item = _items[index];
                            return Padding(

                                  Expanded(

                                  ),
                                  Text('${item['quantity']} ${item['unit']}', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                  GestureDetector(
                                    onTap: () {
                                      setState(() {
                                        _items.removeAt(index);
                                      });
                                    },
                                    child: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.redAccent, size: 20),
                                  ),

                child: ElevatedButton(

                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  ),

                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SupplierSelectionSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<Map<String, dynamic>> items;
  final Function(Map<String, dynamic> supplier) onSupplierSelected;

  const _SupplierSelectionSheet({required this.items, required this.onSupplierSelected});

  @override
  State<_SupplierSelectionSheet> createState() => _SupplierSelectionSheetState();
}

class _SupplierSelectionSheetState extends State<_SupplierSelectionSheet> {
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _suppliers = [];
  bool _loading = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      _fetchSuppliers();
    });
  }

  Future<void> _fetchSuppliers({bool force = false}) async {
    final provider = context.read<PurchasesProvider>();
    
    if (!force && provider.suppliers.isNotEmpty) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _suppliers = provider.suppliers;
          _loading = false;
        });
      }
      return;
    }

    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
    });
    
    try {
      final res = await provider.getSuppliers(force: force);
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _suppliers = res;
          _loading = false;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _loading = false;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _showAddSupplierDialog() async {
    final TextEditingController usernameController = TextEditingController();
    
    String searchStatus = 'idle'; // idle, loading, found, not_found, error
    String foundName = '';
    String foundUsername = '';
    String foundAvatar = '';
    String errorMsg = '';
    
    await showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (dialogCtx) {
        return StatefulBuilder(
          builder: (dialogContext, setDialogState) {
            return AlertDialog(
              backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
              title: Text('purchases.new_supplier'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
              content: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Text('purchases.enter_telegram_username'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  TextField(
                    controller: usernameController,
                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      hintText: '@username',
                      hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white38),
                      filled: true,
                      fillColor: AppColors.background,
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
                      suffixIcon: searchStatus == 'loading' 
                          ? const Padding(
                              padding: EdgeInsets.all(12.0),
                              child: SizedBox(width: 20, height: 20, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2)),
                            )
                          : null,
                    ),
                    onChanged: (val) {
                      if (searchStatus != 'idle') {
                        setDialogState(() => searchStatus = 'idle');
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                  
                  if (searchStatus == 'not_found')
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12.0),
                      child: Text('purchases.profile_not_found'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.redAccent)),
                    ),
                    
                  if (searchStatus == 'error')
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12.0),
                      child: Text(errorMsg, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.redAccent)),
                    ),
                    
                  if (searchStatus == 'found')
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 16.0),
                      child: Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: AppColors.background,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                          border: Border.all(color: AppColors.success.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
                        ),
                        child: Row(
                          children: [
                            CircleAvatar(
                              radius: 24,
                              backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                              backgroundImage: (foundAvatar.isNotEmpty && foundAvatar.startsWith('http')) ? NetworkImage(foundAvatar) : null,
                              child: (foundAvatar.isEmpty || !foundAvatar.startsWith('http')) ? const Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.white) : null,
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(width: 12),
                            Expanded(
                              child: Column(
                                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                children: [
                                  Text(foundName, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
                                  Text('@$foundUsername', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 14)),
                                ],
                              ),
                            )
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    )
                ],
              ),
              actions: [
                TextButton(
                  onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext),
                  child: Text('common.cancel'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)),
                ),
                
                if (searchStatus == 'idle' || searchStatus == 'not_found' || searchStatus == 'error')
                  ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: searchStatus == 'loading' ? null : () async {
                      String username = usernameController.text.trim();
                      if (username.isEmpty) return;
                      
                      if (username.startsWith('@')) {
                        username = username.substring(1);
                      }
                      
                      setDialogState(() => searchStatus = 'loading');
                      
                      try {
                        final url = Uri.parse('https://t.me/$username');
                        final response = await http.get(url);
                        
                        if (response.statusCode == 200) {
                          final html = response.body;
                          final titleMatch = RegExp(r'<meta property="og:title" content="([^"]+)">').firstMatch(html);
                          final imageMatch = RegExp(r'<meta property="og:image" content="([^"]+)">').firstMatch(html);
                          
                          String title = titleMatch?.group(1) ?? '';
                          String imageUrl = imageMatch?.group(1) ?? '';
                          
                          if (title.isNotEmpty && !title.contains('Telegram: Contact')) {
                            setDialogState(() {
                              searchStatus = 'found';
                              foundName = title;
                              foundUsername = username;
                              foundAvatar = imageUrl;
                            });
                          } else {
                            setDialogState(() => searchStatus = 'not_found');
                          }
                        } else {
                          setDialogState(() => searchStatus = 'not_found');
                        }
                      } catch (e) {
                        setDialogState(() {
                          searchStatus = 'error';
                          errorMsg = e.toString();
                        });
                      }
                    },
                    child: Text('purchases.search_btn'.tr()),
                  )
                else if (searchStatus == 'found' || searchStatus == 'adding')
                  ElevatedButton(
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.success),
                    onPressed: searchStatus == 'adding' ? null : () async {
                      setDialogState(() => searchStatus = 'adding');
                      try {
                        final provider = context.read<PurchasesProvider>();
                        await provider.addSupplier(
                          name: foundName,
                          contactName: foundAvatar, 
                          phone: foundUsername
                        );
                        if (dialogContext.mounted) {
                          Navigator.pop(dialogContext);
                        }
                        if (mounted) {
                          _fetchSuppliers(force: true);
                        }
                      } catch (e) {
                        setDialogState(() {
                          searchStatus = 'error';
                          errorMsg = e.toString();
                        });
                      }
                    },
                    child: searchStatus == 'adding'
                        ? const SizedBox(width: 20, height: 20, child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2))
                        : Text('common.add'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                  )
              ],
            );
          }
        );
      }
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Text('purchases.who_to_send'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          if (_loading) const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          else if (_suppliers.isEmpty)
            Expanded(
              child: Center(
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Text('purchases.empty_suppliers'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    ElevatedButton(
                      onPressed: _showAddSupplierDialog,
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent),
                      child: Text('purchases.new_supplier'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                    )
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            )
          else
            Expanded(
              child: ListView.builder(
                itemCount: _suppliers.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  final sup = _suppliers[index];
                  final String? imageUrl = sup['contact_name']; // We stored og:image here
                  return ListTile(
                    leading: CircleAvatar(
                      backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                      backgroundImage: (imageUrl != null && imageUrl.startsWith('http')) ? NetworkImage(imageUrl) : null,
                      child: (imageUrl == null || !imageUrl.startsWith('http')) ? const Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.white) : null,
                    ),
                    title: Text(sup['name'], style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                    subtitle: Text('@${sup['phone'] ?? ''}', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)),
                    trailing: const Icon(Icons.telegram, color: Colors.blue, size: 32),
                    onTap: () {
                      if (sup['phone'] != null && sup['phone'].toString().isNotEmpty) {
                        widget.onSupplierSelected(sup);
                      }
                    },
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          
          if (_suppliers.isNotEmpty && !_loading)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 16.0),
              child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                onPressed: _showAddSupplierDialog,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
                label: Text('purchases.new_supplier'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: AppColors.success,
                  minimumSize: const Size.fromHeight(50),
                ),
              ),
            )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class ProductSearchDelegate extends SearchDelegate<dynamic> {
  final List<dynamic> items;

  ProductSearchDelegate(this.items);

  @override
  String get searchFieldLabel => 'common.search'.tr();

  @override
  ThemeData appBarTheme(BuildContext context) {
    final ThemeData theme = Theme.of(context);
    return theme.copyWith(
      appBarTheme: const AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: const InputDecorationTheme(
        hintStyle: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
        border: InputBorder.none,
      ),
      textTheme: const TextTheme(
        titleLarge: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Widget> buildActions(BuildContext context) {
    return [
      if (query.isNotEmpty)
        IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.clear, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
          onPressed: () => query = '',
        ),
    ];
  }

  @override
  Widget buildLeading(BuildContext context) {
    return IconButton(
      icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
      onPressed: () => close(context, null),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget buildResults(BuildContext context) => _buildList(context);

  @override
  Widget buildSuggestions(BuildContext context) => _buildList(context);

  Widget _buildList(BuildContext context) {
    final normalizedQuery = SearchUtils.normalize(query);
    
    final filteredItems = items.where((item) {
      final normalizedName = SearchUtils.normalize(item.name);
      return normalizedName.contains(normalizedQuery);
    }).toList();

    return Container(
      color: AppColors.background,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          if (query.trim().isNotEmpty)
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.add, color: AppColors.success),
              title: Text('${'common.add'.tr()}: "${query.trim()}"', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.success, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              onTap: () => close(context, {'name': query.trim(), 'unit': 'kg', 'isCustom': true}),
            ),
          if (query.trim().isNotEmpty)
            const Divider(color: Colors.white10, height: 1),
          Expanded(
            child: filteredItems.isEmpty
                ? Center(child: Text('common.not_found'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)))
                : ListView.builder(
                    itemCount: filteredItems.length,
                    itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                      final item = filteredItems[index];
                      return ListTile(
                        title: Text(item.name, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                        subtitle: Text('${'recipes.in_stock'.tr()}: ${item.quantity} ${item.unit}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                        onTap: () => close(context, item),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
