import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/order.dart';
import '../providers/purchases_provider.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import '../../data/services/ai_scanner_service.dart';
import 'create_request_page.dart';

class PurchasesPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const PurchasesPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<PurchasesPage> createState() => _PurchasesPageState();
}

class _PurchasesPageState extends State<PurchasesPage> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late TabController _tabController;
  final AiScannerService _aiScanner = AiScannerService();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabController = TabController(length: 2, vsync: this);
    _tabController.index = 1; // Default to "ÐÐ°ÐºÐ»Ð°Ð´Ð½Ñ‹Ðµ"
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      final provider = context.read<PurchasesProvider>();
      if (provider.orders.isEmpty) {
        provider.fetchPurchases();
      }
    });
  }

  Future<void> _scanInvoice({String? existingPurchaseId}) async {
    final picker = ImagePicker();
    final pickedFile = await picker.pickImage(
      source: ImageSource.gallery,
      imageQuality: 50,
      maxWidth: 1024,
    ); // Or camera
    
    if (pickedFile == null) return;
    if (!mounted) return;
    
    // Show Loading
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (context) => Dialog(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              const CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.blueAccent),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              Text(
                "purchases.ai_reading".tr(),
                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    try {
      final file = File(pickedFile.path);
      List<Map<String, dynamic>> items = await _aiScanner.scanInvoice(file);
      
      // Close loading
      if (mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
      
      if (items.isEmpty) {
        throw Exception("purchases.ai_empty".tr());
      }
      
      // Show confirmation dialog
      _showConfirmationDialog(items, file, existingPurchaseId: existingPurchaseId);
    } catch (e) {
      // Close loading
      if (mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
      
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('${"purchases.error".tr()}: $e'), backgroundColor: AppColors.error, duration: const Duration(seconds: 10)),
      );
    }
  }

  void _showEditItemDialog(BuildContext context, Map<String, dynamic> item, VoidCallback onSave) {
    final nameController = TextEditingController(text: item['name']?.toString() ?? '');
    final qtyController = TextEditingController(text: item['quantity']?.toString() ?? '1');
    final priceController = TextEditingController(text: item['price']?.toString() ?? '0');
    final unitController = TextEditingController(text: item['unit']?.toString() ?? 'kg');

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (dlgContext) {
        return AlertDialog(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
          title: Text('common.edit'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                TextField(
                  controller: nameController,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'purchases.product_name_hint'.tr(),
                    labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      flex: 2,
                      child: TextField(
                        controller: qtyController,
                        keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(decimal: true),
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          labelText: 'purchases.quantity'.tr(),
                          labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(
                      flex: 1,
                      child: TextField(
                        controller: unitController,
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          labelText: 'Birlik',
                          labelStyle: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                TextField(
                  controller: priceController,
                  keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(decimal: true),
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'Narx (${'common.currency_sum'.tr()})',
                    labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dlgContext),
              child: Text('common.cancel'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white60)),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () {
                final qty = double.tryParse(qtyController.text) ?? 1.0;
                final price = double.tryParse(priceController.text) ?? 0.0;
                item['name'] = nameController.text.trim();
                item['quantity'] = qty;
                item['price'] = price;
                item['unit'] = unitController.text.trim();
                onSave();
                Navigator.pop(dlgContext);
              },
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.primary),
              child: Text('common.save'.tr()),
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _showConfirmationDialog(List<Map<String, dynamic>> items, File file, {String? existingPurchaseId}) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (sheetContext) {
        return StatefulBuilder(
          builder: (modalCtx, setModalState) {
            double currentTotal = 0;
            for (var item in items) {
              currentTotal += ((item['quantity'] ?? 0) as num).toDouble() * ((item['price'] ?? 0) as num).toDouble();
            }

            return Container(
              height: MediaQuery.of(modalCtx).size.height * 0.85,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      Text('purchases.ai_confirm'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.add_circle_outline, color: Colors.blueAccent),
                        tooltip: 'Qo\'shish',
                        onPressed: () {
                          final newItem = {'name': '', 'quantity': 1.0, 'unit': 'kg', 'price': 0.0};
                          _showEditItemDialog(modalCtx, newItem, () {
                            if ((newItem['name'] as String).isNotEmpty) {
                              setModalState(() {
                                items.add(newItem);
                              });
                            }
                          });
                        },
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text('${"purchases.total_sum".tr()}: ${currentTotal.toStringAsFixed(0)} ${'common.currency_sum'.tr()}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.success, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  const Divider(color: Colors.white10),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Container(
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: const Color(0xFFFDFBF7),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      ),
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                      child: items.isEmpty
                          ? Center(child: Text('purchases.no_items_in_invoice'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black54)))
                          : ListView.builder(
                              itemCount: items.length,
                              itemBuilder: (itemCtx, index) {
                                final item = items[index];
                                final q = ((item['quantity'] ?? 0) as num).toDouble();
                                final p = ((item['price'] ?? 0) as num).toDouble();
                                final rowSum = q * p;
                                return Padding(
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4.0),
                                  child: Row(
                                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                                    children: [
                                      Text('${index + 1}. ', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black87, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                                      Expanded(
                                        child: Column(
                                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                          children: [
                                            Text(item['name'].toString(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black87, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                                            Text('$q ${item['unit']} x $p ${'common.currency_sum'.tr()}', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black54, fontSize: 12)),
                                          ],
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      Text(rowSum.toStringAsFixed(0), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                                      IconButton(
                                        icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_outlined, size: 18, color: Colors.blueGrey),
                                        onPressed: () {
                                          _showEditItemDialog(modalCtx, item, () {
                                            setModalState(() {});
                                          });
                                        },
                                      ),
                                      IconButton(
                                        icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline, size: 18, color: Colors.redAccent),
                                        onPressed: () {
                                          setModalState(() {
                                            items.removeAt(index);
                                          });
                                        },
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                );
                              },
                            ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: OutlinedButton(
                          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(modalCtx),
                          style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                            side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white24),
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                          ),
                          child: Text('purchases.cancel'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 16),
                      Expanded(
                        child: ElevatedButton(
                          onPressed: items.isEmpty ? null : () async {
                            try {
                              Navigator.pop(modalCtx);
                              showDialog(
                                context: context,
                                barrierDismissible: false,
                                builder: (dialogCtx) => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.blueAccent)),
                              );
                              await context.read<PurchasesProvider>().saveAIPurchase(items, imageFile: file, existingPurchaseId: existingPurchaseId);
                              if (!mounted) return;
                              Navigator.pop(context);
                              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                                SnackBar(content: Text('purchases.saved_successfully'.tr()), backgroundColor: AppColors.success),
                              );
                            } catch (e) {
                              if (!mounted) return;
                              Navigator.pop(context);
                              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                                SnackBar(content: Text('${"purchases.save_error".tr()}: $e'), backgroundColor: AppColors.error),
                              );
                            }
                          },
                          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                          ),
                          child: Text('purchases.confirm'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _createNewRequest() {
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const CreateRequestPage()),
    );
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> _fetchDetailsAndImage(BuildContext context, String purchaseId) async {
    final provider = context.read<PurchasesProvider>();
    final items = await provider.getPurchaseDetails(purchaseId);
    final imageUrl = await provider.getPurchaseDocumentUrl(purchaseId);
    return {
      'items': items,
      'imageUrl': imageUrl,
    };
  }

          return isInvoiceTab

                );
        },

  }

  Widget _buildRequestsList() {
    return Consumer<PurchasesProvider>(
      builder: (context, provider, child) {
        if (provider.isLoading) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.blueAccent));
        }

        if (provider.error != null) {
          return Center(child: Text(provider.error!, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)));
        }

        final requestsList = provider.orders.where((o) => o.status == OrderStatus.active).toList();

        if (requestsList.isEmpty) {
          return Center(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.assignment, size: 64, color: Colors.white24),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                Text(
                  "purchases.no_requests".tr(),
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 18),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  "purchases.add_request_hint".tr(),
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white38, fontSize: 14),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        }

        return ListView.builder(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          itemCount: requestsList.length,
          itemBuilder: (context, index) {
            final order = requestsList[index];
            
            return InkWell(
              onTap: () => _showPurchaseDetails(context, order),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              child: Container(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.surface,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                  border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                ),
                child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    '#${order.id.substring(0, 8)} â€¢ ${order.date}',
                    style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          order.supplierName,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                            fontSize: 16,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        '${order.totalAmount.toInt()} ${"purchases.currency".tr()}',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: AppColors.success,
                          fontSize: 16,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.orangeAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      'purchases.status_expected'.tr(),
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.orangeAccent,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInvoicesList() {
    return Consumer<PurchasesProvider>(
      builder: (context, provider, child) {
        if (provider.isLoading) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.blueAccent));
        }

        if (provider.error != null) {
          return Center(child: Text(provider.error!, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)));
        }

        final invoicesList = provider.orders.where((o) => o.status == OrderStatus.delivered).toList();

        if (invoicesList.isEmpty) {
          return Center(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.receipt_long, size: 64, color: Colors.white24),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                Text(
                  "purchases.no_invoices".tr(),
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 18),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  "purchases.add_invoice_hint".tr(),
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white38, fontSize: 14),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        }

        return ListView.builder(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          itemCount: invoicesList.length,
          itemBuilder: (context, index) {
            final order = invoicesList[index];
            
            return InkWell(
              onTap: () => _showPurchaseDetails(context, order),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              child: Container(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.surface,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                  border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                ),
                child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    '#${order.id.substring(0, 8)} â€¢ ${order.date}',
                    style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          order.supplierName,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                            fontSize: 16,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        '${order.totalAmount.toInt()} ${"purchases.currency".tr()}',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: AppColors.success,
                          fontSize: 16,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.success.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      'purchases.status_delivered'.tr(),
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: AppColors.success,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _showPurchaseDetails(BuildContext context, PurchaseOrder order) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (sheetContext) {
        return DraggableScrollableSheet(
          initialChildSize: 0.8,
          minChildSize: 0.5,
          maxChildSize: 0.95,
          builder: (_, controller) {
            return Container(
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.background,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
              ),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                    decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                      border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: Colors.white10)),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          '${"purchases.invoice".tr()} #${order.id.substring(0, 8)}',
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 18,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            color: Colors.white,
                          ),
                        ),
                        if (order.status == OrderStatus.active)
                          IconButton(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.edit, color: Colors.blueAccent),
                            onPressed: () {
                              Navigator.pop(sheetContext);
                              _fetchDetailsAndImage(context, order.id).then((data) {
                                final items = (data['items'] as List).map((i) {
                                  return {
                                    'name': i['products']?['name'] ?? '',
                                    'quantity': i['quantity'] ?? 0,
                                    'unit': i['unit'] ?? 'kg',
                                  };
                                }).toList();
                                if (!context.mounted) return;
                                Navigator.push(
                                  context,
                                  MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => CreateRequestPage(initialOrder: order, initialItems: items)),
                                );
                              });
                            },
                          ),
                        if (order.status == OrderStatus.active)
                          IconButton(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.delete, color: Colors.redAccent),
                            onPressed: () async {
                              final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
                                context: context,
                                builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
                                  backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
                                  title: Text('purchases.confirm_delete_title'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                                  actions: [
                                    TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: Text('common.cancel'.tr())),
                                    TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true), child: Text('common.delete'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.red))),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              );
                              if (confirm == true) {
                                if (sheetContext.mounted) Navigator.pop(sheetContext);
                                if (context.mounted) {
                                  context.read<PurchasesProvider>().deleteRequest(order.id);
                                }
                              }
                            },
                          ),
                        IconButton(
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.white),
                          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(sheetContext),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Expanded(
                    child: FutureBuilder<Map<String, dynamic>>(
                      future: _fetchDetailsAndImage(context, order.id),
                      builder: (context, snapshot) {
                        if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
                        }
                        if (snapshot.hasError) {
                          return Center(
                            child: Text('purchases.data_load_error'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)),
                          );
                        }
                        final data = snapshot.data ?? {};
                        final items = data['items'] as List<Map<String, dynamic>>? ?? [];
                        final imageUrl = data['imageUrl'] as String?;
                        
                        if (items.isEmpty && imageUrl == null) {
                          return Center(
                            child: Text('purchases.no_items_in_invoice'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.grey)),
                          );
                        }
                        return ListView(
                          controller: controller,
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                          children: [
                            if (imageUrl != null)
                              Container(
                                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 24),
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                                  border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                                ),
                                child: ClipRRect(
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                                  child: Image.network(
                                    imageUrl,
                                    fit: BoxFit.contain,
                                    errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => const Padding(
                                      padding: EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                                      child: Icon(Icons.broken_image, color: Colors.white38, size: 48),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            Container(
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: const Color(0xFFFDFBF7),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                              ),
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                              child: Column(
                                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                                children: [
                                  Text(
                                    'purchases.items_list'.tr(),
                                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                                  const Divider(color: Colors.black38, thickness: 1),
                                  ...items.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                                    final index = entry.key;
                                    final item = entry.value;
                                    final productName = item['products']?['name'] ?? 'purchases.unknown_product'.tr();
                                    final quantity = item['quantity'];
                                    final unit = item['unit'];
                                    final unitPrice = item['unit_price'];
                                    final totalPrice = item['total_price'];

                                    return Padding(
                                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6.0),
                                      child: Row(
                                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                        children: [
                                          Text('${index + 1}. ', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black87, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                                          Expanded(
                                            child: Column(
                                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                              children: [
                                                Text(productName, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black87, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                                                Text('$quantity $unit x ${unitPrice.toInt()} ${'common.currency_sum'.tr()}', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black54, fontSize: 12)),
                                              ],
                                            ),
                                          ),
                                          Text('${totalPrice.toInt()} ${'common.currency_sum'.tr()}', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                                        ],
                                      ),
                                    );
                                  }),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        );
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (order.status == OrderStatus.active)
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                      child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                        onPressed: () {
                          Navigator.pop(sheetContext);
                          _scanInvoice(existingPurchaseId: order.id);
                        },
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.document_scanner, color: Colors.white),
                        label: Text('purchases.scan_invoice'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                          minimumSize: const Size.fromHeight(50),
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }
}



// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\purchases\presentation\providers\purchases_provider.dart
// ==========================================

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../../core/audit/audit_service.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/order.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/purchases_repository.dart';

class PurchasesProvider extends ChangeNotifier {
  final PurchasesRepository _repository = PurchasesRepository();
  
  List<PurchaseOrder> _orders = [];
  List<PurchaseOrder> get orders => _orders;
  
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;
  
  String? _error;
  String? get error => _error;

  final bool _useMockData = true;

  Future<void> fetchPurchases({bool background = false}) async {
    if (!background && _orders.isEmpty) {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
      notifyListeners();
    }
    
    try {
      final data = await _repository.getPurchases();
      if (data.isEmpty && _useMockData) {
        _orders = _getMockOrders();
      } else {
        _orders = data;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      if (_useMockData) {
         _orders = _getMockOrders();
      }
    }
    if (_isLoading) {
      _isLoading = false;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _suppliers = [];
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> get suppliers => _suppliers;
  bool _suppliersLoaded = false;

  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getSuppliers({bool force = false}) async {
    if (!_suppliersLoaded || force) {
      _suppliers = await _repository.getSuppliers();
      _suppliersLoaded = true;
      // notifyListeners(); // optional, but better return it
    }
    return _suppliers;
  }

  Future<void> addSupplier({required String name, required String contactName, required String phone}) async {
    await _repository.addSupplier(name: name, contactName: contactName, phone: phone);
    _suppliersLoaded = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }
  
  Future<void> createRequest(List<Map<String, dynamic>> items, String supplierId) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      await _repository.createRequest(items, supplierId);
      AuditService().logAction(
        department: 'purchases',
        action: 'Yangi zayavka yaratildi',
        details: '${items.length} ta tovar bo`yicha xarid buyurtmasi shakllantirildi.',
      );
      await fetchPurchases();
    } catch (e) {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<void> updateRequest(String orderId, List<Map<String, dynamic>> items, String supplierId) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      await _repository.updateRequest(orderId, items, supplierId);
      await fetchPurchases();
    } catch (e) {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<void> deleteRequest(String orderId) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      await _repository.deleteRequest(orderId);
      _orders.removeWhere((o) => o.id == orderId);
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
    }
    _isLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  List<PurchaseOrder> _getMockOrders() {
    return [
      PurchaseOrder(
        id: '1',
        supplierName: 'MChJ "Baraka Savdo"',
        date: '2023-10-25',
        totalAmount: 1250000,
        status: OrderStatus.delivered,
        itemsCount: 15,
      ),
      PurchaseOrder(
        id: '2',
        supplierName: 'AgroFarm',
        date: '2023-10-26',
        totalAmount: 840000,
        status: OrderStatus.active,
        itemsCount: 8,
      ),
      PurchaseOrder(
        id: '3',
        supplierName: 'Green World',
        date: '2023-10-27',
        totalAmount: 450000,
        status: OrderStatus.active,
        itemsCount: 5,
      ),
    ];
  }
  Future<void> saveAIPurchase(List<Map<String, dynamic>> items, {File? imageFile, String? existingPurchaseId}) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      await _repository.saveAIPurchase(items, imageFile: imageFile, existingPurchaseId: existingPurchaseId);
      AuditService().logAction(
        department: 'purchases',
        action: 'AI Nakladnoy saqlandi',
        details: '${items.length} ta tovarli nakladnoy AI orqali tizimga kiritildi.',
      );
      await fetchPurchases(); // Refresh list after saving
    } catch (e) {
      _isLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getPurchaseDetails(String purchaseId) async {
    return await _repository.getPurchaseDetails(purchaseId);
  }

  Future<String?> getPurchaseDocumentUrl(String purchaseId) async {
    return await _repository.getPurchaseDocumentUrl(purchaseId);
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\recipes\data\models\recipe_model.dart
// ==========================================

import '../../domain/entities/recipe_entity.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/recipe_item_entity.dart';

class RecipeModel {
  final String id;
  final String restaurantId;
  final String name;
  final String? description;
  final double sellingPrice;
  final double outputQuantity;
  final String outputUnit;
  final bool isActive;
  final String? imageUrl;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
  
  // We can attach joined items
  final List<RecipeItemModel> items;

  RecipeModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.restaurantId,
    required this.name,
    this.description,
    required this.sellingPrice,
    required this.outputQuantity,
    required this.outputUnit,
    required this.isActive,
    this.imageUrl,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    this.items = const [],
  });

  factory RecipeModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    var itemsList = <RecipeItemModel>[];
    if (json['recipe_items'] != null) {
      itemsList = (json['recipe_items'] as List)
          .map((i) => RecipeItemModel.fromJson(i))
          .toList();
    }

    return RecipeModel(
      id: json['id'],
      restaurantId: json['restaurant_id'],
      name: json['name'],
      description: json['description'],
      sellingPrice: (json['selling_price'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
      outputQuantity: (json['output_quantity'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
      outputUnit: json['output_unit'] ?? 'Ð³',
      isActive: json['is_active'] ?? true,
      imageUrl: json['image_url'],
      createdAt: DateTime.tryParse(json['created_at']?.toString() ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt: DateTime.tryParse(json['updated_at']?.toString() ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
      items: itemsList,
    );
  }

  RecipeEntity toEntity() {
    List<RecipeItemEntity> recipeItems = [];

    for (var item in items) {
      double itemPrice = item.productPrice; 
      
      recipeItems.add(
        RecipeItemEntity(
          id: item.id,
          recipeId: item.recipeId,
          productId: item.productId,
          productName: item.productName,
          quantity: item.quantity,
          unit: item.unit,
          productPrice: itemPrice,
        ),
      );
    }

    String parsedCategory = 'Asosiy';
    if (description != null && description!.startsWith('Category: ')) {
      parsedCategory = description!.replaceFirst('Category: ', '');
    }

    return RecipeEntity(
      id: id,
      name: name,
      category: parsedCategory,
      description: description,
      sellingPrice: sellingPrice,
      outputQuantity: outputQuantity,
      outputUnit: outputUnit,
      isActive: isActive,
      imageUrl: imageUrl,
      items: recipeItems,
    );
  }
}

class RecipeItemModel {
  final String id;
  final String recipeId;
  final String productId;
  final double quantity;
  final String unit;
  
  // Joined fields from 'products' table
  final String productName;
  final double productPrice;

  RecipeItemModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.recipeId,
    required this.productId,
    required this.quantity,
    required this.unit,
    this.productName = '',
    this.productPrice = 0.0,
  });

  factory RecipeItemModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    // Handling joined product data if available
    String pName = 'Unknown Product';
    double pPrice = 0.0; // In reality, price comes from latest purchase or standard cost
    
    if (json['products'] != null) {
      pName = json['products']['name'] ?? pName;
      pPrice = (json['products']['average_cost'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
    }

    return RecipeItemModel(
      id: json['id'],
      recipeId: json['recipe_id'],
      productId: json['product_id'],
      quantity: (json['quantity'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
      unit: json['unit'] ?? 'Ð³',
      productName: pName,
      productPrice: pPrice,
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\recipes\data\repositories\recipes_repository.dart
// ==========================================

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';
import '../../../../core/network/supabase_service.dart';
import '../models/recipe_model.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/recipe_entity.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/ingredient_option.dart';

class RecipesRepository {
  final SupabaseClient _client = SupabaseService.client;
  
  Future<List<RecipeEntity>> getRecipes() async {
    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('recipes')
          .select('''
            *,
            recipe_items (
              *,
              products (
                name,
                average_cost
              )
            )
          ''')
          .eq('restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId)
          .eq('is_active', true)
          .order('name');
          
      final List<RecipeModel> models = (response as List)
          .map((json) => RecipeModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
          
      return models.map((e) => e.toEntity()).toList();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error fetching recipes: $e');
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<RecipeEntity> addRecipe({
    required String name,
    required String category,
    required double sellingPrice,
    required double outputQuantity,
    required String outputUnit,
  }) async {
    final restaurantId = SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId;

    final response = await _client.from('recipes').insert({
      'id': const Uuid().v4(),
      'restaurant_id': restaurantId,
      'name': name,
      'description': 'Category: $category',
      'selling_price': sellingPrice,
      'output_quantity': outputQuantity,
      'output_unit': outputUnit,
      'is_active': true,
    }).select().single();

    final RecipeModel newRecipe = RecipeModel.fromJson(response);
    return newRecipe.toEntity();
  }

  Future<void> addRecipeItem({
    required String recipeId,
    required String productId,
    required double quantity,
    required String unit,
  }) async {
    await _client.from('recipe_items').insert({
      'id': const Uuid().v4(),
      'recipe_id': recipeId,
      'product_id': productId,
      'quantity': quantity,
      'unit': unit,
    });
  }

  Future<String> uploadRecipeImage(String recipeId, String imagePath) async {
    final file = File(imagePath);
    final bytes = await file.readAsBytes();
    final String path = 'recipe_${recipeId}_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}.jpg';
    
    await _client.storage.from('images').uploadBinary(
      path,
      bytes,
      fileOptions: const FileOptions(contentType: 'image/jpeg', upsert: true),
    );
    
    final imageUrl = _client.storage.from('images').getPublicUrl(path);
    await _client.from('recipes').update({'image_url': imageUrl}).eq('id', recipeId);
    return imageUrl;
  }

  Future<List<IngredientOption>> getIngredientOptions() async {
    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('products_with_stock')
          .select()
          .eq('restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId)
          .eq('is_active', true)
          .order('name');

      final pricesResponse = await _client
          .from('purchase_items')
          .select('product_id, unit_price, purchases!inner(date)')
          .eq('purchases.restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId)
          .order('date', referencedTable: 'purchases', ascending: false);

      final Map<String, double> latestPrices = {};
      for (var row in (pricesResponse as List)) {
        final String pid = row['product_id'];
        if (!latestPrices.containsKey(pid)) {
          latestPrices[pid] = (row['unit_price'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        }
      }
          
      return (response as List).map((json) {
        final option = IngredientOption.fromJson(json);
        final currentStock = (json['current_stock'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        final currentPrice = latestPrices[option.id] ?? option.averageCost;
        return IngredientOption(
          id: option.id,
          name: option.name,
          unit: option.unit,
          averageCost: currentPrice,
          currentQuantity: currentStock,
        );
      }).toList();
    } catch (e) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<void> deleteRecipeItem(String itemId) async {
    await _client.from('recipe_items').delete().eq('id', itemId);
  }

  Future<void> updateRecipeDescription(String recipeId, String description) async {
    await _client.from('recipes').update({
      'description': description,
      'updated_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
    }).eq('id', recipeId);
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\recipes\domain\entities\ingredient_option.dart
// ==========================================

class IngredientOption {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String unit;
  final double currentQuantity;
  final double averageCost;

  IngredientOption({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.unit,
    required this.currentQuantity,
    required this.averageCost,
  });

  factory IngredientOption.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return IngredientOption(
      id: json['id'] as String,
      name: json['name'] as String,
      unit: json['unit'] as String? ?? 'kg',
      currentQuantity: (json['current_stock'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      averageCost: (json['average_cost'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\recipes\domain\entities\recipe_entity.dart
// ==========================================

import 'recipe_item_entity.dart';

class RecipeEntity {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String category;
  final String? description;
  final double sellingPrice;
  final double outputQuantity;
  final String outputUnit;
  final bool isActive;
  final String? imageUrl;
  final List<RecipeItemEntity> items;

  RecipeEntity({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.category,
    this.description,
    required this.sellingPrice,
    required this.outputQuantity,
    required this.outputUnit,
    required this.isActive,
    this.imageUrl,
    required this.items,
  });

  double get costPrice {
    double total = 0;
    for (var item in items) {
      total += item.sum;
    }
    return total;
  }

  double get foodcostPercentage {
    if (sellingPrice == 0) return 0.0;
    return (costPrice / sellingPrice) * 100;
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\recipes\domain\entities\recipe_item_entity.dart
// ==========================================

class RecipeItemEntity {
  final String id;
  final String recipeId;
  final String productId;
  final String productName;
  final double quantity;
  final String unit;
  final double productPrice;

  RecipeItemEntity({
    required this.id,
    required this.recipeId,
    required this.productId,
    required this.productName,
    required this.quantity,
    required this.unit,
    required this.productPrice,
  });

  double get sum => quantity * productPrice;
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../providers/recipes_provider.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'recipe_detail_page.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/recipe_entity.dart';
import '../../../../core/utils/search_utils.dart';

class RecipesPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const RecipesPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<RecipesPage> createState() => _RecipesPageState();
}

class _RecipesPageState extends State<RecipesPage> {
  int _selectedCategoryIndex = 0;
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();
  String? _highlightedRecipeId;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      final provider = context.read<RecipesProvider>();
      provider.fetchRecipes(background: provider.recipes.isNotEmpty);
    });
  }
  
  void _scrollToItem(String recipeId) {
    setState(() {
      _selectedCategoryIndex = 0;
      _highlightedRecipeId = recipeId;
    });

    Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100), () {
      if (!mounted) return;
      final provider = context.read<RecipesProvider>();
      final index = provider.recipes.indexWhere((r) => r.id == recipeId);
      if (index != -1 && _scrollController.hasClients) {
        final maxScroll = _scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent;
        final targetOffset = index * 100.0;
        _scrollController.animateTo(
          targetOffset > maxScroll ? maxScroll : targetOffset,
          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
          curve: Curves.easeInOut,
        );
      }
    });

    Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2), () {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _highlightedRecipeId = null;
        });
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        elevation: 0,
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
          onPressed: () {
            Navigator.of(context).pop();
          },
        ),
        title: Text('recipes.title'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.search, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
            onPressed: () async {
              final provider = context.read<RecipesProvider>();
              final selectedId = await showSearch(
                context: context,
                delegate: RecipeSearchDelegate(provider.recipes),
              );
              if (selectedId != null) {
                _scrollToItem(selectedId);
              }
            },
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.more_vert, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
            onPressed: () {},
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: Consumer<RecipesProvider>(
        builder: (context, provider, child) {
          if (provider.isLoading && provider.recipes.isEmpty) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
          }
          
          if (provider.error != null && provider.recipes.isEmpty) {
            return Center(child: Text(provider.error!, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)));
          }

          final recipes = provider.recipes;
          
          final String allCategory = 'common.all'.tr();
          final Set<String> uniqueCategories = {allCategory};
          for (var recipe in recipes) {
            uniqueCategories.add(recipe.category);
          }
          final categories = uniqueCategories.toList();

          int selectedIndex = _selectedCategoryIndex;
          if (selectedIndex >= categories.length) {
            selectedIndex = 0;
          }

          final selectedCategory = categories[selectedIndex];

          final filteredRecipes = selectedCategory == allCategory
              ? recipes
              : recipes.where((r) => r.category == selectedCategory).toList();

          return Column(
            children: [
              // Categories
              SizedBox(
                height: 48,
                child: ListView.builder(
                  scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                  itemCount: categories.length,
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                    final isSelected = selectedIndex == index;
                    return Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4, vertical: 8),
                      child: InkWell(
                        onTap: () {
                          setState(() {
                            _selectedCategoryIndex = index;
                          });
                        },
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                          alignment: Alignment.center,
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: isSelected ? Colors.blueAccent : AppColors.surface,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                            border: isSelected ? null : Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            categories[index],
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: isSelected ? Colors.white : AppColors.textSecondary,
                              fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.w600 : FontWeight.normal,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                ),
              ),
              
              const SizedBox(height: 8),

              // Recipes List
              Expanded(
                child: filteredRecipes.isEmpty
                    ? Center(child: Text('recipes.no_recipes'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)))
                    : ListView.builder(
                        controller: _scrollController,
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
                        itemCount: filteredRecipes.length,
                        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                          final recipe = filteredRecipes[index];
                          final isHighlighted = recipe.id == _highlightedRecipeId;
                          return GestureDetector(
                            onTap: () {
                              Navigator.push(
                                context,
                                MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => RecipeDetailPage(recipe: recipe)),
                              );
                            },
                            child: AnimatedContainer(
                              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
                              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: isHighlighted ? Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.2) : AppColors.surface,
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                                border: Border.all(color: isHighlighted ? Colors.blueAccent : Colors.white10),
                              ),
                              child: Row(
                                children: [
                                  // Image or Icon
                                  Container(
                                    width: 70,
                                    height: 70,
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: AppColors.surfaceLight,
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                                      image: recipe.imageUrl != null && recipe.imageUrl!.isNotEmpty
                                          ? DecorationImage(
                                              image: NetworkImage(recipe.imageUrl!),
                                              fit: BoxFit.cover,
                                            )
                                          : null,
                                    ),
                                    child: recipe.imageUrl == null || recipe.imageUrl!.isEmpty
                                        ? Center(
                                            child: Text(
                                              recipe.name.isNotEmpty ? recipe.name[0].toUpperCase() : '?',
                                              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                                            ),
                                          )
                                        : null,
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 16),
                                  // Details
                                  Expanded(
                                    child: Column(
                                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                      children: [
                                        Text(
                                          recipe.name,
                                          maxLines: 1,
                                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                          style: const TextStyle(
                                            fontSize: 16,
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                            color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                                        Row(
                                          children: [
                                            const Icon(Icons.pie_chart, size: 14, color: AppColors.success),
                                            const SizedBox(width: 4),
                                            Text(
                                              'Foodcost: ${recipe.foodcostPercentage.toStringAsFixed(1)}%',
                                              style: const TextStyle(
                                                color: AppColors.success,
                                                fontSize: 12,
                                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                              ),
                                            ),
                                            const Spacer(),
                                            Text(
                                              '${recipe.outputQuantity} ${recipe.outputUnit}',
                                              style: const TextStyle(
                                                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                                                fontSize: 12,
                                              ),
                                            ),
                                          ],
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(
        onPressed: _showAddRecipeModal,
        backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
        shape: const CircleBorder(),
        child: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showAddRecipeModal() {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {

    );
  }
}

                final price = double.tryParse(_priceController.text) ?? 0;
                
                if (name.isNotEmpty && quantity > 0) {
                  final provider = context.read<RecipesProvider>();

                  await provider.addRecipe(

                    category: _selectedCategory,
                    sellingPrice: price,
                    outputQuantity: quantity,
                    outputUnit: 'g',
                  );
                  if (nav.canPop()) {

            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class RecipeSearchDelegate extends SearchDelegate<String?> {
  final List<RecipeEntity> recipes;
  static final List<String> _searchHistory = [];

  RecipeSearchDelegate(this.recipes);

  @override
  ThemeData appBarTheme(BuildContext context) {
    return ThemeData(
      appBarTheme: const AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        elevation: 0,
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: const InputDecorationTheme(
        hintStyle: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
        border: InputBorder.none,
      ),
      textTheme: const TextTheme(
        titleLarge: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Widget>? buildActions(BuildContext context) {
    return [
      if (query.isNotEmpty)
        IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.clear),
          onPressed: () {
            query = '';
          },
        ),
    ];
  }

  @override
  Widget? buildLeading(BuildContext context) {
    return IconButton(
      icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
      onPressed: () {
        close(context, null);
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget buildResults(BuildContext context) {
    if (query.isNotEmpty && !_searchHistory.contains(query)) {
      _searchHistory.insert(0, query);
      if (_searchHistory.length > 10) {
        _searchHistory.removeLast();
      }
    }
    return _buildList();
  }

  @override
  Widget buildSuggestions(BuildContext context) {
    if (query.isEmpty) {
      return Container(
        color: AppColors.background,
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            if (_searchHistory.isNotEmpty)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Text('common.search_history'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    TextButton(
                      onPressed: () {
                        _searchHistory.clear();
                        query = ''; // Triggers rebuild
                      },
                      child: Text('common.clear'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.blueAccent)),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            Expanded(
              child: ListView.builder(
                itemCount: _searchHistory.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  return ListTile(
                    leading: const Icon(Icons.history, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    title: Text(_searchHistory[index], style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                    onTap: () {
                      query = _searchHistory[index];
                      showResults(context);
                    },
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }
    return _buildList();
  }

  Widget _buildList() {
    final normalizedQuery = SearchUtils.normalize(query);
    
    final filteredRecipes = recipes.where((recipe) {
      final normalizedName = SearchUtils.normalize(recipe.name);
      final normalizedCategory = SearchUtils.normalize(recipe.category);
      return normalizedName.contains(normalizedQuery) || normalizedCategory.contains(normalizedQuery);
    }).toList();

    return Container(
      color: AppColors.background,
      child: filteredRecipes.isEmpty
          ? Center(child: Text('common.not_found'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)))
          : ListView.builder(
              itemCount: filteredRecipes.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final recipe = filteredRecipes[index];
                return ListTile(
                  title: Text(recipe.name, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                  subtitle: Text(recipe.category, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                  trailing: Text('${recipe.outputQuantity} ${recipe.outputUnit}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  onTap: () {
                    close(context, recipe.id);
                  },
                );
              },
            ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
