class RecipeDetailPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final RecipeEntity recipe;
  const RecipeDetailPage({super.key, required this.recipe});

  @override
  State<RecipeDetailPage> createState() => _RecipeDetailPageState();
}

class _RecipeDetailPageState extends State<RecipeDetailPage> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late TabController _tabController;
  late TextEditingController _techController;
  bool _isSavingTech = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabController = TabController(length: 2, vsync: this);
    _techController = TextEditingController(text: widget.recipe.description ?? '');
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabController.dispose();
    _techController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text(''),
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.more_vert),
            onPressed: () {},
          )
        ],
      ),
      body: Consumer<RecipesProvider>(
        builder: (context, provider, child) {
          final updatedRecipe = provider.recipes.firstWhere(
            (r) => r.id == widget.recipe.id, 
            orElse: () => widget.recipe
          );

          return Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // Header Initial & Label
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                child: Stack(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      height: 120,
                      width: double.infinity,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: AppColors.surfaceLight,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                        image: updatedRecipe.imageUrl != null && updatedRecipe.imageUrl!.isNotEmpty
                            ? DecorationImage(
                                image: NetworkImage(updatedRecipe.imageUrl!),
                                fit: BoxFit.cover,
                              )
                            : null,
                      ),
                      child: Stack(
                        fit: StackFit.expand,
                        children: [
                          if (updatedRecipe.imageUrl == null || updatedRecipe.imageUrl!.isEmpty)
                            Center(
                              child: Text(
                                updatedRecipe.name.isNotEmpty ? updatedRecipe.name[0].toUpperCase() : '?',
                                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 48, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                              ),
                            ),
                          if (provider.isRecipeUploading(widget.recipe.id))
                            Container(
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: Colors.black54,
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                              ),
                              child: const Center(
                                child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white),
                              ),
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    Positioned(
                      bottom: 16,
                      left: 16,
                      child: GestureDetector(
                        onTap: provider.isRecipeUploading(widget.recipe.id) ? null : () async {
                          final ImageSource? source = await showModalBottomSheet<ImageSource>(
                            context: context,
                            backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
                            builder: (context) => SafeArea(
                              child: Wrap(
                                children: [
                                  ListTile(
                                    leading: const Icon(Icons.camera_alt, color: Colors.white),
                                    title: Text('recipes.from_camera'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                                    onTap: () => Navigator.pop(context, ImageSource.camera),
                                  ),
                                  ListTile(
                                    leading: const Icon(Icons.photo_library, color: Colors.white),
                                    title: Text('recipes.from_gallery'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                                    onTap: () => Navigator.pop(context, ImageSource.gallery),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          );

                          if (source != null) {
                            final ImagePicker picker = ImagePicker();
                            final XFile? image = await picker.pickImage(source: source);
                            if (image != null && context.mounted) {
                              context.read<RecipesProvider>().uploadRecipeImage(widget.recipe.id, image.path);
                            }
                          }
                        },
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.black54,
                            shape: BoxShape.circle,
                          ),
                          child: const Icon(Icons.camera_alt, color: Colors.white, size: 20),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Positioned(
                      bottom: 16,
                      right: 16,
                      child: Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: AppColors.success,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          updatedRecipe.category,
                          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        ),
                      ),
                    )
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              // Title
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                child: Text(
                  updatedRecipe.name,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                ),
              ),
              
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              // 3 Stat Cards
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(child: _buildStatCard('recipes.cost_price'.tr(), '${updatedRecipe.costPrice.toInt()} ${'common.currency_sum'.tr()}', AppColors.textPrimary)),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(child: _buildStatCard('recipes.dish_yield'.tr(), '${updatedRecipe.outputQuantity.toInt()} ${updatedRecipe.outputUnit}', AppColors.textPrimary)),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(child: _buildStatCard('recipes.foodcost'.tr(), '${updatedRecipe.foodcostPercentage.toStringAsFixed(1)}%', AppColors.success)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              
              // Tabs
              TabBar(
                controller: _tabController,
                indicatorColor: AppColors.primary,
                labelColor: AppColors.primary,
                unselectedLabelColor: AppColors.textSecondary,
                tabs: [
                  Tab(text: 'recipes.tab_ingredients'.tr()),
                  Tab(text: 'recipes.tab_technology'.tr()),
                ],
              ),
              
              // Tab Content
              Expanded(
                child: TabBarView(
                  controller: _tabController,
                  children: [
                    _buildIngredientsTab(updatedRecipe),
                    _buildTechnologyTab(updatedRecipe),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(String label, String value, Color valueColor) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(value, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: valueColor)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildIngredientsTab(RecipeEntity updatedRecipe) {
    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      children: [
        // Table Header
        Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(flex: 3, child: Text('recipes.col_ingredient'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12))),
            Expanded(flex: 2, child: Text('recipes.col_weight'.tr(), textAlign: TextAlign.right, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12))),
            Expanded(flex: 2, child: Text('recipes.col_price'.tr(), textAlign: TextAlign.right, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12))),
            Expanded(flex: 2, child: Text('recipes.col_sum'.tr(), textAlign: TextAlign.right, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12))),
            const SizedBox(width: 36),
          ],
        ),
        const Divider(color: Colors.white10, height: 24),
        // Ingredients List
        ...updatedRecipe.items.map((ing) {
              String qtyStr = ing.quantity == ing.quantity.toInt() ? ing.quantity.toInt().toString() : ing.quantity.toStringAsFixed(3).replaceAll(RegExp(r'0*$'), '').replaceAll(RegExp(r'\.$'), '');
              String priceStr = ing.productPrice == ing.productPrice.toInt() ? ing.productPrice.toInt().toString() : ing.productPrice.toStringAsFixed(2);
              String sumStr = ing.sum == ing.sum.toInt() ? ing.sum.toInt().toString() : ing.sum.toStringAsFixed(2);

              return Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(flex: 3, child: Text(ing.productName, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary))),
                    Expanded(flex: 2, child: Text('$qtyStr ${ing.unit}', textAlign: TextAlign.right, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary))),
                    Expanded(flex: 2, child: Text(priceStr, textAlign: TextAlign.right, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary))),
                    Expanded(flex: 2, child: Text(sumStr, textAlign: TextAlign.right, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary))),
                    SizedBox(
                      width: 36,
                      child: IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline, size: 18, color: AppColors.error),
                        padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                        constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
                        onPressed: () async {
                          final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
                            context: context,
                            builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
                              backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
                              title: Text('common.confirm'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary)),
                              content: Text('${ing.productName} ${'inventory.delete_confirm'.tr()}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                              actions: [
                                TextButton(
                                  onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false),
                                  child: Text('common.cancel'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                                ),
                                TextButton(
                                  onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
                                  child: Text('common.delete'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          );
                          if (confirm == true && mounted) {
                            await context.read<RecipesProvider>().deleteRecipeItem(widget.recipe.id, ing.id);
                          }
                        },
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            }),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            // Add Button
            TextButton.icon(
              onPressed: () => _showAddIngredientModal(context, widget.recipe.id),
              icon: const Icon(Icons.add, color: AppColors.primary),
              label: Text('recipes.add_ingredient'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.primary)),
              style: TextButton.styleFrom(alignment: Alignment.centerLeft),
            ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTechnologyTab(RecipeEntity updatedRecipe) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Text(
            'recipes.tab_technology'.tr(),
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.surface,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
              ),
              child: TextField(
                controller: _techController,
                maxLines: null,
                expands: true,
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 14),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: 'recipes.tab_technology'.tr(),
                  hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  border: InputBorder.none,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            ),
            onPressed: _isSavingTech ? null : () async {
              setState(() => _isSavingTech = true);
              await context.read<RecipesProvider>().updateRecipeDescription(
                widget.recipe.id,
                _techController.text.trim(),
              );
              if (mounted) {
                setState(() => _isSavingTech = false);
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(
                    content: Text('common.success'.tr()),
                    backgroundColor: AppColors.success,
                  ),
                );
              }
            },
            child: _isSavingTech
                ? const SizedBox(height: 20, width: 20, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white))
                : Text('common.save'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showAddIngredientModal(BuildContext context, String recipeId) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        return AddIngredientModal(recipeId: recipeId);
      },
    );
  }
}

class AddIngredientModal extends StatefulWidget {
  final String recipeId;
  const AddIngredientModal({super.key, required this.recipeId});

  @override
  State<AddIngredientModal> createState() => _AddIngredientModalState();
}

class _AddIngredientModalState extends State<AddIngredientModal> {
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _quantityController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _priceController = TextEditingController();
  
  IngredientOption? _selectedItem;
  bool _isSaving = false;
  bool isLoading = true;
  String _searchQuery = '';
  List<IngredientOption> items = [];
  List<IngredientOption> filteredItems = [];

  double _totalPrice = 0.0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _quantityController.addListener(_calculateTotal);
    _priceController.addListener(_calculateTotal);
    _loadProducts();
  }

  Future<void> _loadProducts() async {
    if (!mounted) return;
    try {
      final provider = context.read<RecipesProvider>();
      final products = await provider.getIngredientOptions();
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          items = products;
          filteredItems = products;
          isLoading = false;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    _quantityController.dispose();
    _priceController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _selectItem(IngredientOption item) {
    setState(() {
      _selectedItem = item;
      _searchController.clear();
      _priceController.text = item.averageCost > 0 ? item.averageCost.toStringAsFixed(2) : '';
      _calculateTotal();
    });
  }

  void _calculateTotal() {
    final qty = double.tryParse(_quantityController.text) ?? 0.0;
    final price = double.tryParse(_priceController.text) ?? 0.0;
    setState(() {
      _totalPrice = qty * price;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    filteredItems = items.where((item) {
      return item.name.toLowerCase().contains(_searchQuery.toLowerCase());
    }).toList();

    return Container(
      height: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.85,
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
      ),
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(
        bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom + 24,
        left: 24,
        right: 24,
        top: 12,
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Center(
            child: Container(
              width: 40,
              height: 4,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white24,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
                child: const Icon(Icons.restaurant_menu, color: AppColors.primary),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Text(
                'recipes.new_ingredient'.tr(), 
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          
          if (_selectedItem == null) ...[
            // SEARCH FIELD
            TextField(
              controller: _searchController,
              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
              onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _searchQuery = val),
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                hintText: 'recipes.search_product'.tr(),
                hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                filled: true,
                fillColor: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
                enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: BorderSide.none, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
                focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            
            // LIST OF PRODUCTS
            Expanded(
              child: isLoading
                  ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: AppColors.primary))
                  : filteredItems.isEmpty
                      ? Center(
                          child: Text(
                            'recipes.product_not_found'.tr(),
                            style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 16),
                          ),
                        )
                      : ListView.separated(
                          itemCount: filteredItems.length,
                          separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const Divider(color: Colors.white10, height: 1),
                          itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                            final item = filteredItems[index];
                            return ListTile(
                              contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                              title: Text(item.name, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                              subtitle: Text('${'recipes.in_stock'.tr()}: ${item.currentQuantity} ${item.unit}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                              trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                              onTap: () => _selectItem(item),
                            );
                          },
                        ),
            ),
          ] else ...[
            // SELECTED ITEM DETAILS
            Expanded(
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: [
                                Text('recipes.selected'.tr(), style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
                                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                Text(_selectedItem!.name, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                                Text('${'recipes.in_stock'.tr()}: ${_selectedItem!.currentQuantity} ${_selectedItem!.unit}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14)),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          IconButton(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                            onPressed: () => setState(() => _selectedItem = null),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    
                    // Quantity Input
                    TextField(
                      controller: _quantityController,
                      keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(decimal: true),
                      style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'recipes.usage_amount'.tr(),
                        labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.scale_outlined, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        suffixText: _selectedItem!.unit,
                        suffixStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        filled: true,
                        fillColor: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
                        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: BorderSide.none, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
                        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    
                    // Price Input
                    TextField(
                      controller: _priceController,
                      keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(decimal: true),
                      style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        labelText: '${'recipes.price_per_unit'.tr()} (1 ${_selectedItem!.unit})',
                        labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.payments_outlined, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        suffixText: 'recipes.currency'.tr(),
                        suffixStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        filled: true,
                        fillColor: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
                        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: BorderSide.none, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
                        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
                      ),
                    ),
                    
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          Text('recipes.total_sum'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500)),
                          Text(
                            '${_totalPrice > 0 ? (_totalPrice == _totalPrice.toInt() ? _totalPrice.toInt() : _totalPrice.toStringAsFixed(2)) : 0} ${'recipes.currency'.tr()}',
                            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.primary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    
                    // Save Button
                    SizedBox(
                      width: double.infinity,
                      height: 56,
                      child: ElevatedButton(
                        onPressed: _isSaving ? null : () async {
                          final qty = double.tryParse(_quantityController.text) ?? 0;
                          final price = double.tryParse(_priceController.text) ?? 0;
                          
                          if (qty > 0) {
                            setState(() => _isSaving = true);
                            try {
                              final provider = context.read<RecipesProvider>();
                              await provider.addRecipeItem(
                                recipeId: widget.recipeId,
                                productId: _selectedItem!.id,
                                productName: _selectedItem!.name,
                                quantity: qty,
                                unit: _selectedItem!.unit,
                                productPrice: price,
                              );
                              
                              if (context.mounted) {
                                Navigator.pop(context);
                                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                                  SnackBar(
                                    content: Text('recipes.ingredient_added_success'.tr()),
                                    backgroundColor: AppColors.success,
                                    behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                                  )
                                );
                              }
                            } catch (e) {
                              if (context.mounted) {
                                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                                  SnackBar(
                                    content: Text('${'recipes.error_occurred'.tr()}: $e'),
                                    backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
                                    behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                                  )
                                );
                              }
                            } finally {
                              if (mounted) setState(() => _isSaving = false);
                            }
                          } else {
                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              SnackBar(
                                content: Text('recipes.enter_valid_amount'.tr()),
                                backgroundColor: Colors.orange,
                                behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                              )
                            );
                          }
                        },
                        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                          disabledBackgroundColor: AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
                          elevation: 0,
                        ),
                        child: _isSaving
                            ? const SizedBox(
                                width: 24,
                                height: 24,
                                child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2.5),
                              )
                            : Text('recipes.add'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\recipes\presentation\providers\recipes_provider.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/recipe_entity.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/recipe_item_entity.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/ingredient_option.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/recipes_repository.dart';
import '../../../../core/utils/unit_converter.dart';

class RecipesProvider extends ChangeNotifier {
  final RecipesRepository _repository = RecipesRepository();
  
  List<RecipeEntity> _recipes = [];
  List<RecipeEntity> get recipes => _recipes;
  
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;
  
  final Map<String, bool> _uploadingRecipes = {};
  bool isRecipeUploading(String id) => _uploadingRecipes[id] ?? false;
  
  String? _error;
  String? get error => _error;

  // For testing, mock data fallback
  final bool _useMockData = false;

  List<IngredientOption> _cachedIngredientOptions = [];
  bool _hasLoadedIngredients = false;

  Future<void> fetchRecipes({bool background = false}) async {
    if (!background && _recipes.isEmpty) {
      _isLoading = true;
      notifyListeners();
    }
    _error = null;
    
    try {
      final results = await Future.wait([
        _repository.getRecipes(),
        _repository.getIngredientOptions(),
      ]);
      
      List<RecipeEntity> fetchedRecipes = results[0] as List<RecipeEntity>;
      _cachedIngredientOptions = results[1] as List<IngredientOption>;
      _hasLoadedIngredients = true;
      
      // Inject the current dynamic price into the recipe items
      _recipes = fetchedRecipes.map((recipe) {
        final updatedItems = recipe.items.map((item) {
          try {
            final option = _cachedIngredientOptions.firstWhere((o) => o.id == item.productId);
            final factor = UnitConverter.convert(1.0, item.unit, option.unit);
            return RecipeItemEntity(
              id: item.id,
              recipeId: item.recipeId,
              productId: item.productId,
              productName: item.productName,
              quantity: item.quantity,
              unit: item.unit,
              productPrice: option.averageCost * factor,
            );
          } catch (_) {
            return item;
          }
        }).toList();
        
        return RecipeEntity(
          id: recipe.id,
          name: recipe.name,
          category: recipe.category,
          description: recipe.description,
          sellingPrice: recipe.sellingPrice,
          outputQuantity: recipe.outputQuantity,
          outputUnit: recipe.outputUnit,
          isActive: recipe.isActive,
          imageUrl: recipe.imageUrl,
          items: updatedItems,
        );
      }).toList();
      
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      if (_useMockData) {
         _recipes = _getMockRecipes();
      }
    }
    if (!background) {
      _isLoading = false;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<List<IngredientOption>> getIngredientOptions({bool forceRefresh = false}) async {
    if (_hasLoadedIngredients && !forceRefresh) {
      return _cachedIngredientOptions;
    }
    _cachedIngredientOptions = await _repository.getIngredientOptions();
    _hasLoadedIngredients = true;
    return _cachedIngredientOptions;
  }

  Future<void> addRecipe({
    required String name,
    required String category,
    required double sellingPrice,
    required double outputQuantity,
    required String outputUnit,
    String? imageUrl,
  }) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();
    
    try {
      final newRecipe = await _repository.addRecipe(
        name: name,
        category: category,
        sellingPrice: sellingPrice,
        outputQuantity: outputQuantity,
        outputUnit: outputUnit,
      );
      _recipes.add(newRecipe);
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
    }
    
    _isLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> addRecipeItem({
    required String recipeId,
    required String productId,
    required String productName,
    required double quantity,
    required String unit,
    required double productPrice,
  }) async {
    _isLoading = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();
    
    try {
      await _repository.addRecipeItem(
        recipeId: recipeId,
        productId: productId,
        quantity: quantity,
        unit: unit,
      );

      // Update local state
      final recipeIndex = _recipes.indexWhere((r) => r.id == recipeId);
      if (recipeIndex != -1) {
        final recipe = _recipes[recipeIndex];
        final newIngredient = RecipeItemEntity(
          id: 'temp_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
          recipeId: recipeId,
          productId: productId,
          productName: productName,
          quantity: quantity,
          unit: unit,
          productPrice: productPrice,
        );
        
        final updatedIngredients = List<RecipeItemEntity>.from(recipe.items)..add(newIngredient);
        
        _recipes[recipeIndex] = RecipeEntity(
          id: recipe.id,
          name: recipe.name,
          category: recipe.category,
          sellingPrice: recipe.sellingPrice,
          outputQuantity: recipe.outputQuantity,
          outputUnit: recipe.outputUnit,
          isActive: recipe.isActive,
          imageUrl: recipe.imageUrl,
          items: updatedIngredients,
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
    }
    
    _isLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  // Fallback data if DB is empty or disconnected
  List<RecipeEntity> _getMockRecipes() {
    return [
      RecipeEntity(
        id: '1',
        name: 'Ribay Steyk',
        category: 'Issiq taomlar',
        sellingPrice: 150000,
        outputQuantity: 350,
        outputUnit: 'g',
        isActive: true,
        items: [
          RecipeItemEntity(id: 'i1', recipeId: '1', productId: 'p1', productName: 'Go\'sht', quantity: 400, unit: 'g', productPrice: 90000 / 1000),
          RecipeItemEntity(id: 'i2', recipeId: '1', productId: 'p2', productName: 'Sariyog\'', quantity: 30, unit: 'g', productPrice: 40000 / 1000),
        ],
      ),
      RecipeEntity(
        id: '2',
        name: 'Sezar Salati',
        category: 'Salatlar',
        sellingPrice: 75000,
        outputQuantity: 250,
        outputUnit: 'g',
        isActive: true,
        items: [],
      ),
    ];
  }

  Future<void> uploadRecipeImage(String recipeId, String imagePath) async {
    _uploadingRecipes[recipeId] = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final imageUrl = await _repository.uploadRecipeImage(recipeId, imagePath);
      final recipeIndex = _recipes.indexWhere((r) => r.id == recipeId);
      if (recipeIndex != -1) {
        final r = _recipes[recipeIndex];
        _recipes[recipeIndex] = RecipeEntity(
          id: r.id, 
          name: r.name, 
          category: r.category, 
          description: r.description,
          sellingPrice: r.sellingPrice,
          outputQuantity: r.outputQuantity, 
          outputUnit: r.outputUnit, 
          isActive: r.isActive, 
          items: r.items, 
          imageUrl: imageUrl,
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
    }
    _uploadingRecipes[recipeId] = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> deleteRecipeItem(String recipeId, String itemId) async {
    try {
      await _repository.deleteRecipeItem(itemId);
      await fetchRecipes(background: true);
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> updateRecipeDescription(String recipeId, String description) async {
    try {
      await _repository.updateRecipeDescription(recipeId, description);
      await fetchRecipes(background: true);
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
}

// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\sales\data\models\sales_model.dart
// ==========================================

class SalesModel {
  final String id;
  final String restaurantId;
  final String? orderId;
  final String paymentMethod;
  final double amount;
  final DateTime date;

  SalesModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.restaurantId,
    this.orderId,
    required this.paymentMethod,
    required this.amount,
    required this.date,
  });

  factory SalesModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    final rawDate = json['date'] ?? json['created_at'];
    return SalesModel(
      id: json['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      restaurantId: json['restaurant_id']?.toString() ?? '',
      orderId: json['order_id']?.toString(),
      paymentMethod: json['payment_method']?.toString() ?? 'cash',
      amount: (json['total_amount'] ?? json['amount'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
      date: DateTime.tryParse(rawDate?.toString() ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\sales\data\repositories\sales_repository.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import '../../../../core/network/supabase_service.dart';
import '../models/sales_model.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/sales_summary.dart';

class SalesRepository {
  final SupabaseClient _client = SupabaseService.client;
  
  Future<SalesSummary> getTodaySalesSummary() async {
    try {
      final now = DateTime.now();
      final todayStart = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day).toIso8601String();
      final todayEnd = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 23, 59, 59).toIso8601String();
      
      final response = await _client
          .from('sales')
          .select('*')
          .eq('restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId)
          .eq('status', 'completed')
          .gte('date', todayStart)
          .lte('date', todayEnd);
          
      final List<SalesModel> todaySales = (response as List)
          .map((json) => SalesModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
          
      double totalRev = 0;
      for (var s in todaySales) {
        totalRev += s.amount;
      }

      int receipts = todaySales.length;
      double avg = receipts > 0 ? totalRev / receipts : 0;

      // Query top dishes
      final saleItemsResponse = await _client
          .from('sale_items')
          .select('quantity, total_price, recipes(name), sales!inner(restaurant_id, date, status)')
          .eq('sales.restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId)
          .gte('sales.date', todayStart)
          .lte('sales.date', todayEnd)
          .eq('sales.status', 'completed');

      Map<String, TopDish> dishMap = {};
      for (var row in (saleItemsResponse as List)) {
        final recipe = row['recipes'];
        if (recipe != null) {
          final name = recipe['name'] as String;
          final num rawQty = row['quantity'] ?? 0;
          final int qty = rawQty.toInt();
          final num rawSum = row['total_price'] ?? 0;
          final double sum = rawSum.toDouble();
          
          if (dishMap.containsKey(name)) {
            dishMap[name] = TopDish(
              name: name,
              portions: dishMap[name]!.portions + qty,
              sum: dishMap[name]!.sum + sum,
            );
          } else {
            dishMap[name] = TopDish(name: name, portions: qty, sum: sum);
          }
        }
      }

      final topDishesList = dishMap.values.toList()
        ..sort((a, b) => b.sum.compareTo(a.sum));
      
      final topDishes = topDishesList.take(5).toList();

      return SalesSummary(
        totalRevenue: totalRev,
        previousRevenue: totalRev * 0.9, // mock 10% growth
        totalReceipts: receipts,
        averageReceipt: avg,
        topDishes: topDishes,
        dailyRevenues: [totalRev],
      );
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error fetching sales: $e');
      return SalesSummary(
        totalRevenue: 0,
        previousRevenue: 0,
        totalReceipts: 0,
        averageReceipt: 0,
      );
    }
  }

  Future<SalesSummary> getSalesSummaryByRange(DateTime start, DateTime end) async {
    try {
      final rangeStart = DateTime(start.year, start.month, start.day).toIso8601String();
      final rangeEnd = DateTime(end.year, end.month, end.day, 23, 59, 59).toIso8601String();
      
      final response = await _client
          .from('sales')
          .select('*')
          .eq('restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId)
          .eq('status', 'completed')
          .gte('date', rangeStart)
          .lte('date', rangeEnd);
          
      final List<SalesModel> sales = (response as List)
          .map((json) => SalesModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
          
      double totalRev = 0;
      for (var s in sales) {
        totalRev += s.amount;
      }

      int receipts = sales.length;
      double avg = receipts > 0 ? totalRev / receipts : 0;

      final List<double> dailyRevs = List.filled(7, 0.0);
      for (var s in sales) {
        final diffDays = s.date.difference(DateTime(start.year, start.month, start.day)).inDays;
        if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays < 7) {
          dailyRevs[diffDays] += s.amount;
        } else {
          dailyRevs[s.date.weekday % 7] += s.amount;
        }
      }

      final saleItemsResponse = await _client
          .from('sale_items')
          .select('quantity, total_price, recipes(name), sales!inner(restaurant_id, date, status)')
          .eq('sales.restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId)
          .gte('sales.date', rangeStart)
          .lte('sales.date', rangeEnd)
          .eq('sales.status', 'completed');

      Map<String, TopDish> dishMap = {};
      for (var row in (saleItemsResponse as List)) {
        final recipe = row['recipes'];
        if (recipe != null) {
          final name = recipe['name'] as String;
          final num rawQty = row['quantity'] ?? 0;
          final int qty = rawQty.toInt();
          final num rawSum = row['total_price'] ?? 0;
          final double sum = rawSum.toDouble();
          
          if (dishMap.containsKey(name)) {
            dishMap[name] = TopDish(
              name: name,
              portions: dishMap[name]!.portions + qty,
              sum: dishMap[name]!.sum + sum,
            );
          } else {
            dishMap[name] = TopDish(name: name, portions: qty, sum: sum);
          }
        }
      }

      final topDishesList = dishMap.values.toList()
        ..sort((a, b) => b.sum.compareTo(a.sum));
      
      final topDishes = topDishesList.take(5).toList();

      return SalesSummary(
        totalRevenue: totalRev,
        previousRevenue: totalRev * 0.9,
        totalReceipts: receipts,
        averageReceipt: avg,
        topDishes: topDishes,
        dailyRevenues: dailyRevs,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error fetching sales by range: $e');
      return SalesSummary(
        totalRevenue: 0,
        previousRevenue: 0,
        totalReceipts: 0,
        averageReceipt: 0,
      );
    }
  }

  Future<bool> refundSale(String saleId, {String reason = 'Bekor qilindi'}) async {
    try {
      final response = await _client.rpc('process_pos_refund', params: {
        'p_sale_id': saleId,
        'p_reason': reason,
      });
      return response == true;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error refunding sale: $e');
      throw Exception('Chekni bekor qilishda xatolik: $e');
    }
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\sales\domain\entities\sales_summary.dart
// ==========================================

class TopDish {
  final String name;
  final int portions;
  final double sum;

  TopDish({
    required this.name,
    required this.portions,
    required this.sum,
  });
}

class SalesSummary {
  final double totalRevenue;
  final double previousRevenue;
  final int totalReceipts;
  final double averageReceipt;
  final List<TopDish> topDishes;
  final List<double> dailyRevenues;

  SalesSummary({
    required this.totalRevenue,
    required this.previousRevenue,
    required this.totalReceipts,
    required this.averageReceipt,
    this.topDishes = const [],
    this.dailyRevenues = const [],
  });

  double get percentageChange {
    if (previousRevenue == 0) return 100.0;
    return ((totalRevenue - previousRevenue) / previousRevenue) * 100;
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\sales\presentation\pages\sales_page.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../providers/sales_provider.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

class SalesPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const SalesPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<SalesPage> createState() => _SalesPageState();
}

class _SalesPageState extends State<SalesPage> {
  DateTimeRange? _dateRange;

  Future<void> _selectDateRange() async {
    final DateTimeRange? picked = await showDateRangePicker(
      context: context,
      firstDate: DateTime(2020),
      lastDate: DateTime.now(),
      initialDateRange: _dateRange,
      builder: (context, child) {
        return Theme(
          data: ThemeData.dark().copyWith(
            colorScheme: const ColorScheme.dark(
              primary: Colors.blueAccent,
              onPrimary: Colors.white,
              surface: AppColors.surface,
              onSurface: AppColors.textPrimary,
            ),
          ),
          child: child!,
        );
      },
    );
    if (picked != null && picked != _dateRange) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _dateRange = picked;
      });
      context.read<SalesProvider>().fetchSalesByRange(picked.start, picked.end);
    }
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final now = DateTime.now();
    _dateRange = DateTimeRange(
      start: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 6)),
      end: now,
    );
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      context.read<SalesProvider>().fetchSalesByRange(_dateRange!.start, _dateRange!.end);
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.background,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
        elevation: 0,
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
        title: Text('sales.title'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.more_vert, color: AppColors.textPrimary),
            onPressed: () {},
          )
        ],
      ),
      body: _buildSalesContent(),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSalesContent() {
    return Consumer<SalesProvider>(
      builder: (context, provider, child) {
        if (provider.isLoading) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        }

        if (provider.error != null) {
          return Center(child: Text(provider.error!, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)));
        }

        final summary = provider.summary;
        if (summary == null) {
          return Center(child: Text("common.no_data".tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)));
        }

        final bool isPositive = summary.percentageChange >= 0;
        final String sign = isPositive ? '+' : '';

        return ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          children: [
        // Date Range Picker
        InkWell(
          onTap: _selectDateRange,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          child: Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.surface,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Text(
                  _dateRange == null 
                    ? '' 
                    : '${DateFormat('d MMM').format(_dateRange!.start)} - ${DateFormat('d MMM yyyy').format(_dateRange!.end)}', 
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 14)
                ),
                const Icon(Icons.calendar_today, color: AppColors.textSecondary, size: 20),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        
        // Main Revenue Card
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.surface,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
          ),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                'sales.revenue'.tr(),
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      NumberFormat.currency(locale: 'ru', symbol: 'common.currency_sum'.tr(), decimalDigits: 0).format(summary.totalRevenue),
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                        fontSize: 22,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                      maxLines: 1,
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: (isPositive ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error).withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        Icon(isPositive ? Icons.arrow_upward : Icons.arrow_downward, 
                             color: isPositive ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error, 
                             size: 14),
                        const SizedBox(width: 4),
                        Text('$sign${summary.percentageChange.toStringAsFixed(1)}%', 
                             style: TextStyle(color: isPositive ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error, 
                                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, 
                                              fontSize: 12)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              const Divider(color: Colors.white10),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text('sales.receipts'.tr(), maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                        Text('${summary.totalReceipts}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Container(width: 1, height: 30, color: Colors.white10),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text('sales.avg_receipt'.tr(), maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                        Text(
                          NumberFormat.currency(locale: 'ru', symbol: 'common.currency_sum'.tr(), decimalDigits: 0).format(summary.averageReceipt), 
                          maxLines: 1,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              )
            ],
          ),
        ),
        
        const SizedBox(height: 24),

        // Chart Placeholder (matches Screen 8)
        Container(
          height: 180,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.surface,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
          ),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                  children: [
                    for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++)
                      Builder(
                        builder: (context) {
                          final double amount = (i < summary.dailyRevenues.length)
                              ? summary.dailyRevenues[i]
                              : 0.0;
                          final double maxRev = summary.dailyRevenues.fold(0.0, (m, v) => v > m ? v : m);
                          final double height = maxRev > 0
                              ? ((amount / maxRev) * 100).clamp(6.0, 110.0)
                              : 10.0;
                          return Container(
                            width: 24,
                            height: height,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: amount > 0 ? Colors.blueAccent : Colors.white24,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                children: [
                  Text('sales.day_mon'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
                  Text('sales.day_tue'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
                  Text('sales.day_wed'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
                  Text('sales.day_thu'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
                  Text('sales.day_fri'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
                  Text('sales.day_sat'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
                  Text('sales.day_sun'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        
        const SizedBox(height: 24),
        
        // Top Dishes
        Text(
          'sales.top_dishes'.tr(),
          style: const TextStyle(
            color: AppColors.textPrimary,
            fontSize: 18,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        ...summary.topDishes.map((d) => _buildTopDish(d.name, d.portions, d.sum)),

        const SizedBox(height: 24),
        OutlinedButton.icon(
          onPressed: () {},
          icon: const Icon(Icons.download),
          label: Text('sales.download_report'.tr()),
          style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white24),
            foregroundColor: AppColors.textPrimary,
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
          ),
        )
      ],
    );
    });
  }


  Widget _buildTopDish(String name, int portions, double sum) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(name, maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text('$portions ${'sales.portions'.tr()}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Text(
            '${sum.toInt()} ${'common.currency_sum'.tr()}',
            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.primary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\sales\presentation\providers\sales_provider.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/sales_summary.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/sales_repository.dart';

class SalesProvider extends ChangeNotifier {
  final SalesRepository _repository = SalesRepository();
  
  SalesSummary? _summary;
  SalesSummary? get summary => _summary;
  
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;
  
  String? _error;
  String? get error => _error;

  final bool _useMockData = false;

  Future<void> fetchTodaySales({bool background = false}) async {
    if (!background && _summary == null) {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
      notifyListeners();
    }
    
    try {
      final data = await _repository.getTodaySalesSummary();
      if (data.totalReceipts == 0 && _useMockData) {
        _summary = _getMockSummary();
      } else {
        _summary = data;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      if (_useMockData) {
         _summary = _getMockSummary();
      }
    }
    if (_isLoading) {
      _isLoading = false;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> fetchSalesByRange(DateTime start, DateTime end, {bool background = false}) async {
    if (!background) {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
      notifyListeners();
    }

    try {
      final data = await _repository.getSalesSummaryByRange(start, end);
      _summary = data;
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
    } finally {
      if (_isLoading) {
        _isLoading = false;
      }
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  SalesSummary _getMockSummary() {
    return SalesSummary(
      totalRevenue: 1250000,
      previousRevenue: 1086956, // to get +15%
      totalReceipts: 45,
      averageReceipt: 27000,
      topDishes: [
        TopDish(name: 'Pasta Karbonara', portions: 15, sum: 225000),
        TopDish(name: 'Steyk Ribay', portions: 8, sum: 960000),
        TopDish(name: 'Salat Sezar', portions: 12, sum: 144000),
      ]
    );
  }
}

// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\settings\presentation\providers\settings_provider.dart
// ==========================================

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import '../../../../core/session/session_manager.dart';
import '../../../../core/audit/audit_service.dart';

class SettingsProvider with ChangeNotifier {
  static final SettingsProvider _instance = SettingsProvider._internal();
  factory SettingsProvider() => _instance;
  SettingsProvider._internal() {
    init();
  }

  bool _notificationsEnabled = true;
  bool get notificationsEnabled => _notificationsEnabled;

  final List<String> _customCategories = [];
  final List<Map<String, String>> _customUnits = [];

  List<String> get categories => [
        'settings.cat_meat'.tr(),
        'settings.cat_vegetables'.tr(),
        'settings.cat_dairy'.tr(),
        'settings.cat_dry'.tr(),
        'settings.cat_oils'.tr(),
        'settings.cat_drinks'.tr(),
        'settings.cat_bakery'.tr(),
        'settings.cat_spices'.tr(),
        ..._customCategories,
      ];

  List<Map<String, String>> get units => [
        {'name': 'settings.unit_kg'.tr(), 'symbol': 'kg'},
        {'name': 'settings.unit_liter'.tr(), 'symbol': 'l'},
        {'name': 'settings.unit_piece'.tr(), 'symbol': 'dona'},
        {'name': 'settings.unit_gram'.tr(), 'symbol': 'g'},
        {'name': 'settings.unit_ml'.tr(), 'symbol': 'ml'},
        {'name': 'settings.unit_portion'.tr(), 'symbol': 'por'},
        {'name': 'settings.unit_box'.tr(), 'symbol': 'quti'},
        ..._customUnits,
      ];

  Future<void> init() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final savedCats = prefs.getStringList('chefcontrol_custom_categories');
      if (savedCats != null && savedCats.isNotEmpty) {
        _customCategories.clear();
        _customCategories.addAll(savedCats);
      }

      final savedUnits = prefs.getString('chefcontrol_custom_units');
      if (savedUnits != null) {
        final List<dynamic> list = jsonDecode(savedUnits);
        _customUnits.clear();
        for (var item in list) {
          if (item is Map) {
            _customUnits.add({
              'name': item['name']?.toString() ?? '',
              'symbol': item['symbol']?.toString() ?? '',
            });
          }
        }
      }
      notifyListeners();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error loading settings from prefs: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveCategories() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await prefs.setStringList('chefcontrol_custom_categories', _customCategories);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error saving custom categories: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveUnits() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await prefs.setString('chefcontrol_custom_units', jsonEncode(_customUnits));
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error saving custom units: $e');
    }
  }

  void toggleNotifications(bool value) {
    _notificationsEnabled = value;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  bool updateProfile({
    required String name,
    required String restaurantName,
  }) {
    final cleanName = name.trim();
    final cleanRestName = restaurantName.trim();

    if (cleanName.isEmpty || cleanRestName.isEmpty) {
      return false;
    }

    SessionManager().updateBossProfile(
      name: cleanName,
      restaurantName: cleanRestName,
    );

    AuditService().logAction(
      department: 'settings',
      action: 'Restoran profili yangilandi',
      details: 'Restoran nomi: "$cleanRestName", Egasi: "$cleanName".',
    );

    notifyListeners();
    return true;
  }

  bool addCategory(String categoryName) {
    final cleanName = categoryName.trim();
    if (cleanName.isEmpty || categories.contains(cleanName)) {
      return false;
    }

    _customCategories.add(cleanName);
    _saveCategories();

    AuditService().logAction(
      department: 'settings',
      action: 'Yangi kategoriya qo`shildi',
      details: 'Kategoriya nomi: "$cleanName".',
    );

    notifyListeners();
    return true;
  }

  bool addUnit({
    required String name,
    required String symbol,
  }) {
    final cleanName = name.trim();
    final cleanSymbol = symbol.trim();

    if (cleanName.isEmpty || cleanSymbol.isEmpty) {
      return false;
    }

    final exists = units.any((u) => u['name'] == cleanName || u['symbol'] == cleanSymbol);
    if (exists) {
      return false;
    }

    _customUnits.add({'name': cleanName, 'symbol': cleanSymbol});
    _saveUnits();

    AuditService().logAction(
      department: 'settings',
      action: 'Yangi o`lchov birligi qo`shildi',
      details: 'Birlik: $cleanName ($cleanSymbol).',
    );

    notifyListeners();
    return true;
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\settings\presentation\widgets\categories_sheet.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../providers/settings_provider.dart';

class CategoriesSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  const CategoriesSheet({super.key});

  static Future<void> show(BuildContext context) {
    return showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (_) => const CategoriesSheet(),
    );
  }

  void _showAddDialog(BuildContext context) {
    final controller = TextEditingController();

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) {
        return AlertDialog(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
          title: Text(
            'settings.add_category'.tr(),
            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16),
          ),
          content: TextField(
            controller: controller,
            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              hintText: 'settings.category_name'.tr(),
              hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10),
              ),
              focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
              child: Text('common.cancel'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () {
                final success = SettingsProvider().addCategory(controller.text);
                if (success) {
                  Navigator.pop(ctx);
                } else {
                  Navigator.pop(ctx);
                }
              },
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent),
              child: Text('common.save'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ListenableBuilder(
      listenable: SettingsProvider(),
      builder: (context, _) {
        final categories = SettingsProvider().categories;

        return SafeArea(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      'settings.categories'.tr(),
                      style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                    ),
                    IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.blueAccent),
                      tooltip: 'settings.add_category'.tr(),
                      onPressed: () => _showAddDialog(context),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Wrap(
                  spacing: 8,
                  runSpacing: 8,
                  children: categories.map((cat) {
                    return Chip(
                      label: Text(cat),
                      backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceLight,
                      labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 12),
                      side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10),
                    );
                  }).toList(),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\settings\presentation\widgets\language_dialog.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';

class LanguageDialog extends StatelessWidget {
  const LanguageDialog({super.key});

  static Future<void> show(BuildContext context) {
    return showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => const LanguageDialog(),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
      title: Text('settings.select_language'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16)),
      content: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          _buildOption(context, 'settings.lang_uz_latin'.tr(), const Locale('uz')),
          _buildOption(context, 'settings.lang_uz_cyrl'.tr(), const Locale('uz', 'Cyrl')),
          _buildOption(context, 'settings.lang_ru'.tr(), const Locale('ru')),
          _buildOption(context, 'settings.lang_en'.tr(), const Locale('en')),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildOption(BuildContext context, String title, Locale locale) {
    final isSelected = context.locale == locale;

    return ListTile(
      title: Text(
        title,
        style: TextStyle(
          color: isSelected ? Colors.blueAccent : Colors.white,
          fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
        ),
      ),
      trailing: isSelected ? const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.blueAccent) : null,
      onTap: () async {
        await context.setLocale(locale);
        if (context.mounted) {
          Navigator.pop(context);
        }
      },
    );
  }
}
