// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\settings\presentation\widgets\profile_edit_sheet.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../../core/session/session_manager.dart';
import '../providers/settings_provider.dart';

class ProfileEditSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  const ProfileEditSheet({super.key});

  static Future<void> show(BuildContext context) {
    return showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (_) => const ProfileEditSheet(),
    );
  }

  @override
  State<ProfileEditSheet> createState() => _ProfileEditSheetState();
}

class _ProfileEditSheetState extends State<ProfileEditSheet> {
  late final TextEditingController _nameController;
  late final TextEditingController _restNameController;
  late final TextEditingController _phoneController;
  String _selectedCurrency = 'UZS';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final boss = SessionManager().bossProfile;
    _nameController = TextEditingController(text: boss.name);
    _restNameController = TextEditingController(text: boss.restaurantName);
    _phoneController = TextEditingController(text: '+998 90 123 45 67');
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _restNameController.dispose();
    _phoneController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onSave() {
    final success = SettingsProvider().updateProfile(
      name: _nameController.text,
      restaurantName: _restNameController.text,
    );

    if (success) {
      Navigator.pop(context);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('settings.save_success'.tr()),
          backgroundColor: Colors.green,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(
        bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom + 20,
        left: 20,
        right: 20,
        top: 20,
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(
                'settings.edit_profile'.tr(),
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                  fontSize: 18,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          TextField(
            controller: _restNameController,
            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              labelText: 'settings.restaurant_name'.tr(),
              labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.storefront, color: Colors.deepOrange),
              enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10),
              ),
              focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          TextField(
            controller: _nameController,
            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              labelText: 'settings.owner_name'.tr(),
              labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.person_outline, color: Colors.blueAccent),
              enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10),
              ),
              focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          TextField(
            controller: _phoneController,
            keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              labelText: 'settings.phone'.tr(),
              labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.phone_outlined, color: Colors.green),
              enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10),
              ),
              focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Text(
            'settings.currency'.tr(),
            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(
            children: [
              ChoiceChip(
                label: Text('settings.currency_uzs'.tr()),
                selected: _selectedCurrency == 'UZS',
                selectedColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceLight,
                labelStyle: TextStyle(
                  color: _selectedCurrency == 'UZS' ? Colors.white : AppColors.textSecondary,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
                onSelected: (selected) {
                  if (selected) {
                    setState(() => _selectedCurrency = 'UZS');
                  }
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              ChoiceChip(
                label: Text('settings.currency_usd'.tr()),
                selected: _selectedCurrency == 'USD',
                selectedColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                backgroundColor: AppColors.surfaceLight,
                labelStyle: TextStyle(
                  color: _selectedCurrency == 'USD' ? Colors.white : AppColors.textSecondary,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
                onSelected: (selected) {
                  if (selected) {
                    setState(() => _selectedCurrency = 'USD');
                  }
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            height: 50,
            child: ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: _onSave,
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
              ),
              child: Text(
                'common.save'.tr(),
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.white),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class SystemStatusSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  const SystemStatusSheet({super.key});

  static Future<void> show(BuildContext context) {
    return showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (_) => const SystemStatusSheet(),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final session = SessionManager();
    final auditCount = AuditService().entries.length;

    return SafeArea(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              'settings.system_status'.tr(),
              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            _buildStatusRow(
              Icons.cloud_done,
              Colors.green,
              'settings.cloud_database'.tr(),
              'settings.db_connected'.tr(),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _buildStatusRow(
              Icons.security,
              Colors.blueAccent,
              'staff.tab_audit'.tr(),
              'settings.audit_records_saved'.tr(args: [auditCount.toString()]),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _buildStatusRow(
              Icons.phone_android,
              Colors.amber,
              'settings.app_version_title'.tr(),
              'settings.version_prod'.tr(),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _buildStatusRow(
              Icons.lock_outline,
              Colors.tealAccent,
              'settings.restaurant_id'.tr(),
              session.bossProfile.restaurantId,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatusRow(IconData icon, Color color, String title, String value) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.background,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Icon(icon, color: color, size: 22),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(title, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 11)),
                const SizedBox(height: 2),
                Text(value, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\settings\presentation\widgets\units_sheet.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../providers/settings_provider.dart';

class UnitsSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  const UnitsSheet({super.key});

  static Future<void> show(BuildContext context) {
    return showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (_) => const UnitsSheet(),
    );
  }

  void _showAddDialog(BuildContext context) {
    final nameCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final symbolCtrl = TextEditingController();

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) {
        return AlertDialog(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
          title: Text('settings.add_unit'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16)),
          content: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              TextField(
                controller: nameCtrl,
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: 'settings.unit_name'.tr(),
                  hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10),
                  ),
                  focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              TextField(
                controller: symbolCtrl,
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: 'settings.unit_symbol'.tr(),
                  hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10),
                  ),
                  focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
              child: Text('common.cancel'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () {
                final success = SettingsProvider().addUnit(
                  name: nameCtrl.text,
                  symbol: symbolCtrl.text,
                );
                if (success) {
                  Navigator.pop(ctx);
                } else {
                  Navigator.pop(ctx);
                }
              },
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent),
              child: Text('common.save'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ListenableBuilder(
      listenable: SettingsProvider(),
      builder: (context, _) {
        final units = SettingsProvider().units;

        return SafeArea(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      'settings.units'.tr(),
                      style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                    ),
                    IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.blueAccent),
                      tooltip: 'settings.add_unit'.tr(),
                      onPressed: () => _showAddDialog(context),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                ...units.map((unit) {
                  return Container(
                    margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.background,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                      border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          unit['name'] ?? '',
                          style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                        ),
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            unit['symbol'] ?? '',
                            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.blueAccent, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  );
                }),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

  @override

                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            emp.name,
                            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            '${emp.id} â€¢ ${emp.roleName}',
                            style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 11),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: emp.status == 'active'
                            ? Colors.green.withValues(alpha: 0.15)
                            : Colors.amber.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        emp.status == 'active' ? 'settings.status_active'.tr() : 'settings.status_pending'.tr(),
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: emp.status == 'active' ? Colors.green : Colors.amber,
                          fontSize: 10,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            }),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            if (onOpenStaff != null)
              SizedBox(
                width: double.infinity,
                child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: () {
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    onOpenStaff!();
                  },
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.launch, size: 16),
                  label: Text('staff.title'.tr()),

      ),
    );

}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\staff\data\models\staff_model.dart
// ==========================================

class StaffModel {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String role;
  final bool isActive;

  StaffModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.role,
    required this.isActive,
  });

  factory StaffModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return StaffModel(
      id: json['id'],
      name: json['name'] ?? '',
      role: json['role'] ?? '',
      isActive: json['is_active'] ?? true,
    );
  }
}

class ShiftModel {
  final String id;
  final String userId;
  final DateTime startTime;
  final DateTime? endTime;
  final String status;
  final StaffModel? staff;

  ShiftModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.userId,
    required this.startTime,
    this.endTime,
    required this.status,
    this.staff,
  });

  factory ShiftModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    StaffModel? staff;
    if (json['users'] != null) {
      staff = StaffModel.fromJson(json['users']);
    }

    return ShiftModel(
      id: json['id'],
      userId: json['user_id'],
      startTime: DateTime.tryParse(json['start_time']?.toString() ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
      endTime: json['end_time'] != null ? DateTime.tryParse(json['end_time']?.toString() ?? '') : null,
      status: json['status'] ?? 'active',
      staff: staff,
    );
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\staff\data\repositories\staff_repository.dart
// ==========================================

import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
import '../../../../core/network/supabase_service.dart';
import '../models/staff_model.dart';

class StaffRepository {
  final SupabaseClient _client = SupabaseService.client;
  
  Future<List<ShiftModel>> getTodayShifts() async {
    try {
      final today = DateTime.now();
      final startOfDay = DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day).toIso8601String();
      final endOfDay = DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day, 23, 59, 59).toIso8601String();
      
      final response = await _client
          .from('shifts')
          .select('''
            *,
            users (id, name, role, is_active)
          ''')
          .eq('restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId)
          .gte('start_time', startOfDay)
          .lte('start_time', endOfDay);
          
      return (response as List)
          .map((json) => ShiftModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } catch (e) {
      // Error fetching shifts: $e
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<List<StaffModel>> getStaffList() async {
    try {
      final response = await _client
          .from('users')
          .select()
          .eq('restaurant_id', SupabaseService.currentRestaurantId);
          
      return (response as List)
          .map((json) => StaffModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } catch (e) {
      // Error fetching staff: $e
      return [];
    }
  }
}

class EmployeeShift {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String roleKey; // translation key for role
  final String startTime;
  final String endTime;

  EmployeeShift({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.roleKey,
    required this.startTime,
    required this.endTime,
  });
}

  // TAB 2: Schedule / Shifts
  Widget _buildScheduleTab() {
    return Consumer<StaffProvider>(
      builder: (context, provider, child) {
        if (provider.isLoading) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        }
        if (provider.error != null) {
          return Center(child: Text(provider.error!, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error)));
        }

        final shifts = provider.todayShifts;
        if (shifts.isEmpty) {
          return Center(child: Text('staff.no_active_shifts'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary)));
        }

        return ListView.builder(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
          itemCount: shifts.length,
          itemBuilder: (context, index) {
            final shift = shifts[index];
            final name = shift.staff?.name ?? 'staff.unknown'.tr();
            final role = shift.staff?.role ?? 'staff.employee'.tr();

            return Container(
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.surface,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    width: 44,
                    height: 44,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                    child: const Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.blueAccent),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 16),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          name,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            fontSize: 15,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                        Text(
                          role,
                          style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: shift.status == 'active' ? AppColors.success : AppColors.surfaceLight,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      shift.status == 'active' ? 'staff.on_shift'.tr() : 'staff.day_off'.tr(),
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: shift.status == 'active' ? Colors.white : AppColors.textSecondary,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  // TAB 3: Employee Activity / Audit Log with ID Search
  Widget _buildAuditTab() {
    return ListenableBuilder(
      listenable: AuditService(),
      builder: (context, _) {
        final auditService = AuditService();
        final session = SessionManager();
        final events = auditService.getTodayEvents(query: _selectedEmployeeQuery);
        final employees = session.employees;

        return Column(
          children: [
            // Search Bar by Employee ID
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
              child: TextField(
                controller: _searchController,
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 14),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: 'staff.search_by_id'.tr(),
                  hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13),
                  prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, color: Colors.blueAccent, size: 20),
                  suffixIcon: _searchController.text.isNotEmpty
                      ? IconButton(
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.clear, color: AppColors.textSecondary, size: 18),
                          onPressed: () {
                            setState(() {
                              _searchController.clear();
                              _selectedEmployeeQuery = '';
                            });
                          },
                        )
                      : null,
                  filled: true,
                  fillColor: AppColors.surface,
                  contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 12),
                  enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10),
                  ),
                  focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent),
                  ),
                ),
                onChanged: (val) {
                  setState(() {
                    _selectedEmployeeQuery = val;
                  });
                },
              ),
            ),

            // Quick Filter Chips by Employee ID
            SingleChildScrollView(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 4),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  ChoiceChip(
                    label: Text('common.all'.tr()),
                    selected: _selectedEmployeeQuery.isEmpty,
                    onSelected: (selected) {
                      setState(() {
                        _selectedEmployeeQuery = '';
                        _searchController.clear();
                      });
                    },
                    selectedColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                    backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
                    labelStyle: TextStyle(
                      color: _selectedEmployeeQuery.isEmpty ? Colors.white : AppColors.textSecondary,
                      fontSize: 12,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  ...employees.map((emp) {
                    final isSelected = _selectedEmployeeQuery.toLowerCase() == emp.id.toLowerCase();
                    return Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8),
                      child: ChoiceChip(
                        label: Text('${emp.id} (${emp.name.split(" ").first})'),
                        selected: isSelected,
                        onSelected: (selected) {
                          setState(() {
                            _selectedEmployeeQuery = selected ? emp.id : '';
                            _searchController.text = selected ? emp.id : '';
                          });
                        },
                        selectedColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                        backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
                        labelStyle: TextStyle(
                          color: isSelected ? Colors.white : AppColors.textSecondary,
                          fontSize: 12,
                          fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  }),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),

            // Timeline List of Activities
            Expanded(
              child: events.isEmpty
                  ? Center(
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
                        child: Column(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                          children: [
                            const Icon(Icons.history_toggle_off, size: 54, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                            const SizedBox(height: 12),
                            Text(
                              'staff.no_activity_today'.tr(),
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    )
                  : ListView.builder(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
                      itemCount: events.length,
                      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                        final item = events[index];
                        final timeStr =
                            '${item.timestamp.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${item.timestamp.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

                        Color deptColor = Colors.blueAccent;
                        IconData deptIcon = Icons.inventory_2;
                        switch (item.department) {
                          case 'warehouse':
                            deptColor = Colors.blueAccent;
                            deptIcon = Icons.inventory_2;
                            break;
                          case 'pos':
                            deptColor = Colors.deepPurpleAccent;
                            deptIcon = Icons.point_of_sale;
                            break;
                          case 'purchases':
                            deptColor = Colors.green;
                            deptIcon = Icons.shopping_cart;
                            break;
                          case 'kitchen':
                            deptColor = Colors.deepOrange;
                            deptIcon = Icons.soup_kitchen;
                            break;
                          default:
                            deptColor = Colors.teal;
                            deptIcon = Icons.bolt;
                            break;
                        }

                        return Container(
                          margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: AppColors.surface,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                            border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                          ),
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Row(
                                children: [
                                  Container(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: deptColor.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                                    ),
                                    child: Icon(deptIcon, color: deptColor, size: 16),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                                  Expanded(
                                    child: Text(
                                      item.action,
                                      style: const TextStyle(
                                        color: AppColors.textPrimary,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                        fontSize: 14,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  Container(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: Colors.blueAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                                    ),
                                    child: Text(
                                      item.employeeId,
                                      style: const TextStyle(
                                        color: Colors.blueAccent,
                                        fontSize: 11,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                  Text(
                                    timeStr,
                                    style: const TextStyle(
                                      color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                                      fontSize: 12,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 8),
                              Text(
                                item.details,
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                                  fontSize: 13,
                                  height: 1.3,
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 6),
                              Text(
                                item.employeeName,
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: AppColors.textSecondary.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                                  fontSize: 11,
                                  fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                    ),
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  // Modal: Assign Department to Employee
  void _showAssignDepartmentModal(BuildContext context, SessionManager session, EmployeeProfile emp) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        return SafeArea(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'staff.select_dept'.tr(),
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  '${emp.name} (${emp.id})',
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.blueAccent, fontSize: 13),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                _buildDepartmentOption(context, session, emp, Department.warehouse, 'staff.dept_warehouse'.tr(), Icons.inventory_2, Colors.blueAccent),
                _buildDepartmentOption(context, session, emp, Department.pos, 'staff.dept_pos'.tr(), Icons.point_of_sale, Colors.deepPurpleAccent),
                _buildDepartmentOption(context, session, emp, Department.purchases, 'staff.dept_purchases'.tr(), Icons.shopping_cart, Colors.green),
                _buildDepartmentOption(context, session, emp, Department.kitchen, 'staff.dept_kitchen'.tr(), Icons.soup_kitchen, Colors.deepOrange),
                const Divider(color: Colors.white10),
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.redAccent),
                  title: Text(
                    'staff.dept_unassign'.tr(),
                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.redAccent, fontSize: 14),
                  ),
                  onTap: () {
                    session.assignDepartment(emp.id, Department.none);
                    AuditService().logAction(
                      department: 'staff',
                      action: 'Bo`lim bekor qilindi',
                      details: '${emp.name} (${emp.id}) ning bo`limi bekor qilindi.',
                    );
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDepartmentOption(
    BuildContext context,
    SessionManager session,
    EmployeeProfile emp,
    Department dept,
    String label,
    IconData icon,
    Color color,
  ) {
    final isSelected = emp.assignedDepartment == dept;

    return ListTile(
      leading: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
        ),
        child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 20),
      ),
      title: Text(
        label,
        style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
      ),
      trailing: isSelected ? const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.blueAccent) : null,
      onTap: () {
        session.assignDepartment(emp.id, dept);
        AuditService().logAction(
          department: 'staff',
          action: 'Bo`lim biriktirildi',
          details: '${emp.name} (${emp.id}) ga $label bo`limi biriktirildi.',
        );
        Navigator.pop(context);
      },
    );
  }

  // Modal: Show Invite QR Code and Link
  void _showInviteQrModal(BuildContext context, [EmployeeProfile? targetEmp]) {
    final session = SessionManager();
    final emp = targetEmp ??
        session.employees.firstWhere(
          (e) => e.status == 'waiting',
          orElse: () => session.employees.last,
        );
    final inviteLink = session.getInviteLink(emp.id);

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        return SafeArea(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'staff.invite_title'.tr(),
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  'staff.invite_desc'.tr(),
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),

                // Visual QR Card
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      QrImageView(
                        data: inviteLink,
                        size: 160,
                        version: QrVersions.auto,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Text(
                        session.bossProfile.restaurantName,
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black87, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text(
                        '${emp.name} â€¢ ${emp.id}',
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black87, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),

                // Link container
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: AppColors.background,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          inviteLink,
                          maxLines: 1,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.blueAccent, fontSize: 12),
                        ),
                      ),
                      IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.copy, size: 18, color: Colors.white70),
                        onPressed: () {
                          Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: inviteLink));
                          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                            SnackBar(
                              content: Text('staff.link_copied'.tr()),
                              backgroundColor: Colors.green,
                              duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                SizedBox(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  height: 48,
                  child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () {
                      Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: inviteLink));
                      Navigator.pop(context);
                      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                        SnackBar(
                          content: Text('staff.link_copied'.tr()),
                          backgroundColor: Colors.green,
                          duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.copy, size: 18),
                    label: Text('staff.copy_link'.tr()),
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _showAddStaffModal() {
    final nameController = TextEditingController();

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: AppColors.surface,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (context) {
        return Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.only(
            bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom,
            left: 20,
            right: 20,
            top: 20,
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                'staff.add_employee'.tr(),
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              TextField(
                controller: nameController,
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'staff.name'.tr(),
                  labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                  focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.blueAccent),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              SizedBox(
                width: double.infinity,
                height: 50,
                child: ElevatedButton(
                  onPressed: () {
                    final name = nameController.text.trim();
                    if (name.isNotEmpty) {
                      final newEmp = SessionManager().registerNewEmployee(name: name);
                      AuditService().logAction(
                        department: 'staff',
                        action: 'Yangi xodim qo`shildi',
                        details: '${newEmp.name} (${newEmp.id}) ro`yxatdan o`tkazildi.',
                      );
                    }
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                  },
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: Colors.blueAccent,
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  ),
                  child: Text('common.save'.tr(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16)),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

// FILE: D:\Desktop\bot\mobile\lib\features\staff\presentation\providers\staff_provider.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../data/models/staff_model.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/staff_repository.dart';

class StaffProvider extends ChangeNotifier {
  final StaffRepository _repository = StaffRepository();
  
  List<ShiftModel> _todayShifts = [];
  List<ShiftModel> get todayShifts => _todayShifts;
  
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;
  
  String? _error;
  String? get error => _error;

  final bool _useMockData = true;

  Future<void> fetchStaffData({bool background = false}) async {
    if (!background && _todayShifts.isEmpty) {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
      notifyListeners();
    }
    
    try {
      final shifts = await _repository.getTodayShifts();
      if (shifts.isEmpty && _useMockData) {
        _todayShifts = _getMockShifts();
      } else {
        _todayShifts = shifts;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _error = e.toString();
      if (_useMockData) {
         _todayShifts = _getMockShifts();
      }
    }
    if (_isLoading) {
      _isLoading = false;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  List<ShiftModel> _getMockShifts() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    return [
      ShiftModel(
        id: '1',
        userId: 'u1',
        startTime: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 10, 0),
        status: 'active',
        staff: StaffModel(id: 'u1', name: 'Ivanov A.', role: 'chef', isActive: true),
      ),
      ShiftModel(
        id: '2',
        userId: 'u2',
        startTime: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 10, 0),
        status: 'active',
        staff: StaffModel(id: 'u2', name: 'Smirnov V.', role: 'waiter', isActive: true),
      ),
      ShiftModel(
        id: '3',
        userId: 'u3',
        startTime: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 10, 0),
        status: 'completed',
        endTime: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 16, 0),
        staff: StaffModel(id: 'u3', name: 'Petrov D.', role: 'waiter', isActive: true),
      ),
      ShiftModel(
        id: '4',
        userId: 'u4',
        startTime: DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day, 9, 0),
        status: 'active',
        staff: StaffModel(id: 'u4', name: 'Kim A.', role: 'cleaner', isActive: true),
      ),
    ];
  }
}
